# The Japan Year Book 1928 — Who's Who & Directories

1,977 entries, LLM-transcribed. These entries were transcribed by the vision model of Opus 5.5. Before using any of these details, you must verify specific details with the original source which is linked to whenever possible.

### News Agencies

**Nippon Shimbun Rengo** (est. '26) Former Kokusai Tsushin (originally established in '14), was incorporated with the Toho Tsushin (est. '22) and assumed the present name in '26 with the backing of all leading newspapers in Tokyo, its organisation being modelled on the Associated Press of America. Office, Kojimachi-ku Uchisaiwaicho, Tokyo. — p. 296 ([scan leaf 341](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n341/mode/1up))

**Jiyu Tsushin** (est. '99) Office, Kyobashi Shin-Sakanacho, Tokyo. — p. 296 ([scan leaf 341](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n341/mode/1up))

**Nihon Dempo Tsushin** (est. '01) Office, Marunouchi Nakadori, Kojimachi-ku, Tokyo. — p. 296 ([scan leaf 341](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n341/mode/1up))

**Teikoku Tsushin** (est. '88) Office, Kyobashi Yamashitacho, Tokyo. — p. 296 ([scan leaf 341](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n341/mode/1up))

**Tokyo Tsushin** Office, Kaijo Building, Kojimachi-ku Eirakucho, Tokyo. — p. 296 ([scan leaf 341](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n341/mode/1up))


## Who's Who in Japan

**Abe, Fusajiro** Vice-Pres. of the Oriental Spinning Co., Ltd.; Dir. of the Omi Bank, Ltd., etc.; b. 1868 in Shiga-ken; studied at the Keiogijuku and assisted in opening the Omi Bank on graduation. Removed to Osaka in '95 and started flax and umbrella business to rise to be a prominent businessman there. Add. Sumiyoshi, Hyogo-ken. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Goichi** Chairman of the Board of Dir. of the Ibaraki Smokeless Coal Co., Ltd., Karatsu Mining Co., Ltd., etc.; b. 1873 in Gifu-ken. Has risen to the present position through connection with Mr. S. Asano's business. Add. Kyobashi, Tokyo. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Hidetaro** Auditor of Russo-Japanese Fishery Co., Ex-Sec. of the Bank of Chosen; b. 1878 in Ehime-ken; grad. from the Law Dep't., Tokyo Imp. Univ., and on graduation entered the service of the Chosen Bank, in which he had charge of a Branch at Fusan and other places; later Sectional Chief of the head office; removed to the present post. Add. Hisakata-machi, Koishikawa-ku, Tokyo. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Iohitaro** Pres. of the Gosho Co., Cotton-Yarn and Cotton-Stuff Exporter; b. in 1882 in Shiga-ken; grad. from Keio Univ. and went to U.S.A. where he studied at Columbia Univ., New York. Add. Minami-kyutaro-machi, Higashi-ku, Osaka. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Isoh** Lecturer at Waseda Univ.; b. 1865 in Fukuoka-ken; studied in the Doshisha, Kyoto, class '84, then a pastor for some years; studied at Theological Seminary at Hartford, 91-94 and at Glasgow; Principal of the Doshisha Middle School for some years; Prof. at Waseda Univ., '04-27, and then lecturer; founded the Japan Febian Society in '24; organized in '26 with others the Social Democratic Party of which he is leader. Works; Interpretation of Social Questions, Municipal Monopolies, etc. An authority on social politics and local administration. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Jiro** Dr. Litt., Prof. at Tohoku (Sendai) Univ.; b. in 1883 in Yamagata-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Philosophy class '07 and further at the post-graduate course and in Europe (about two years). Works; Essentials of Ethics, Fine Arts, Criticism on some of Nietzsche's Works, etc. Add. Sendai. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Jujun** Permanent Vice-Minister of Agriculture and Forestry Dep't since April '25; b. in 1879 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '05. After passing higher civil service examination, he served at both provincial and Central Govts. as Secretary; Sec. and Counsellor to the Home Office and the Legislative Bureau; Gov. of Tottori; then Dep't Chief of the former Census Board; Dir. of the Statistics Bureau; Dep't Chief of the Patent Bureau. Add. Ikebukuro, near Tokyo. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Masaya** Dir. of Kokusai Steamship Co., Ltd., and Kawa- [continues on next page] saki Dockyard Co., Ltd.; b. 1867 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. from the ship-building course, Eng. Dep't., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93, and entered the service of Kawasaki Dockyard Co., then transferred to the present post in '16. Add. Suma, Kobe. — p.  ([scan leaf 798](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n798/mode/1up))

**Abe, Yoshinari** Prof. of Seoul Univ.; b. 1883 at Matsuyama; edu. at the First High School and Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Occidental Philosophy, class '09. Works; Ancient and Medieval Philosophy in Europe, Modern European Philosophy, Kant's Ethical Philosophy, etc. Add. Seoul. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Abiko, Kyutaro** a prominent Japanese journalist in San Francisco where he runs the Nichi-bei (Japan-America) News, besides acting as Dir. of the Nichi-bei Bank, etc., and has also founded the Yamato Colony. He was b. in 1864 in Niigata-ken and m. sister of Miss Tsuda in 1908. Educated at California University. Add. Nichi-bei Office, 'Frisco. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Abo, Kiyokazu** Baron (suc. 1909), Admiral, Commander-in-Chief of Yokosuka Naval Station since Apr. '27; b. 1879 in Miye-ken; grad. from the Naval Academy in '97; Lieut.-Comdr., '00; Comdr., '06; Naval Attache to the Japanese Embassy in London and Superintendent of Naval Construction, March '13; attached to the Naval Board of Command Aug., '15; Comdr. of the warship Aki, Dec. '15; Vice-Chief of the naval Board of Command in '16; Vice-Minister of Navy in '23; Commander-in-Chief of Kure Naval Station in '25; Supreme War Councillor '26. Add. Ichigaya Tomihisa-cho, Ushigome-ku, Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Adachi, Buntaro** Dr. Medicine, Prof. Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. 1865 in Shizuoka-ken; grad. from the Medical Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ., '94; Assist. Prof. at his alma-mater and Prof. 3rd High School till '98; studied anatomy in Germany, '99-04. Add. Kamikyo Yoshida-machi, Kyoto. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Adachi, Kenzo** M.P., ex-Min. of Communications ('25-27), a leader of the Minisei Party; b. 1864 in Kumamoto; went to Korea and established several newspapers to promote the Japanese interest in the peninsula and was involved in the Korean Queen assassination affair but acquitted on trial; Par. Senior under-Sec. of Foreign Department in the Okuma Cabinet. Made a tour through Europe and America, 19[?]. Add. Ushigome Tsukudo-Hachiman, Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Adachi, Mine-ichiro** D.L. (Japan), Amb. to Paris; b. 1869 in Yamagata-ken; graduated from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '92; attache in Rome, '93; removed to Paris in '97; attached to the head office in '03; was on suite of Peace Envoy to Portsmouth in '05; Senior Counsellor at head office before transfer as Emb. Counsellor to Paris; Minister to Mexico '13-16; Ambassador to Belgium '17-27. Add. Jap. Emb., Paris. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Adachi, Shinzaburo** Lawyer in Osaka; b. 1874 in Okayama-ken; grad. from the Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02, and appointed a probationary judicial officer; left the service soon after and called to the Bar, practicing in Osaka. Add. (Office) 29, Masago-cho, Kita-ku, Osaka. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Adachi, So** Pres. of Japan Chemical Industry Co., Ltd.; b. 1870 in Tottori-ken; grad. from Keio Univ., '92; entered the Jiji Shimpo with which he was connected for about nineteen years, and then became a Dir. of the Japan Conscription Ins. Co., Ltd Add. Shirokane Sanko-cho, Shiba-ku, Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 799](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n799/mode/1up))

**Aiko, Kanesato** Standing Dir. of the Fifteenth Bank, Ltd.; Dir. of the Taisho Bank, Ltd., etc.; b. 1863 in Kagoshima city; stayed in China and Korea, and studied English and Chinese, etc., was a member of Kagoshima City Assembly, and also of Kagoshima Chamber of Commerce. Add. Tokyo Club and Tokyo Bankers Club. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Aioi, Yoshitaro** Mem. of Dairen City Assembly, Chairman of Dairen Chamber of Commerce, etc.; b. 1865 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial School '96, and entered the Mitsui Mining Co., and then the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; later transferred to South Manchuria Railway Co., which he left and started business in Dairen. Add. Kirishima-cho, Dairen City. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akabane, Katsumi** Dir. S.M.R. since '22; b. in 1869; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School in 1904 and took to business; Dept. Chief of the Mitsui Bussan till '21. Add. Dairen. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akaboshi, Tetsuma** ex-Pres. of Taisho Bank, Dir. Chiyoda Fire Ins. Co., and founder of the Keimei Kai devoted to aiding cultural researches; b. in 1882, eldest son of Yanosuke, a millionaire; edu. at Pennsylvania Univ., class '07. Add. Akasaka Dai-machi, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Aka-ike, Jo** Crown mem. House of Peers, Metropolitan Police Superintendent at the time of the Kiyoura Cabinet; b. 1878 in Nagano-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '07, and further studied at the Univ. Hall. Passing the civil service examination held the secretaryship at one province and another; Sec. of the Home Office; Gov. of Shizuoka; Bureau Dir. of Home Dept. in the Korean Govt-General '23-4. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akamatsu, Katsumaro** staff member of the Japan Federation of Labour; b. 1894 at Tokuyama; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Law, class '19. Was once writer of the Kaiho and the Oriental Economist (Tokyo). Works; Social Revolution, etc. Add. Riratsuka Koyama, near Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akashi, Yoichiro** ex-Vice-Min. of Education Dept. (22); b. in Tokyo 1876; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law '97; studied in France and Germany, '20-04; Dir. General Education Bureau '16-22; Pers. Sec. to the Minister in addition to Counsellorship '05. Add. Hongo Harukicho, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akatsuka, Shosuke** Minister to Austria '24-26; b. in Kagoshima 1872; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, '98; passed diplomatic service examination '98; Eleve-Consul at Fusan; Sec. at Berlin and Washington; Consul at Manila; Sec. Foreign Office '90; Consul-General at Mukden '12-22. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akimoto, Harutomo** Viscount (suc. '17), Mem. House of Peers; b. 1881 in Tokyo; adopted son of the late Akimoto Okitomo, formerly Daimyo; grad. from the Peers' School; further studied in America, Germany and England and was a Gov't. Commissioner at the Anglo-Japanese Exhibition, '10; Personal Secretary to the Minister of Railways '21. Add. Surugadai Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akita, Kiyoshi** M.P. barrister, Parl. Vice-Min. of Communications since Apr. '27; b. 1881 in Tokushima; studied law in a private Univ. and was appointed Judge; ex- Prop. of the Niroku (daily paper). Add. Hongo Fujimaye, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 800](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n800/mode/1up))

**Akita Ujaku** a dramatist and member of Japan Social Federation; b. 1883 at Kuroishi, Aomori-ken; edu; at Aomori 1st Middle Sch. and Waseda Univ. in English Literature, class '07. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Akiyama, Masanosuke** D.L., ex-Dir. of Civil Administration Bureau, Tsingtao, 1917-22; b. 1866 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '86, to be connected afterward with civil affairs section of War and Navy; Councillor of Korean Gov't-Gen. '13-17. Add. Ushigome Yakuoji, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Akiyama, Shohachi** Dir. of the Engineering Bureau, Dep't of Railways; b. 1877 in Hiroshima; edu. at the Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Mech. Eng. Entered the service of the State Railway as engineer and was despatched abroad for studying his speciality. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Akiyama, Yoshifuru** Gen. (ret. 1923), ex-Dir. Mil. Edu. Board '20-23; b. 1855 in Ehime-ken; Sub-Lieut., '79; Major-Gen., '99; Lieut.-Gen., '09; took part in Japan-China War, Boxers Trouble and Russo-Japanese War; Sup't. of Cavalry, Mil. Edu. Bd.; then Commander of 13th Div. '13; Com. of Guards Division '15; Com. of Chosen Garrison, '16; full Gen. in Nov. '16; Mem. War Council '17; director of the Hokuyo Middle School (private), Matsuyama, Ehime-ken, since 1925. Add. Matsuyama, Ehime-ken. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Akizuki, Satsuo** Dir. of a Japanese daily at Seoul; 1858 in Miyazaki-ken; edu. at former Law Coll., Justice Dep't in '84; further studied in Belgium and Germany from '85 to '91; Counsellor For. Office in '92; Consul at Fusan in '96; Legation Secretary at Paris in '99; to Petrograd in '02; Minister Resident to Sweden-Norway in '04; Minister at Brussels in May '07; Amb. at Vienna '09-14; Dir. of the Yomiuri for a while. Placed on the Peace Envoy staff in 1919. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Akutsu, Saburo** D. Med., prop. of Akutsu Surgical Hospital; b. 1873; grad. at Medical Coll. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., in '98 and further studied in Germany; became chief of Surgical Dept. of the Juntendo Hospital. Add. Kanda Awaji-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Amano, Hichisaburo** prop. of Amano Railway Car Works in Tokyo; b. in 1874 in Kyoto; edu. at Doshisha and the Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Mechanical Engineering; is Mng.-Dir. of the Japan Car Works, Ltd., in which the interest of the Amano family is predominant. Add. Honjo Aioi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Amano, Tameyuki** D.L., economist; was on Board of Dir. of Waseda University till 1917; Dir. Waseda Business School; b. in Saga-ken, 1859 and grad. Imp. Tokyo Univ. '82; M.P. at 1st and 2nd sessions. Wrote "Principles of Economics" and others; once editor and proprietor of the "Oriental Economist," a thrice-a-month journal. Add. Koj. Iidamachi, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Amaoka, Naoyoshi** ex-Dir. Postal Savings Bureau, Communication Dept.; b. 1880; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and passed Higher Civil Service Exam. in '07. Add. Azabu Hommura-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Anamizu, Yoshichi** Mng.-Dir. of the Fuji Paper Mill Co., Ltd., Pres. of Hokkaido Electric Light Co., etc.; b. 1875 in Yamanashi-ken; was twice elected M.P. from Yamanashi. Add. Azabu Nagasaka-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 801](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n801/mode/1up))

**Ando, Koko** a famous lady-violinist, sister of Rohan Koda, wellknown novelist; grad. from the Tokyo Academy of Music; further studied at the Government Musical School in Berlin, Germany; is Instructor of the Tokyo Academy of Music since 1903. Add. Koishikawa Ohote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Ando, Masazumi** M.P., Parl. Counselor of Education Dept., journalist; b. in 1876 in Tokyo; edu. at Toyo and Waseda Univs. and Tokyo Foreign Language Sch. was on editorial staff of the Tokyo Asahi as its managing editor, also connected with the Osaka Asahi and other journals in Tokyo and Osaka; was a special correspondent of the two Asahis in the Washington conference. Add. Asakusa Matsuba-cho, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Anesaki, Masaharu** Litt. D., Prof., Coll. of Literature, Imp. Tokyo Univ. since '04; Chief Librarian of the Imperial Tokyo Univ.; Manager of Imperial Academy; Mem. House of Peers (elected 1926); b. 1873 in Kyoto; edu. at the Univ. in Philosophy class '96; further studied at Kiel, British Museum and Benares, '00-03; Exchange Prof. at Harvard '12-15. Add. Koishikawa Hakusan Gotenmachi, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Anraku, Kanemichi** ex-Sup't of the Metropolitan Police, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1850 in Kagoshima; was connected with the Police service from about 1875; next a local Governor. He resigned the post in '08 and entered commercial circles. Pres. of Chiyoda Gas Co. till '11, and reappointed Met. Police Sup't '11-13; and for 3rd time in '13-14. Add. Nishiokubo, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Arata** Minister to Mexico since '26; b. 1881 in Kumamoto; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Politics and passed the Dip. and Con. Serv. Examin. the following year; Eleve-Consul at Chicago '09 and then New York; at London, 11; Consul at Suchow, 13; Sec. at Head Office, '16; 3rd Emb. Sec. in Russia, '16; 2nd Sec. '17; Sec. at Head Office in '18 with additional duty as Per. Sec. to the Minister; Sectional Chief, '19-22; 1st Emb. Sec. at Washington '23; Emb. Coun. in Brazil the same year; Consul-Gen. at Honolulu '24-25. Add. Japanese Legation, Mexico City. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Gohei** Mng.-Dir. of the Tokyo Musline Mfg. Co.; b. 1871, Tokyo; first son of Aoki Gohei, famous draper; edu. at the Tokyo Higher Com. School, class '90; was for a time Mng.-Dir. of the Tokyo Calico Mfg. Co. '06. Add. Kojimachi Kami-rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Kikuo** Mng.-Dir. of the General Dept. of the Mitsubishi Firm since '15; b. in 1867 in Nara-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law class '93 and has since been connected with Mitsubishi. Add. Mitsubishi Firm, Koj., Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Nobumitsu** Visc., Mem. of House of Peers since 1897; b. in '67, 2nd son of late I. Nakayama, of Mito clan and adopted by the Aoki family which was the petty daimiate at Asada, Settsu; was auditor of the Bank of Japan till '25. Took active part in the formation of Kiyoura Cabinet. Add. Ushigome Ichigaya, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Shuzo** Crown Mem. House of Peers since 1926; b. 1875 in Yamaguchi; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '02 and passed the higher civil serv. examination two years after; was long in the service of the State Railways in which he ultimately rose to be Director of the Finance Bureau; resigned and became Managing-Director of the Chikuho Electric Railway Co. in '20; Deputy-Mayor of Yokohama in '22 and in the terrible disaster there in '23 he lost two children; was Permanent Vice-Minister of Railway Dep't., '24-26. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 802](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n802/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Tetsuji** D.L., lawyer and patent attorney: b. in 1876 in Gifu-ken; grad. from Keio 1897, passed the examination for the Bench in '98; a Probationary Judge at Kobe for a few months. Dispatched by the alma mater to study Commercial Law at Berlin and Gottingen '99-02; Prof. at Keio, but left it in '08 to open law office. Add. Shiba Shirokane-Imazato, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Tetsutaro** Pres. of the Takasago Com. and Ind. Bank, Ltd.; b. 1867 in Okayama city; grad. from the political course, Law Dept., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89, and on graduation entered the service of the Finance Dep't.; entered the Bank of Japan, '90; transferred to the Yokohama Specie Bank, '91; stayed about ten years in Europe and America in charge of the Branches in New York, San Francisco and London, returning home in '05; left the Specie Bank in '09; was the Government Commercial Agent at Hongkong, '10-13; then Dir. of the Japan Industrial Bank, Ltd., which he resigned in '18. Add. Kami-Osaki, Ebara-gun, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Aoyama, Rokuro** Dir. of the Teikoku Electric Light Co., Manager of Tokyo Branch of L.J. Healing & Co. Ltd. (London); b. 1875 in Mie-ken; graduated from Post and Telegraph School (Dep't. Communications) '02 and entered Gov't. service; then resigning the post entered Messrs. Healing as Mng. Add. Shiba Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Aoyama, Yukinobu** Visc., Mem. House of Peers where he is a leader of the Kenkyukai, the most powerful group; is also on the Board of Directors of the Fifteenth Bank and other concerns; b. 1861, a ninth descendant of a small daimiate in Mino; has a seat in the Upper House since 1890; is Manager of the Tokyo Rifle Society. Add. Koj. Fujimi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Arahata, Katsuzo** a socialist and labor leader; b. at Yokohama in 1887. After finishing common school he earned his bread as messenger boy, apprentice, pedlar of Socialist literature. Later he undertook with others the publication of labor and socialist organs and has been imprisoned three times on charge of disturbing public order. Has translated works of Sidney Webb and Bernard Shaw, published "A Socialist Dictionary," etc., besides issuing some original works. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Arai, Kentaro** Privy Councillor since 1926; Min. Agriculture and Commerce in Adm. Kato Cabinet formed in [?], and Crown-Mem. of House of Peers; b. 1863 in Niigata; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in law, '90; entered the Treasury in which he rose to the position of Vice-Minister in '06; Dir. of Finance Dep't. of Korean Gov't. Gen. Add. Koishikawa Kanatomi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Arakawa, Bunroku** Dr. Eng., M.A. (Cornell), Prof. of the Engineering Dep't., Kyushu Imp. Univ.; b. 1878 in Yokohama; grad. from the Eng. Dep't., Tokyo Imp. Univ. 1900; lecturer at his alma mater, 1900; sent to Germany, England and U.S.A. for study, '08-10; present post since 1911. Works: Science of Electrical Engineering. Add. Jigyo-nishi-machi, Fukuoka city. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 803](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n803/mode/1up))

**Arakawa, Goro** Chief Edit. of Hiroshima Chugoku, M.P. and ex-Par. Jr. Secy. of Communication Dep't; b. 1865 in Hiroshima; grad. Kokugakuin and Nippon Univ.; became Edit. of present daily; established a girls' school in Hiroshima. Add. "Chugoku Shimbun," Hiroshima. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Araki, Torasaburo** M.D. (Strasburg), Pres. Imp. Kyoto Univ. since 1915; b. 1866 in Gumma-ken; edu. at Special Course, Med. Coll. Imp. Tokyo Univ. '89; studied physiology at the Strasburg Univ., Germany, '89-91; Prof. the 3rd High School '95; transferred to Imp. Kyoto Univ; Dean of its Med. Coll. Add. Kyoto. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Ariga, Mitsutoyo** Pres. of the Chosen Development Bank, Ltd.; b. 1873 in Nagano-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Hogakuin (now Chuo Univ.); was in the service of the Chosen Government-General. Add. Seishomonnai, Seoul. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Ariga, Nagabumi** Manager of the Mitsui Firm; b. 1865 in Osaka-fu and younger brother to the late Dr. Nagao Ariga; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Law, '86; was in Government service for a long while ultimately rising to be a Bureau Director in Dep't of Agriculture and Commerce about '02. Add. Azabu Iigura, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Arima, Ryokitsu** Adm. (ret. '22) hero of the Port Arthur blockading operation in Russo-Japanese war; b. at Wakayama in 1862; midshipman '88; Rear-Adm. '09; Com. of 1st Squadron '12; Vice-Admiral '13; President of the Naval Academy '14-16; Chief of the Naval Education Board, '16-18. Add. Nakashibuya Oyama, Tokyo-fu. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Arima, Sumibumi** Visc. Sub.-Lieut. of Cavalry (ret.), Lord Steward to Prince Higashi-Fushimi; b. 1868 of a former daimyo's family at Maruoka, Echizen; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Agriculture '97; Chamberlain to the Crown Prince '00-10. Add. Yotsuya Ukyo-machi, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Arisaka, Shozo** D. Eng., and Director of Naval Arsenal, Tokyo, Prof. at the Imperial University of Tokyo; b. in 1867 in Tokyo; edu. at the University, class '90; sent by the Navy to France where he studied at Hotchkiss; devised a new plug for quickfirers; has been sent twice to England on official business. Add. Tamagawa, Ebaragun, Tokyo-fu. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Arishima, Ikuma** foreign style painter and novelist, bro. of Takeo Arishima whose suicide created a sensation; b. 1882 at Yokohama; edu. at Tokyo Foreign Language School and next learned oil painting for several years in Paris and Rome. Works; a number of novels, Reminiscence of Cezanne, etc. Add. Koj. Shimoniban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Ariyoshi, Akira** Ambassador in Brazil since '27; b. 1876 in Kyoto; edu. at the Tokyo Higher Commercial School, class '98; passed the Diplomatic service examination '98; Consul at Fusan, and then Newchuang; later Secretary to Japanese Embassy at Paris; Con.-Gen. at Shanghai, '09-19; Min. in Berne, '20-26. Add. Rio de Janeiro. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Ariyoshi, Chuichi** Mayor of Yokohama since '25; b. 1870 in Tango; edu. in Law at Imp. Tokyo Univ. class '96; Sec. Home Office till '08; Governor of Chiba-ken '08-12; Dir. of Gen. Affairs, Korean Res.-Gen. in '10; Gov. of Miyazaki '11-15; Kanagawa '15-18, Hyogo-ken '18-22; Dir.-Gen. of Pol. Affairs, Korean Gov't-Gen., '22-24. Add. Yokohama City Office. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 804](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n804/mode/1up))

**Asabuki, Tsunekichi** Mng-Dir. Mitsubishi Dry Goods Store; b. 1877 in Tokyo, son of the late Eiji Asabuki; studied at Keio and then in England; entered Bank of Japan; then Mitsui Busan and transferred to New York Branch; later established the Chiyoda-gumi. Add. Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asada, Tokunori** Mem. House of Peers: b. 1848 in Kyoto; 1st Class Counsellor to Japanese Legation, Washington, '74; Counsellor of the Foreign Office '79; Director of Commercial Bureau '86; Governor of Nagano-ken and next of Kanagawa-ken, '99; Vice-Minister for Foreign Affairs, '00; that of Communications, which he resigned '02. Add. Koj. Shimo Rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asahi, Kenkichi** D.M., Prof. of Med. Dep't., Kyushu Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Kyoto-fu; grad. from Med. Dep't, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; then studied in Germany and on his return home was appointed Assist.-Prof. at the Kyushu Imp. Univ.; proprietor of an Ear, Nose & Throat Hospital. Add. Fukuoka city. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asakawa, Kan-ichi** Prof. Yale University; b. about 1870; grad. from Waseda and early went over to America for study; grad. from Yale and has since been lecturer and then Prof. of Japanese History at Yale. Add. Yale. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asakura, Denjiro** Mng.-Dir. of Japan-America Trust Co., Ltd.; b. 1872 in Ishikawa-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99; Chief of the Mining Section of the South Manchuria Railway Co.; then present post. Add. Japan-America Trust Co., Kitamaki-cho, Nihombashi-ku, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asano, Osuke** Dr. Eng., Emeritus Prof. of Waseda; b. in '59 at Okayama; edu. in electricity at the Gov. Eng. College, class 1881; electrician to Department of Communications till '16 and was despatched to Europe and America two or three times. Add. Azabu Kogai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asano, Ryozo** Dir. of the Asano Cement Co. and several other undertakings started by his father Soichiro Asano; b. in 1889, second son; edu. at Harvard in Economy, class '12. Add. Mita Tsunamachi, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asano, Soichiro** Pres. Toyo S.S. Co., Asano Dockyards and Dir. of many other concerns; b. 1848 in Toyama-ken. He arrived in Tokyo '71 with empty purse; purchased from the Yokohama Gas Works coal-tar and other refuses then left unutilized as first step to his success. In '75 he bought the Gov. Cement Works at Fukagawa and organized Asano Cement Co.; also started tank oil business, '93 and then promoted with others the Toyo S.S. Co., '95. Add. Shiba Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Asano, Taijiro** Dir. of a number of the Asano undertakings; b. '84, eldest son of Soichiro Asano; edu. at Waseda Univ. Add. Shiba Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Ashida, Hitoshi** First Secretary of the Embassy in Constantinople since '26; b. 1887 in Kyoto-fu; passed Dip. and Con. Service Exam. in '11, and grad. in the following year from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Attache in '12 and next 3rd Emb. Sec., '17, in Russia; in France, '18-20; 2nd Emb. Sec. '20; Junior commissioner at the various international conferences held at Paris and Geneva; Sec. at the Head Office and Sectional Chief at the Intelligence Dep't '23-25. Publications; European Diplomacy after Paris Conference, 1923; My Impressions in Russia, 1924; The Powers and Situation of their Home Politics, 1924. Add. Jap. Embassy, Constantinople. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 805](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n805/mode/1up))

**Aso, Hisashi** a labor leader and Executive of the Japan Labor-Farm Party; b. 1891 in Oita-ken; edu. at Third High Sch. and Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law; on the staff of the Tokyo Nichi Nichi 1917-'19; joined the Yu-ai-kai Labor Ass'n. and has since taken prominent part in upholding the cause of labor. Add. Komagome Kichijoji, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Aso, Jiro** Manager of the Bank of Japan; b. 1871 in Osaka; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Law, class '94; has since been in the service of the Bank, having been Dir. of its Branches at Nagoya, Osaka, etc. and also Dir. of the Loan and the Banking Dept. at the head office. Add. Azabu Torilzaka, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Aso, Keijiro** D.Agr., Prof. of Agri. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1875 in Tokyo; grad. from Agri. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., with honours in '99; then studied at the University Hall; Assist.-Prof. at his alma mater, '01; sent to Germany, etc. for study of agricultural chemistry, '08-'12. Add. Minami-Tera-machi, Yotsuya, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Atobe, Sadajiro** D.C.L., Prof. at Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1872 in Fukui-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., 1899; further studied in Germany; Assist.-Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '05; full Prof. in '06. Add. Shimokamo, Kamikyo-ku, Kyoto. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Awaya, Ken** Perm. Vice-Min. of Education; b. in 1883 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '07. After passing the higher civil service exam. he was for a time secretary of the Ibaraki Prefectural Office; transferred to the Education Dept. where he was Dir. of Special Education Bureau till Apr. '27. Add. Akasaka Tango-cho, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Awazu, Seiryo** D.L., Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., Waseda Univ., etc., insurance expert; b. Kyoto in 1871; edu. in Law at Imp. Tokyo Univ. '94; has since been engaged in insurance business at the Department of Agriculture and Commerce, as organizer of a number of life insurance cos, besides undertaking teaching. Attended the World's Insurance Congress held at New York '03. Add. Nakano Uyeno-hara, Tokyo-fu. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Ayai, Tadahiko** Aud. of Mural Sav. Bank, Shibaura Iron Works, and Chief of Acct. Sec. of Tokyo Electric Co.; b. 1871 in Oita-ken; edu. at Post and Telegraph School in Tokyo; was in service of Communications Dept. for years; went to U.S.A. to accomplish his study in '90; after returning home he joined the Murai Co. Add. Takada, near Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Ayukawa, Gisuke** mechanical engineer and partner of the Kuhara and the Kaijima interests, being connected with both families by marriage; b. 1880 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. at Coll. of Eng., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '03; served for some years at the Shibaura Eng. Works and then went abroad for study and inspection. Add. Ushigome Sanaicho, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 806](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n806/mode/1up))

**Baba, Ei-ichi** D.L., Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank since '27; Crown Mem. House of Peers, b. in 1879; edu. at Law in Imp. Tokyo Univ. '03; passed Higher Civil Service Exam.; Taxation Superintendent and then Sec. to Korean Govt-Gen; Pres. of Legislation Bureau in the Kato (Adm.) Cabinet, '22-24. Add. Hongo Maruyama-Fukuyama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Baba, Genji** ex-Judge of Court of Cassation; b. 1860; grad. Law, Imp. Univ. of Tokyo, class '85; was appointed first a junior judge in Tokyo Court and Judge of Supreme Court; resigned and began practice '24. Add. Mita Tsunamachi, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Banzai, Rihachiro** Lt.-Gen. (ret.), ex-Mil. Adviser to the Chinese Govt.; b. 1873 in Tokyo; Sub.-Lieut. '92; Major, '03; Colonel, '12; Maj.-Gen. '18; had more than ten years' connection with the Chinese Government and is now one of the authorities of things Chinese. Add. Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Chiba, Shin-ichiro** M.D., Pres. of Chiba Hospital (private) in Tokyo for diseases of Ear, Nose and Throat; b. 1880 in Tokyo. Edu. at Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '05 and further studied his speciality at the post-grad. course and next at Strasburg Univ., and Frankfort Hospital, '08-11; served at the Junten-do Hospital '08-20 and then established the present Hospital. Add. Nihombashi Hama-cho Nichome, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Chinda, Sutemi** Count (Bar. '70, Visc. '11), Privy Councillor, Lord Chamberlain to the Emperor; b. at Hirosaki in 1856; grad. from an American University '81; appointed a clerk of Foreign Office, '85 and had charge of the Telegraph Section in the same office; Consul at San Francisco and Chemulpo; Consul-General at Shanghai, '95; Minister Resident at Brazil and next at the Hague '99-00; Minister Ple. Envoy Ext. at Petrograd, 1900; took charge in '01 of the For. Office while the Minister Baron Komura was away to Portsmouth and Peking; Amb. to Berlin '08; Washington '11; London '20; Peace Envoy Dec. '18; Count in '20. Was on the suite of the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '21; Grand Steward to the Prince Regent '21-27. Add. Koj. Kami-Nibancho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Chiwaki, Morinosuke** L.L.D. (Loyola Univ., Chicago), Pres. the Tokyo Dental Coll.; b. 1870 at Abiko, Hitachi; grad. from the Keio Univ., was for some years on the staff of the Tokyo Shimpo; grad. from Dr. Takayama's Dental School, and after practice for some years established the present school. Add. Tokyo Dental Coll. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Chujo, Sei-ichiro** Architect, Hon. Mem. of Society of Architects (London), and of Royal Society of Arts (London); b. 1868 at Yonezawa; grad. Eng. Coll. (Architecture) Imp. Tokyo Univ.; further studied at Cambridge (Eng.); on returning home entered Edu. Dept; again studied in England '03-07; opened Sone-Chujo Office '08, besides his own. Add. Komagome Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Daido, Ryota** Dir. of Tokyo Electric Light Co., ex-Dir. of Tokyo Municipal Electric Office of Tokyo, Railway Bureau; b. 1879 in Shiga-ken; grad. from Law, Kyoto Imp. Univ. in '03; Private Secretary to Home Minister, '04; Councillor, and then Secretary of the Dep't of Communications; Chief of the General Affairs Section of the Kobe Railway Control Bureau, Councillor and then Director of the Imp. Railway Board, 1915-16; present post in '26. Add. Ushigome Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 807](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n807/mode/1up))

**Daikuhara, Gintaro** D. Agr., President of Kyushu Imperial University since 1926; b. 1868 in Nagano-ken; graduated from Tokyo Imp. University in 1894 in Agricultural Chemistry; served the Department of Agriculture and Commerce as technical official either at head office or at the Nishigahara Experimental Institute; held similar post at the Korean Gov.-General before transfer to the present post. Add. Fukuoka. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**Danno, Reisuke** Man.-Dir. of Russo-Japanese Fishery Co., Ltd.; b. 1875 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. 1899; was Mng. of Wakamatsu Branch of the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; then Mng. Sapporo Branch, Hokkaido Colliery Steamship Co., Ltd.; present post in 1923. Add. Myogadani, Koishikawa, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**Date, Gen-ichiro** Gen.-Man. of the Eastern News Agency; b. 1878 in Shimane-ken; grad. from the Doshisha Univ., Kyoto; entered the Kokumin Shimbun, '00; Kokusai News Agency, '15; Chief editor of the Yomiuri, '19; established the Eastern News Agency in '22; represented Japan at the international journalistic conference at Washington '27; made tour several times in China and Europe and America and served in the Daily Telegraph London, for several months (1910). Add. Nakashibuya, near Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**Dan, Takuma** D. Eng., Gen. Director of Mitsui Firm; b. 1858 at Fukuoka; was sent for study to America early in Meiji by the Lord of Fukuoka and completed the course of mining at the Boston Tech. Institute, U.S.A., and was for some years teacher of English at Gov. schools; technical official at the Gov. Meteorological Observatory and next at the Miike Coal mine, with the purchase of which by the Mitsui he entered the service of the Firm. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**de Becker, J. E** L.L.B.; D.C.L., international Lawyer; b. in London, England 1863, came to Japan in '87; is the Legal Adviser to the Kobe Foreign Board of Trade and standing Counsel of the leading foreign banks in Japan. Local correspondent of the Society of Comparative Legislation, London. Has been practising law since '94, and contributed several works to Japanese legal literature. Works: "Annotated Civil Code of Japan," "Commentary on the Commercial Code of Japan," "Elements of Japanese Jurisprudence, etc. Add. Kobe. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**Debuchi, Katsuji** Vice-Minister of Foreign Affairs since '24; b. 1878 in Iwate-ken; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and Post-Graduate course, class, '02, and passed the Dip. and Con. Service exam. the same year; 3rd Emb. Sec. at Berlin, '07-10; 2nd Emb. Sec. '10; Sec. Chief at the head office, '11; 1st Sec. at Peking, '14; at Washington, '18; Counsellor at Berlin, '20; attended as assist. the 2nd League of Nations congress and the Washington Conference; on the Shantung Settlement Com., '22; Emb. Counsellor. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**Den, Kenjiro** Baron (cr. '07). Mem. House of Peers, Chairman of Japan Electric Society etc.; b. 1855 in Hyogo-ken; held at several places post of local chief Police Inspector; Vice-Min. of Communications, Jan.-June '98; President of the Kansai R'ly Co., at the same time M.P. for Hyogo-ken; Vice-Min. of Communications '00-06; Min. of the Dept. '16-17; Gov.-Gen. of Formosa '18-20; Min. of Agri. and Com. '22-23. Add. Tamagawa Kaminoge, near Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 808](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n808/mode/1up))

**Den, Sho** ex-Perm. Vice-Min. Finance Dept. ('24-27); b. 1878; grad. at Politics, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '04; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Sec. of Tobacco Monopoly Bureau, and of the Dept; stayed in London as Finance Commissioner; Dir. of Account Bureau before promotion. Add. Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Dewa, Shigeto** Adm. (ret.) Baron ('07); b. 1855 at Aizu; grad. from Naval Academy in '82; was Capt. of cruiser Takachiho in the Japan-China war of 1894-95; chief of committee to bring home Cruiser Tokiwa from England '99; Rear-Adm. 1900, and Com. of the Standing Squadron; Chief Construction Board at Yokosuka; Dir. of Naval Affairs Bureau, etc.; represented Japan at Panama Exposition '15. Add. Kami-Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Dohi, Keizo** M.D., dermatologist, Prof. Med. Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ. and Chairman of the Japanese Derm. Society; grad. at Med. Coll. 1891 and Univ. Hall; studied his speciality in Austria, Germany, and France, 1894-98. Corresponding member of the Vienna ('02), Berlin ('03), and Paris Derm. Society ('11); recommended to be honorable member of the Italian Derm. Society '06; Editor of the Japanese Journal of Derm. and Urinology. Add. Koj. Shimo-Nibancho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Doki, Kahei** ex-Gov. of Hokkaido ('24-25); Dir. of Civil Affairs, Kwantung Leased Territory '21-23; b. in Wakayama-ken in 1875; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '01; passed the Exam. for the Bar while at school; passed Higher Civil Service Exam.; entered Home Office in which he served in various localities; Gov. of Ishikawa-ken '21-24. Add. Sapporo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Ebina, Danjo** one of the veteran pastors, and Pres. of the Doshisha, Kyoto, since '20; b. 1856 in Yanagawa; studied at the Foreign Language School in Kumamoto; then theology at the Doshisha; has subsequently led an active life in evangelistic and educational work; pastor of the Hongo Church, Tokyo, for a long time. Add. Doshisha, Kyoto. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Egi, Senshi** Privy Councillor since '24 and Lord-in-Waiting of the Kinkei Hall; b. 1853, studied Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and was in service of Education Dept. '72-77; transferred to Home Office as Dir. of Local Adm. Bureau '90-97; Governor of Ibaraki, Aichi, Hiroshima and Kumamoto till '06 when he resigned. Add. Kanda Awaji-cho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Egi, Yoku** Adviser to the Minsei-to Party, Mem. of House of Peers (nom. '16); b. 1873 in Yamaguchi-ken, adopted son and heir of the above; grad. Law Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ. '97, and studied at Univ. Hall; Sec. Chief of Colonial Bureau (abolished later) '10-13; Chief Secy. of 2nd Okuma Cabinet, '14-16; same post in the Kato (Visc.) Cabinet '24-25; Justice Minister '25-27. Add. Mita Shikokumachi, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Eguchi, Teijo** Dir. Mitsubishi Firm; b. 1865 in Kochi; grad. from Tokyo Higher Com. School '87 and after having been a school teacher for some years, entered the Firm '91 as member on the staff of Banking Department; sub-Mng. of Mining Dep't at Head Office in '97; Dir. of the Land Dep't '00-22. Add. Ushigome Akagishita, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Eitaki, Hisakichi** Mng. Dir. of Tokyo Building Co., ex-Con.-Gen. at Honolulu '12-14; b. 1866 in Echigo; studied law and became Judge in '93 and transferred to the Foreign Office same year, attached to Consulate at Fusan in '93; Consul at Jinsen, Shanghai, Shashi, Sydney and Hankow between '94 and '06; Consul-General at Shanghai '05 to '09, then at Chientao till '12. Add. Tozuka, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 809](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n809/mode/1up))

**Ema, Shun-ichi** Barrister, ex-M.P.; mesmerist; b. in 1861 and studied politics at a private Univ., Tokyo; admitted to the Bar in '93 and afterwards elected Pres. of the Tokyo Barrister's Association; Vice-Pres. of Tokyo Prefectural Assembly '89. Add. Koishikawa Hakusan-maye, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Endo, Ryukichi** D. Litt., Founder and Pres. of the Sugamo Middle School, Pres. of Philosophical and Sociological Ass'ns.; b. 1867 in Mayebashi; grad. from the Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. Works: Chinese Philosophy, Loatze Philosophy, etc. Add. Sugamo, near Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujii, Kenjiro** D. Sc., Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1866 in Ishikawa-ken; grad. from Science, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '92; went to Germany for study, '01; appointed Prof. at his alma mater, May '12. Add. Hongo-ku, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujii, Kenjiro** Litt. Dr. and Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ. since '20 in Ethics; b. 1872 in Yamagata-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '98 and Munchen and Berlin '05-07. Works; Principles of Subjective Ethics, National Ethics; was for some years connected with Waseda Univ. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujii, Otoo** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1867 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; was Prof. of the gov't high schools, '98-'11; present post, '11. Add. Kyoto. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujikawa, Yu** D.M., D. Litt., formerly Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. '89; further studied in Germany; was once lecturer on pathology at Kyoto Imp. Univ. Add. Hongo-ku, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujimori, Seikichi** novelist; b. 1892 at Suwa; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in German Literature; has written a number of stories. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujimura, Yoshiro** Baron, Mem. House of Peers. Bar. of Middle Tem., ex-Sect. Chief of the Mitsui Bussan; b. in Kumamoto in 1870; edu. in Law at Cambridge, returning home '03, and entered the Mitsui; its London agent till '10; removed to the head office, but left it soon after. Was Minister of Foreign Affairs and then of Communications in the Kiyoura cabinet, '24. Add. Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujioka, Ichisuke** Dr. Eng. (Japan), Pres. Iwakuni Elec. Tram. and Tokyo Elec. Cos., b. in 1856; grad. from Tokyo Engineering College in '81; appointed its Professor '84; retired '86 to start electric business; chief electrician to the Tokyo Electric Light Co. in '92 and founder of the Tokyo Street Railway Co.; he has been to Europe and America several times for study, on business, etc. and was under Edison for some while. Add. Azabu Higashicho, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujioka, Katsuji** D. Litt., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1872 in Tokyo; grad. from Litt. Coll. Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '97; further studied in Germany '01; after returning home was appointed Assist.-Prof. of his alma mater; full-Prof. '10. Add. Hongo-ku, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujisawa, Ikunosuke** Lawyer, M.P., Adviser to the Minsei- to Party; b. 1859 in Sendai; studied law and became lawyer in '80 and distinguished himself in connexion with the Fukushima affair in '83; elected mem. of Sendai Municipal Assembly, and then of Miyagi Prefectural Assembly, of which he was once Chairman; Parliamentary Secy. of Communication Dep't. July '15; later removed to Home Dep't; Min. of Com. & Ind. '26-27. Add. Uyenohara, Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 810](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n810/mode/1up))

**Fujisawa, Rikitaro** Dr. Sc., Emeritus Prof. of Mathematics at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. House of Peers (elected Sept. '24); b. in Niigata-ken 1861; pursued his special study in England and Germany, '83-87; Exchange-Lecturer in U.S.A. '22; has written several works on mathematics. Add. Koishikawa Suwa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujise, Masajiro** Dir. of the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; b. 1867 at Nagasaki city; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School '85 and entered the Co. to be promoted to present position, meanwhile in charge of branches at London, New York, etc. Add. Shirokane Imazatocho, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujishima, Hampei** Dr. Eng., Dir. Nippon Yusen Kaisha; b. in Miye-ken, 1871; grad. from the Eng. Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ., and entered the Co., '94; sent by the Co. to England to study shipbuilding '98-00. Add. Ichigaya-Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Heitaro** 2nd Baron, one of the millionaires in Osaka; b. in Osaka, 1869; son of late Denzaburo Fujita who started in Osaka the Fujita Firm to undertake contract business with the Government, besides undertaking mining and engineering works. Educated at Cambridge Univ. Add. Kita-ku Amijima, Osaka. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Keishin** Prof. of Fishery at Hokkaido Imp. Univ.; b. 1879 Tokyo; grad. from Sapporo Agr. Coll., '90 and Tokyo Imp. Univ., '92; further studied in England and Germany. Add. Sapporo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Ken-ichi** Chairman Tokyo Chamber of Commerce since '25; b. 1873 at Hirosaki; grad. from the now defunct Meiji Law Coll. '98; in civil service for a time; Manager of Iwaya Shokai, then famous tobacconist in Tokyo; founded the Toyo Salt Industrial Co. which developed later into Dai Nippon Salt Mfg. Co., Kwantung Leased Territory. Add. Shiba Shirokane. Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Shiro** Member of the House of Peers, b. at Tokyo, 1861; attache to Japanese Legation in Vienna, 1886. then in Berlin; afterwards Private Secretary to the Minister of Communication and of Agriculture and Commerce, &c.; Vice-Minister of Agriculture and Commerce, '98; now Pres. of Taiwan Sugar Co. and other concerns. Add. Shiba Mita, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Tokujiro** Dir. of Fujita Bank (Osaka) and other cos. run by the Fujita family, being younger brother of Baron Fujita; b. 1880; grad. from Columbia Univ., '07. Add. Kiyamachi, Kyoto. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Yuzuru** Mng.-Dir. of the Meiji Life Ins. Co.; b. 1865 in Okayama-ken; grad. from Keio '88; further studied at the Pacific Commercial School, San Francisco, '91; a clerk at a certain American store and returned home '95 to enter the present Co.; Manager of its Nagoya Branch '98; present post '17. Add. Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 811](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n811/mode/1up))

**Fujiwara, Ginjiro** President and Mng.-Dir. of the Oji Paper Manufacturing Co. since 1911; b. 1868 in Nagano-ken; graduated from Keio '91 and then took to journalism in the provinces; entered the Mitsui Bank and was transferred to the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha and had charge of its Branches at Shanghai and Taihoku '15. Add. Azabu Shin-ami, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fujiwara, Sakuhei** D. Sc., Meteorologist; b. in 1884 at Shimo Suwa, Nagano-ken; graduated at the Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Theoretical Physics, '09; appointed assistant at the Central Meteorological Observatory the same year; granted the Doctorate '15; promoted to Meteorologist (titular) in '18; proceeded to Europe on official business, '20; Prof. at his alma mater as additional duty, '23; attended the International Meteorological Observatory directors conference in 1926. Is specialized in Theoretical Meteorology, especially vortex motions. Add. Central Meteo. Observatory, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fujiwara, Toshio** Prop. Fujiwara Trading Co., and Mng.-Dir. Naigai Kogyo (International Industrial) Co.; b. 1867, in Okayama-ken; studied in U.S.; was once in the service of the Mitsui Bank; founder of the above Cos. Add. Akasaka-Daimachi, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fujiyama, Raita** President of Nippon Sugar Refinery Co. etc.; Dir. of various concerns; b. in 1865 in Karatsu; grad. at Keio-Gijuku in '90; was in the service of Mitsui Bussan; then entered the present Co. after the notorious scandal culminating in the suicide of its former President; Chairman of Tokyo Chamber of Commerce till '25 as successor of Visc. Shibusawa. Add. Shiba Shirokane, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fukada, Kosan** Litt. Dr., and Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. 1878 at Kanazawa; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Philosophy, class '02 and further studied in England, Germany and France. Works; Translation of Kant's Critique, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fukai, Eigo** Dir. of Nippon Ginko; b. 1871 in Gumma-ken; educated at the Doshisha College, Kyoto; on the staff of the Kokumin Shimbun '98-05; Private Secretary to Gov. of the Bank of Japan. '06-12; accompanied the Peace Envoys to Versailles in 1918 as an unofficial member. Add. Akasaka Hikawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fukuda, Masataro** Gen., ex-Mem. Supreme Council; b. 1870 in Nagasaki-ken; Sub-Lieut. '87; Captain '95; stayed in Berlin and Vienna as Military Attache for years; took part in '04-05 war as a staff officer to Marshal Oyama; Colonel '07; Maj.-Gen. and brigade commander '11; Chief of Staff, Military Department, Kwantung Government; Lieut.-Gen. and Commander of the 5th Div. '16; Vice-Chief of Gen. Staff; Full Gen. in '22; Com-in-Chief of Formosan Garrison. '18-22; Com. of Tokyo Garrison at the time of the Earthquake. Add. Yoyogi near Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fukuda, Tokuzo** D.Litt., Prof. of Tokyo Coll. of Commerce; b. 1874 in Tokyo; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial School, class '94; was dispatched by the Gov. to Germany, France, and Italy to make further study in economics; again visited Europe and America '25-26. Works: several works on Economics. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fukuhara, Ryojiro** Pres. Peers' School since '23, Chairman of Imp. Art Gallery, etc.; b. 1869 in Miye-ken; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '92; held various junior posts at Central Government and in the provinces for some years after; as Councillor and Inspector of the Education Dep't '98; dispatched to Europe '99 to investigate affairs of educational administration; Bureau Director in '04; Vice-Minister '11-16; Mem. House of Peers (nom. in '16); Pres. Tohoku Univ. in '17. Add. Ushigome Kita-machi, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 812](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n812/mode/1up))

**Fukuhara, Toshimaru** 2nd Baron, Mem. House of Peers; Dir. of Onoda Portland Cement Mfg. Co.; b. 1886 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Eng. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '01; Lecturer at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '02-'04; Army technical expert '04-'06; despatched to Europe and America on official mission, '08-'09; was also on the faculty of Waseda Univ. Add. Yotsuya, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Fukui, Kikusaburo** Mng.-Dir. of the Mitsui Bussan; b. 1866 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School in 1883 and then entered the service of the Mitsui, to be attached to the branches at Shanghai, Singapore, Hongkong, Osaka, etc., and then the agent at New York for several years; was on the suite of the Peace Envoy Dec. '18. Add. Mitsui & Co., Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Fukuoka, Hidei** 2nd Visc., L.L.B., M.L. (Yale), Prof. at Tokyo School for Foreign Languages and Peers School, etc.; b. 1871 in Kochi-ken; went to America and studied at Washington, Lee, Cumberland and Yale Universities. Add. Koishikawa, Kanatomi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Fukuzawa, Ichitaro** Chancellor of Keio-gijuku; b. 1863, eldest son of the late Y. Fukuzawa; educated at Yale Univ. after he finished the Keio. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Fukuzawa, Momosuke** ex-M.P., Pres. of Daido Elec. Power Co., etc., brother-in-law of the Fukuzawa brothers, being husband of their sister; b. in 1864 at Kawagoye; studied at Keio; and further at a Commercial Coll. in U.S.A. Returning home '89, he first entered Tokyo Ice Works., then Oji Paper Mill, and finally Hokkaido Colliery and Steamship Co., of which he was agent for Tokyo. Add. Shimo-shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Funakoshi, Mitsunojo** 2nd Baron, Crown Mem. of House of Peers; b. 1867 in Hiroshima; studied in Germany, '86-93; passed Diplomatic Service Examination in '94; Eleve Consul at Vladivostok the same year, at San Francisco in '95; Legation Sec. at Washington in '97; to London in '99; Secretary at head office '02; Sec. at Vienna in '03; at Berlin '06-09; and at For. Office and lastly Emb. Counsellor at Berlin '12-14. Add. Motozonocho, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Funatsu, Shin-ichiro** former Con-Gen. at Mukden; b. 1873 in Saga-ken; edu. at the Shoin-gakusha, Saga, and passed the "Foreign student exam." of the Foreign Office '94; was Consul-General at Tientsin and then at Mukden '24-26. Represents Japanese Cotton-mill interest in China. Add. Shanghai, China. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Furuichi, Koi** Baron (cr. 1919), Dr. Eng., Privy Councillor since '24; Emeritus Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. at Himeji in 1854; studied engineering in France '75-80; was successively Director of the Public Works Bureau, Home Office; Vice-Minister of Communications, Director-General of the Government Railways and Director of Seoul-Fusan R'ly '03-07; Pres. of Tokyo Subway Co. till '25. He is one of the best amateur "no" singers and actors. Add. Hongo Yumi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 813](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n813/mode/1up))

**Furukawa, Toranosuke** Baron (cr. '15), President of the Furukawa Mining Co., son of the late celebrated Ichibei Furukawa and inherited his father's immense properties including more than twenty mines; b. in 1882 and graduated from Mining at Columbia, U.S.A., class '07. Add. Nihonbashi Setomono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Furuya, Shigetsuna** Minister to Argentina, Paraguay & Uruguay; b. 1876 in Ehime-ken; edu. at Doshi-sha, Kyoto, and Michigan; passed the Dip. and Con. service exam. in '02; 3rd Emb. Sec. at London, '08; 2nd Emb. Sec., '10; Sectional Chief at the head office '16; Con.-Gen. at Ottawa, '18; Emb. Counsellor at Washington, '20; Minister to Mexico '22. Add. Buenos Aires. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Futa-ara, Yoshinori** 1st Count; b. 1886, of the house of Date, formerly a daimyo, and was adopted by the family; studied law at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Secretary to a local Govt. for a time; on the suite of the Crown Prince when he visited England '21. Lady Futa-ara is sister to the late Prince Kitashirakawa. Add. Yotsuya Kasumiga-oka, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Futakami, Hyoji** Crown Mem. of House of Peers nominated in '24, Chief Secretary of the Privy Council; b. in 1878 in Toyama prefecture; graduated from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., in '04 and passed the Higher Civil Servce exam. the same year; was long connected with the Department of Communications as jun. secretary, Counsellor, etc. before transferring to the Privy Council. Add. Off. Res., Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Futaki, Kenzo** D.M., Expert of the Epidemic Diseases Research Institute, Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1873 in Akita-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Hongo Motofujicho, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Gamo, Yasusato** Dir. of Fukushima Higher Com. Sch. since its creation in 1922; b. in Nagasaki-ken, 1873; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch. (Special class) in '98; visited England, Germany and U.S.A. to study commerce; Prof. of Kumamoto High Sch.; Dir. of Ehime Com. Sch.; Prof. Yamaguchi Higher Com. Sch. Add. Fukushima. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Go, Seinosuke** 2nd Baron, Mem. House of Peers, President of the Tokyo Electric Light Co., and Oriental Steel Foundry, Dir. of various Cos.; b. 1863 in Tokyo, son of late Junzo Go; studied in Europe; after several years in Govt. services he entered business circles, was chairman of Tokyo Stock Ex. till '24. Add. Koj. Kaminibancho, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Gonda, Yasunosuke** writer; b. 1887 in Tokyo; edu. at Foreign Language School, German course, and next at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Literature, making fine art his speciality, class '13. Works; Economy and Civilization, Applied Art, etc. Is also connected with Ohara Social Research Institute. Add. Otsuka Kubocho, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Goral, Kinzo** (penname Sosen), writer and Prof. at Waseda; b. in 1875 in Ibaraki-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in French Law, class '00, also in France and Germany '04-09; edit. of the Yomiuri '14; edit. of the monthly magazine Taikan, Marq. Okuma's organ at the time, in '18. Works; France and French- men, Political Ethics, Future of Social Revolution, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 814](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n814/mode/1up))

**Goto, Fumio** Dir. of General Affairs, Formosan Gov't-General since '24; b. 1884 in Oita. Edu. in Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '08; Sec. or Chief Police Inspector in the provinces till '13; transferred to the Police Bureau, Home Office, the same year, first as sectional chief and then its Director, and last to the present post. Add. Taihoku, Formosa. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Goto Seitaro** D.Sc., Mem. of the Imp. Academy, Prof. of Zoology and Dean of the Coll. Sc., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1867 in Yamaguchi-ken. Edu. at the above institution, '90, and further at the Univ. Hall, and went to America to study entomology; attended the 3rd International Zoological Conference held at Leyden, Holland, in '95; Prof. at the First High School on return and next at the present institution. Add. Imp. Tokyo Univ. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Goto, Shimpei** Viscount (Baron '06, Visc. in '22), Mem. of House of Peers; b. 1856 in Iwate-ken; studied medicine in Japan and Germany; chief of Nagoya Hospital '76-78; App. Sanitary Commissioner at the time of the Japan-China War, and then Director of the Sanitary Bureau. Home Office; Dir. of the Civil Adm. Bureau, Formosa; Pres. of S. Manchuria Railway '06; Minister of Communications in '08, combining the office of Deputy-Pres. of the Colonial Bureau. Retired in '11 but again held it with an additional post of Pres. of I.G.R. in the 3rd Katsura Cabinet; formed the Doshikai with late Prince Katsura, but left it in '13; Min. Home Affairs and next of For. Affairs '16-18; Mayor of Tokyo '21-22; Home Minister. '23; visited Russia Dec. '27. Add. Azabu Miyamuracho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Haga, Gonshiro** Lieutenant (ret.) Dir. Gov. Sericultural Institute at Nakano; b. in 1867 in Saitama-ken; graduated from Agriculture, Imperial Tokyo University, '92. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Hamada, Kunimatsu** Lawyer; M.P.; b. 1868 in Miye-ken; grad. from Chuo Univ.; returned to the House of Representatives thrice from Miye-ken and was once Vice-Pres. of the House. Add. Kojimachi-ku Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Hamada, Tsunenosuke** Governor of Kyoto since '26; b. 1871 in Kochi-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '96; served as Counsellor or Chief Police Inspector in the provinces till '07; Gov. of Toyama, '07; of Miyagi till '20; after some years on the retired list was Dir. of Colonial Affairs Bureau '25-26. Add. Kyoto Pref. Office. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Hamaguchi, Tan** B.A. (Cambridge), founder of Inawashiro Hydro-Elec. Power Co.; b. 1872 in Wakayama-ken; grad. Keio Univ. '91 and Waseda, '94; further studied at Cambridge; M.P. in '04 from Wakayama-ken. Add. Koj. Shimo-rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Hamaguchi, Yuko** M. P., Pres. of the Minsei-to Party (former Kensei-kai); b. 1870 in Kochi-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. '95; Director of the Tobacco Monopoly Bureau '07-12; Vice-Min. of Communications '12-13; Vice-Min. of Finance in 2nd Okuma Cabinet '14-15; Parl. Jun. Secy. to Finance Dept. for a while in '15; Minister of Finance in '24; Minister of Home Affairs '26-27. Add. Zoshigaya, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Hanai, Takuzo** D.L., Barrister. Mem. House of Peers (nom. '22); b. 1868 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from the English Law School in Tokyo, class '85; passed Barristers' Examination a year or two after, is an acknowledged authority in criminal affairs. Has written several popular essays on Criminal Law; M.P. for several terms and was once Vice-Pres. of House of Representatives. Add. Kanda Nishiki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 815](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n815/mode/1up))

**Hanihara, Masanao** Ambassador unattached '23; b. 1876 in Yamanashi-ken; grad. from Waseda '97; attache at Seoul Legation '99; at Washington, '01; Secy. at the same, '02; then Sec. Chief at Foreign Office; Consul-Gen. at San Francisco '16-17; Dir. Political Affairs, '17-19; was among the four Delegates to the Washington Conference 1921-'22; Vice-Min. of For. Affairs '19-'22; Amb. to Washington, '23-24. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Hanta, Ryotaro** Partner of Hunter & Co. of Osaka, Dir. of the Osaka Marine Ins. Co. and Aud. of Osaka Shosen Kaisha; b. 1871 in Kobe; grad. from the Engin. Coll., Glasgow Univ., '88. Add. Nishi-ku, Osaka. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Hara, Kado** lawyer, D.L., Minister of Justice since Apr. '27; b. 1867 in Nagano-ken; grad. at Tokyo University, '89; Councillor, Dep't Agr. and Com. in '09; Chief of the Tokyo Mine Inspection Office and that of Osaka '01-03; then opened law office. Add. Koj. Iida-machi, Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Hara, Kingo** Pres. of the Kyoritsu Fire Ins. Co. (non-tariff); b. 1867 in Tokyo; edu. at the Tokyo Higher Com. School. Was for many years in the service of Mitsui Bussan; organized the above Co. with Mitsui's backing. Add. Azabu Hommura, Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Hara, Kunizo** ex-Pres. of the One Hundredth Bank, etc.; b. 1883 in Hyogo-ken; adopted son of Rokuro Hara, a prominent retired businessman; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '07 and passed Higher Civil Service Examination the next year. Add. Kita Shinagawa-juku, Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Hara, Rokuro** a prominent business-man: b. 1844 in Tajima and took part in the work of the Restoration, having fought on the Imperial side in the civil war; studied in England and U.S. '71-77. Entered business circles and founded with a few others the One Hundredth Bank: Pres. Yokohama Specie Bank '83-90. Add. Gotenyama, near Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Hara, Tomitaro** Pres. of 2nd Bank, head of the Hara Firm, Yokohama; b. in Gifu-ken 1870; studied at Waseda Univ.; was adopted by the Hara family as husband of its daughter; is a Director of the Yokohama Fire Insurance Co. and a leading businessman in Yokohama; noted as collector of art objects. Add. Bentendori, Yokohama. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Harada, Tasuku** L.L.D. (Edinburgh), D.D. (Amherst), ex-Pres. of Doshisha, Kyoto, '07-19; Professor at Hawaiian University since '20; b. 1863 in Kumamoto; studied at Foreign Language School in Kumamoto, and later at the Doshisha; entered Yale and grad. from Divinity School, class '91; travelled through Europe in '92; pastor in Tokyo, Kyoto and Kobe; ed. of Rikugo Zasshi, and the "Christian World". Pres. of the Christian Endeavor Union of Japan '93-'06 and attended the world convention in London '00 and similar religious meetings abroad; lectured at Harvard, Yale and other Universities in America '09. Add. Honolulu. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 816](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n816/mode/1up))

**Haruki, Ichiro** D.L., Prof. Law at Imp. Univ. of Tokyo; b. in 1870 at Tokyo; grad. from the Imp. Univ. of Tokyo, '94; further studied Roman Law in Germany, 1900-04; Prof., Imp. Kyoto Univ. in '05; transferred to Tokyo later. Add Hongo Nezu, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hasegawa, Manjiro** (penname Nyozekan), journalist and socialist writer; b. 1875 in Tokyo; was once on the staff of the Nippon, the Osaka Asahi and other journals; started a monthly organ "We" in '18 with his socialist friends. Works; London, Criticism of Modern State, Modern Society Criticized, etc. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hasegawa, Shogo** Dir. Kisha-Seizo (Car Mfg.) Co.; b. in Osaka, 1872; grad. Mechanics at the Imp. Tokyo Univ. '96. Add. Tennoji, Osaka. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hashimoto, Keizaburo** Mem. House of Peers, Pres. of Yokohama Warehouse Co.; b. 1865 in Niigata; grad. College of Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '90; Secretary of the Privy Council '92; Chief of Accountant's Bureau and then Vice-Min. of Finance '11-12; Vice-Min. of Agr. and Com., '13-14; Pres. the Hoden Petroleum Co. till it merged with the Japan Petroleum Co. in '27. Add. Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hashimoto, Kizo** M.P., Pres. of the Dojima Bldg. Co., Dir. of the Hashimoto Steamship Co.; b. 1872, stayed abroad for study many years. Add. Shiba Nihon-enoki, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hashizume, Sutesaburo** Dir. of Kanegafuchi Spinning Co. Ltd.; born 1867 in Fukushima-ken; grad. from the Law College of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. in 1895; entered the service of the Kanegafuchi Spinning Co. and promoted successively to the present post. Add. Totsuka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hata, Benzo** M.D., Prof. of Surgery and Dean of Coll. Med., Hokkaido Imp. Univ.; b. 1879 in Fukui. Grad. from Medicine, Imp. Tokyo Univ. class '02. After serving as Assis. at the Alma mater's hospital and next at the Sapporo Hospital, he was sent for study to Berlin and Prague in '12; on return home in '14 appointed Prof. on the establishment of the present college and elected its Dean. Add. Sapporo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hata Eitaro** Lieut.-Gen., Vice-Minister of War; born 1872; graduating from the Military Cadets School in 1893 began his military career first as sub-lieutenant; further studied at the Military Staff College, which he graduated in 1903; after holding the posts of military attache to Japanese Embassy in London, regimental commander, etc. successively was appointed Director of Military Affairs Bureau, War Office, in 1923; then present post in '26. Was on the staff of the Japanese expedition to Tsingtao under General Kamio in 1914 and rendered distinguished services co-operating with the British forces under Brigadier General Barnardiston. Add. War Office, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hata Ryotaro** ex-Minister to Brazil ('20-23); b. 1867 in Nagano; entered diplomatic service after graduating from Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '90; Emb. Counsellor at Berlin '06; Secretary of Foreign Office '08; on retired list, '25. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hata, Sahachiro** M.D., co-worker of Dr. Erlich in the discovery of No. 606 or Salvarsan; b. 1872 in Iwami; grad. with honors from Medical Dept. of Okayama High School in '95; was Dr. Kitazato's assistant at the Epidemic Investigation Laboratory '98-07; studied at Berlin and Frankfort '07-11; Depart. Chief of Keio Hospital. Add. Nakameguro, near Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 817](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n817/mode/1up))

**Hata, Toyosuke** M.P., ex-Parl. Vice-Min. of Navy; b. in Tokyo, 1872; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '96; Governor of various localities; Parl. Vice-Min. of Communications and then of Navy '24-25. Add. Tsukiji, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))
