**Hatai, Shinkishi** Ph. D., Prof. at Faculty of Science, Imp. Tohoku Univ. (Sendai), Prof. at Pennsylvania Univ. (U.S.A.). Educated at the Tohoku Gakuin; studied zoology under Prof. Goto at the 1st High School in Tokyo; studied at Chicago University and appointed lecturer of the alma mater upon graduation; later Prof. at Cincinnati University and then Prof. at Pennsylvania University and Dir. of Biological Dep't. of the Wistar Institute attached to the Univ.; appointed Prof. at the Imp. Tohoku Univ. '23 while holding professorship at Pennsylvania Univ. Add. Wistar Institute, Pennsylvania Univ. (U.S.A.) & Imp. Tohoku Univ. (Sendai). — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hatano, Seiichi** Litt. Dr.; Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ. since '17; b. in 1877 at Matsumoto; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Philosophy, class '99; studied in Germany '04-06; Prof. at Waseda till '17 after his return home. Works; Origin of Christianity, History of Western Philosophy, Spinoza, etc. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hatano, Shogoro** Dir. of Mitsui Firm, Aud. of Mitsui Bank, Mining Co., etc.; b. 1859 in Tokyo; grad. from Keio, '76; was on the staff of Jiji when it was started; entered the Foreign Office and was appointed Consul at Tientsin, next Sec. at the head office; M.P. in '21-24. Add. Koj. Kami-Nibancho, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hatoyama, Hideo** D.C.L., Lawyer, formerly Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1884 in Tokyo; younger brother of Ichiro Hatoyama, M.P.; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '08; further studied in Germany and France. Add. Koishikawa, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hatoyama, Ichiro** Lawyer, M.P. & Chief Secretary to the Cabinet, Company Dir.; b. in 1883, eldest son of late Dr. Kazuo Hatoyama. Grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., Class '07; began practice at father's office and at same time taught at Waseda; was for years Chairman of Tokyo Municipal Office; seceded from Seiyukai when it split and was one of those who formed Seiyu-Honto Party; returned to Seiyukai in 1926. Add. Koishikawa, Otowa, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hatta, Saburo** D.Sc., Prof. of Zoology and Entomology, Imp. Hokkaido Univ.; b. 1865 in Kumamoto. Grad. from the Coll. Sc., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '91; was app. Prof. at the Peers School, 93; at the Sapporo Agr. Coll., which has been expanded to the present Univ.; visited America, '06; sent for study to Europe, '12-15. Add. Sapporo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hatta, Yoshiaki** Perm. Vice-Min. of Railways since '26; b. in Tokyo; grad. from Civil Eng. Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '08; entered the service of the Gov't Railways, of which he had been Dir. of Construction Bureau till promotion to the present post in '26. Add. Nishi Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hattori, Kintaro** Crown mem. House of Peers, Pres. of K. Hattori & Co. Ltd., that run Seikosha Watch and Clock Works; Dir. of First Mutual Life Ins. Co. and of Chosen Industrial Co. Ltd.; b. 1860 in Tokyo; established in business 1881 and incorporated '17. Add. Ginza Shichome, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 818](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n818/mode/1up))

**Hattori, Unokichi** Litt. Dr., Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1867 in Fukushima-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., Philosophy '87-90; studied Chinese classics in China and method of teaching in Germany, '99-07; Prof. at alma mater on return; Prof. at Chinese Univ., Peking, till '09; Prof. at Harvard, U.S.A. '15. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayakawa, Tetsuya** ex-M.P., b. Okayama, 1865; grad. Sapporo Agri. Coll. '84; studied law in U.S.A. and Germany; entered the diplomatic service, '85 and attache at Seoul Legation until '88; to Consulate at San Francisco; to the Legation at Berlin, '89-92; Private Secy. to the Minister of Agriculture, '92-97; Minister Resident, '97-98; Secy. of the Cabinet, '98; Dir. of the Political Bureau, Foreign Office, in the 1st Okuma Cabinet. Succeeded in procuring foreign loan for the Hokkaido Colliery. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayakawa, Yoshitaro** Pres. of Hayakawa Bill Broker Bank; b. 1885 in Tokyo; grad. from Keio '06; Served as Military interpreter and then as chief of Loan Dep't of the Tokai Bank, Ltd., before he promoted the present Bank in '18. Add. Ushigome Funagawara, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Aisaku** b. 1873 in Gumma, studied in U.S.A. for some years and entered business after his return home; Mng. of the Imperial hotel '[?]0-21. Add. [illegible] Komazawa-mura, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Gonsuke** Baron (cr. '07) Ex-Ambassador to the Court of St. James ('20-25); b. at Aizu 1860; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and entered Foreign Office '85; Consul and Secretary of the Legation, '87-09; Director of Commercial Bureau '9[?]; Minister at Seoul '99; at Peking '06-08 and again in '16-1[?]; Ambassador to Rome, '08-16; Gov. of Kwantung Leased Territory '19-20; Ambassador in England '20, but returning home in Sept. '25, resigned the post; accompanied Prince Chichibu to England in 1925 as a member of the suite, returning home with the Prince in January '27. Add. Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Haruo** M.D., Prof. Medicine, the Imp. Tokyo Univ. and Dir. of the Infectious Diseases Investigation Laboratory; b. 1874 in Aichi-ken and was adopted into the family; studied at the above Coll.; its Assist.-Prof. 1900 and Prof. '09. Add. Shitaya Yanaka, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Hirotaro** 2nd Count, Mem. House of Peers & Lecturer at the Literature Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Pres. of the Imperial Education Association; b. 186[?]; grad. in Philosophy from Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Prof. at Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch. and Master of Ceremonies for a time; chairman of Educational Investigation Committee since '22. Add. 399 Yoyohata Hatagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Kiroku** President of Keio Univ; b. 1872 at Karatsu; edu. at Keio in Literature and further studied Diplomacy at Paris 1901-04; M.P. in '20 and Senior Secretary of For. Office. Works; "Modern European Diplomacy," etc. Add. Shimo-Shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 819](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n819/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Kyujiro** ex-Minister to Siam ('25-28); b. in 1882 in Tochigi-ken; edu. at Waseda, in Law, class '03; passed the Diplomatic Service exam. in '06; has since been in the diplomatic and consular service. Eleve Con. at Seattle, '08; Consul at Tientsin, '14; 2nd Emb. Sec. at London, '18; Con-Gen. at Fuchow, '19; at Hankow '23-25. Add. Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Raizaburo** D.L., Perm. Vice-Minister of Justice '24-27; b. in 1878; grad. from the Chuo Univ., '97; Judge till '21; Dir. Bureau of Criminal Affairs. Add. Koj. Yoban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Tsuruichi** D.Sc. and mathematician, Dean Sc. Coll., Imp. Tohoku Univ. at Sendai; b. 1873 in Tokushima-ken; edu. at Coll. of Science, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Assist.-Prof. Imp. Kyoto Univ. '98; Prof. Tokyo Higher Normal School, '07; Prof. Imp. Tohoku Univ. since '11; is associate of various scientific societies of Germany, France, Italy, etc. Add. Sendai. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Higuchi, Hideo** M.P.; b. 1875 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; was lecturer at the Shinshu Univ. and the Bukkyo Univ. Add. Nippori, near Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Higuchi, Shigeji** M.D. (Japan and Rostock, Germany), Obstetrician and Gynecologist; b. 1876 in Niigata-ken; edu. in Medicine, First High School, class '99, and further at Breslau, Rostock, Halle, Berlin and London between '00 and '11 with some break spent at home; now Prof. at Tokyo Jikei-kai Medical Coll. and is connected with its hospital; also practices at his Higuchi Hospital at Shiba Tamura-cho. Add. Nishikubo Shirayama, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hijikata, Kyucho** Deputy Governor of the Bank of Japan since '26; b. 1870; graduated from the Law Coll. of Imp. Univ. of Tokyo, '93; entered the service of the Bank of Japan with which he was connected for years finally becoming its director; Pres. of the Industrial Bank of Japan 1918-22; re-entered the Bank of Japan in '26, being appointed its Deputy-Governor. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hijikata, Yasushi** D.L., Crown Mem. House of Peers since '22 and ex-Dean of the Law College, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1859; edu. in Law at the Univ. in '82, and with a short break occasioned by his study in England from which he returned in '94 he was on the University faculty till '22. Add. Kojimachi Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hirai, Masamichi** M. D., Surg.-Gen., ex-Pres. of Red Cross Hospital; b. in Wakasa in 1863; edu. in Medicine, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '89; attached to Otsu Regiment '90; studied in Germany '97-00; Director of Tokyo Garrison Hos., Vice-Pres. of Red Cross Hos. '06; then its Pres. '09-20. Add. Ushigome Yarai, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hiranuma, Ki-ichiro** Baron (cr. '26), D.L., Vice-Pres. of Privy Council; b. 1865 in Okayama-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. '88; appointed to the Bench, having filled chair of sectional chief and Procurator in the Tokyo Appeal Court, '95; Bureau Director '06-11, Vice-Minister of Justice '11-12; Procurator-Gen. till '21; then Pres. of the Supreme Court; Privy Councillor '24. Add. Nishi-Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hiranuma, Yoshiro** D.L., Dean of Commercial Dep't. Waseda Univ.; b. in 1864 in Tsuyama, being brother of above; was in journalism for some years; educational work at Sendai High School, Osaka Com. School, etc; Prof. at Waseda in '04. Add. Waseda Tsurumakicho, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 820](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n820/mode/1up))

**Hiraoka, Ki** pioneer of rolling-stock makers in Japan, ex-Vice-Pres. Osaka Loco. Works; b. 1855 in Tokyo; went to U.S.A. in '71, and entered a rolling stock shop, N.Y., returning home about '80; appointed overseer of the works at Shimoasai, till about '90, and started the Hiraoka Rolling-Stock Works at Honjo, Tokyo, which was fused with Osaka Loco. Works. Add. Kojimachi Nagatacho, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hiratsuka, Akiko** Miss, author and a "new woman"; b. 1886 in Tokyo; edu. at Ochanomizu Girls' High School and Womens Univ., Tokyo, in Domestic Economy. Works; Women's Life of Today, Men and Women of the Day, Rights of Women and Children; has also written some stories. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hirayama, Kinzo** M.D., Dir. of the Gastro-enteric Hospital in Tokyo, etc; b. 1876 in Ibaraki-ken. Edu. at Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '03; served as assistant at the alma mater '03-06, and was next appointed Vice-Pres. of the Hospital founded by Dr. Nagayo; studied at Heidelberg, Berlin, etc. '07-10. Add. Koi. Hara-machi, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hirayama, Narinobu** Baron (cr. '25), Pres. of Japan Red Cross since '20 and Mem. of the Privy Council; b. 1850 in Kagoshima; was once in the Dep't of Finance and of Home Affairs. Nominated Member of House of Peers in '93; Steward to Prince Arisugawa, in '07. Add. Koishikawa Hara-machi, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hirayama, Shin** D.Sc., Mem. of Imp. Academy, Prof. of Astronomy, at the Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1867 in Tokyo; edu. at Science Coll. of the Univ., in '90; further studied at the University Hall and in England and Germany; Prof. at his alma mater on his return home in '94. Add. Azabu Nagasaka-cho, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hiroi, Isamu** D. Eng. (Japan). Emeritus Prof. of Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1862 in Kochi; edu. at Sapporo Coll., class '81; proceeded to U.S. Dec. '83; was successively Assist. Eng. in Miss. Riv. Comm., Eng'g Office of C. Shaler Smith, Edge Moore Bridge Co., etc. '84-87; Chief Engr. to Hokkaido-cho '89-95; Dir. of Harbour Works at Hakodate and Otaru '96-07; Prof. of Civ. Eng'g at Imp. Tokyo Univ. '99-19; Mem. of Harbor Invest. Comm., Mem. National Research Council and Mem. of Shanghai Harb. Invest. Comm., '21. Works (in English): Plate-girder Construction, fifth ed., the statistically Indeterminate Stresses in frames commonly used for bridges, 2nd ed. Add. Ichigaya Nakano-cho, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hiro-oka, Keizo** Pres. the Kashima Bank, Osaka, and is head of the Kashima family, one of the millionaire houses in the city; b. 1876 in Tokyo, 2nd s. of Visc. Hitotsu-yanagi and adopted by the family; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ. '00, and his activity afterward has been confined to various interests of his father-in-law whose daughter he married. Add. Kashima Bank, Osaka. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hirosawa, Kinjiro** Count, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1871 at Yamaguchi; proceeded to England for study '86; Private Secretary to the Premier; Min. to Spain and Portugal '21-24. He is a son of the celebrated State Councillor Hirosawa who took active part in the work of Restoration. Add. Azabu Zaimokucho, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 821](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n821/mode/1up))

**Hirota, Koki** Minister to the Hague since '27; b. 1878 in Fukuoka; edu. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; passed Dip. Service Examin., '06; attached the same year to the Chinese Legation; to England '09; Sectional Chief of the Commercial Bureau '14; 1st Emb. Sec. at Washington '18; Sectional Chief of the Intelligence Bureau '21; Dir. Euro-American Bureau, Foreign Office, '24-27. Add. Japanese Legation, Hague. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Hirota, Tsukasa** M. D., specialist in Children's Diseases, Emeritus Prof., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1856 in Kochi-ken; edu. at Coll. Med. of said institution, class '80; served at the Kumamoto Med. School and its hospital, '81-83; further studied at Strasburg, '85-88; Prof. at his alma mater, '89-21; was once Physician-in-Ordinary to the Court. Add. Iidamachi Rokuchome, Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Homma, Hisao** a writer and Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 188[?] at Yonezawa; edu. at Waseda in English Literature, class '09. Works: Current Thoughts and Literature, Life and Art, Essentials of Ellen Kay, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Honda, Iwajiro** Dir. of the Tokyo Higher Sericulture School; b. in 1866 in Oita-ken; edu. at Tokyo Higher Agr. and For. School, class '88; was expert of the Dept. of Agr. and Com. and Dir. of Tokyo Ser. Institute. Add. Nishi Sugamo-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Honda, Kotaro** D. Sc., Prof. at Tohoku (Sendai) Imp. Univ. since '09, Adm. of Gov't. Metal Laboratory; b. in Aichi-ken in '70; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Physics, class '97; further studied at the Imp. Univ. Hall, specializing in Electro-Magnetism; sent to Europe by Gov't. for further study; distinguished by his researches in steel; won the Imp. Academy prize; invented the Permanent Magnet patented in Japan and other countries. Add. Sendai. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Honda, Kumataro** ex-Ambassador to Germany; b. 1874 in Wakayama-ken; edu. at Tokyo Hogaku-in in Law but left unfinished, '94; passed the Foreign Office Clerks Exam., '95 and Dip. and Con. Service Exam. '98; Attache in China and next Belgium, '99; Per. Sec. to the Foreign Minister, '01; Member of the Peace Envoy suite to America; 2nd Sec. of Embassy at London, '97; Consul-Gen. at Harbin, '12; Emb. Counsellor at London, '14; Minister to Switzerland, '18; Minister to Austria, '22; Ambassador in '23 and ordered to proceed to Turkey; accredited to Germany '23-26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Honda, Seiroku** Dr. Dendrology, Emeritus Prof. Agr. Coll. Imp. Tokyo Univ., ('00-27); b. 1866 in Saitama-ken; edu. Dendrology. Agr. College, '88; further studied in Germany. Add. Komaba, Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Hongo, Fusataro** Gen. (ret.); b. 1860 in Hyogo-ken; grad. Mil. Cadet School and Sub-Lieut. of Infantry '89; Maj.-Gen. '05; attached to the Berlin Legation during Japan-Russia War and commissioned with exchange of prisoners; Dir. Personnel Bureau of War Office in '09; Div. Chief of Mil. Education Board '10; Vice-Min. '13-14; Div. Com. and of Tsingtao Garison '17; full Gen. '18. Add. Aoyama Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 822](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n822/mode/1up))

**Honjo, Eijiro** Dr. of Economics, (Japan), Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ., his alma mater; further studied in Europe '21-22. Works: History of Socialism in Japan, Researches on Economic History, etc. Add. Imp. Kyoto Univ. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Hori, Keijiro** Pres. of Osaka Shosen Kaisha; b. 1867 in Kanazawa; edu. in Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '93; entered the same year Russian Oil Co.: the present Co. in '95; Chief of Chemulpo branch, etc.; Dir. in '08; Vice-Pres. '11; Pres. '15. Add. Edobori Kitadori, Nishiku, Osaka. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Hori, Yoshikata** Counsellor at the Legation in Peking since 1926; b. 1885 in Kagoshima; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and its post-graduate course, class '08; passed the Dip. and Con. Serv. Exam. the same year; was in the Consular service at New York, '11; Vancouver, '13; Canton, '16; Pers. Sec. to Foreign Minister, then 1st Legation Secy. in China '17-21; commissioner of political affairs to the Siberian Expedition, '22; Consul-Gen. in London, '23-25. Add. Japanese Legation, Peking. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Horiguchi, Kumaichi** ex-Minister to Brazil; b. 1865 in Niigata-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '93; passed Diplomatic Service Exam. and Eleve-Con. '94; Legation Secretary in Brazil '99-06; Sweden, '08-09; Mexico, '09-13; and Madrid, '13-18; Min. to Brazil '18-24, placed on retired list, '25. Add. Foreign Office, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Horikiri, Zembei** M.P., Prof. at Keio; b. in Fukushima-ken; edu. at Keio, class '03; studied finance in Harvard, Cambridge and Berlin Univ.; on the editorial staff of the Jiji; Private Sec. to the Premier in the Takahashi Ministry; Parl. Counsellor, Dep't of Com. & Agr. till Ap'l. '25. Add. Ichigaya Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Horikiri, Zenjiro** Dir. of Reconstruction Bureau, Home Dept.; b. 1884 in Fukushima Pref.; younger brother of Zembei Horikiri; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. '09; on graduation, entered Home Affairs Dept.; after holding the posts of Appraiser, Secretary, Chairman of Town Planning Committee, etc., was appointed to the present post in '26. Add Koishikawa Takata Oimatsucho, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Horikoshi, Zenjuro** Pres. Horikoshi Firm; b. in 1863 at Ashikaga; in early days connected with Mr. Mason's in New York carrying on business in Japanese silk goods; returning home he organized the Horikoshi Shokai with branches at New York, London, Paris, Lyons, etc. Add. Tsukiji, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Horiuchi, Saburo** Vice-Adm.; b. 1869; early entered the Navy and was gradually promoted till attaining the present post; was Dir. of Military Affairs Bureau, Navy Dept. and Com. Training Squadron, Dir. Naval Staff Coll. ('22-23), etc. Add. Navy Office. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Horiye, Sen-ichiro** D. L. (Japan), L.L.M. (Michigan), lawyer, Lecturer at Central Univ. (Tokyo) etc.; b. '76 in Hiroshima-ken; edu. at Tokyo Hogaku-in (now Central Univ.), class '99, the National English Ins., '01, and at Michigan, '07. Meanwhile passed the Judicial and Barrister's Exam. '02 and served at Dr. Kishi's Office for some years. Works: Does Suicide Nullify Insurance Contracts?, etc. Add. Kyobashi Minami Nabe-cho, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Hoshi, Hajime** Pres. of Hoshi Pharmaceutical Co.: b. 1874 in Fukushima; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and Columbia Univ.; published monthly "Japan and America" in N. Y.; dispatched by Govt. to Europe to inspect industry. Returned '07 and started present Co.; was once elected M.P. Add. Shinagawa Gotenyama, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 823](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n823/mode/1up))

**Hoshijima, Jiro** M. P. social reformer and journalist; b. in Okayama-ken in 1887; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. '17; Manager of Y.M.S. of said Univ., and Dir. of Central Legal Advisory Council. Add. Hongo-Masago-cho, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Hoshino, Seki** Chairman of Tokyo Business-men's Confederation; Member of the Tokyo Chamber of Commerce, Mng-Dir. of the Tokyo Printing Co.; b. 1854 in Tokyo, started printing business in '74; went to America in '87 to investigate printing business; is promoter of the Oji Paper Mill; Mem. House of Rep. in '12. Add. Nihombashi Hamacho, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Hoshino, Sho** Pres. of the Kawasaki Dai Hyaku Bank; b. 1876 in Hiroshima pref.; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. '05; entered the Nihon Ginko, and served at its London branch '09-12; returned home and became the examiner of the Western branches; Dir. of the Kyoto branch '13; private secretary to the Governor of the Nihon Ginko '15-17; Dir. of the Nagoya branch '18; Dir. of the Osaka branch which resigned in '27. Add. Akasaka Daimachi, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Hosokawa, Moritatsu** 3rd Marquis (suc. '14); Mem. House of Peers; b. 1883. His house formerly held the fief of Kumamoto. Add. Koji-machi, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Hotari, Riichiro** Ph. D. (Chicago Univ.). Lecturer at Waseda; b. at Futsukaichi, Fukuoka-ken in 1881; edu. at Tokyo Hogakuin Univ., South California Univ., and Chicago, devoting himself to the study of Divinity and Philosophy; Lecturer at Waseda after returning home and was sentenced to imprisonment for his socialistic article in a magazine but the execution was suspended. Works; Religion and Humanity, Humanitarian Poet Browning, Translation of Dewey's Works, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Hozumi, Shigeto** Baron (suc. in '26), D.L. Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. b. 1883 in Tokyo, eldest son of the late Baron Nobushige Hozumi, Pres. of the Privy Council; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '08, at Bonn, Paris, Cambridge, etc. '11-16 studying law of family, inheritance, etc. Works: Elements of Jurisprudence, Outline of Law of Family, Russian Revolution and Marriage, On Divorce, etc. Add. Ushigome Minamicho, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Ibaraki, Seijiro** Dir. of Urawa High School; b. in Ishikawa-ken, 1876; Edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. (English Lit.); Prof. at 4th High Sch.; educational inspector; Dir. of Tokyo Musical Academy, Matsumoto High Sch. and Tokyo Girls' Higher Normal Sch. Add. Urawa, Saitama-ken. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Ibuka, Kajinosuke** M.A. (Princeton), D.D. (Rutgars), Pres. (hon.) of the Meiji Gakuin; Chairman, National Committee, Y.M.C.A. Union of Japan; vice-chairman, World Students Fed.; b. in Aizu; studied at Dr. Brown's School in Yokohama and then at the Union Theological Seminary, New York; represented Japan in the Worlds' Y.M.C.A. and other religious conferences held abroad. Mrs. Ibuka is B. A., (Mount Holyoke, Mass.) Add. Meiji Gakuin, Shiba, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 824](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n824/mode/1up))

**Ichikawa, Sadanji** (Eijiro Takahashi's nom de guerre), prominent actor; b. in Tokyo, son of the late 4th Sadanji who was one of the greatest actors of Meiji Era; succeeded to his father's name on his death, 1906. Toured abroad '21; combines classical and "new" style of acting. Add. Kanda Kita-Koga-cho, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ichikawa, Sanki** Litt. D., Prof. of English and Philology, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1886 in Tokyo.; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Philology, class '09; further studied in England, '12-'15; given chair of English and English Literature at the alma mater in Jan. '16. The Doctorate, '23. Works; Studies in English Grammar, An English Pronouncing Dictionary for Japanese Students; Notes on Shakespear's Othello, Hamlet, etc., Sheridan's The Rivals and The School for Scandal; Stevenson's Treasure Island. Add. Imp. Tokyo Univ. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ichiki, Kitokuro** D. L., Minister of Imp. Household since '25; b. 1867 at Shizuoka; studied constitution, administrative politics, etc. in Germany '90-93; Prof. at Tokyo University '94; Mem. of House of Peers 1900; Vice-Minister of Home Affairs, '08-11; Chief of Bureau of Legislation '12-13; Minister of Education; next of Home Affairs '16-18; Privy Councillor, '17. Add. Hongo Akebono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ichiki, Otohiko** Mayor of Tokyo since Jan. '28; b. 1872 in Kagoshima; graduated Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '96; Sec. to Formosan Government, and to Finance Dept.; Chief of Accountant Bureau of the Dept.; Vice-Minister of Finance '16-18; Minister of Finance in Kato Cabinet '22; Gov. of Bank of Japan, '23-27. Add. Ushigome Shin-Ogawa-machi, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ichimura, Sanjiro** D. Litt., Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1864 in Ibaraki-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Imp. Univ., in '89; Prof. of the Peers' School and then of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Totsuka, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ichimura, Uzaemon** Professional name of Rokutaro Ichimura, actor at the Imperial, Tokyo; b. in Tokyo, 1874, son of the late Kakitsu Bando famous on the stage in Meiji Era; assumed the name of the 15th Uzaemon Ichimura at the age of 13. Add. Shiba Nishikubo-Akefune-cho, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ichinomiya, Raitaro** Vice-Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank since '22; b. in Tokyo-fu in 1870; studied at the Tokyo Senshu Gakko and entered the service of the Bank; Manager of its New York Branch before he was called back to the Head Office as Dir.; present post at the time of its reorganization in '22. Add. Yokohama. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Ide, Kenji** Admiral (retired), once Supreme Military Councillor; b. 1872 in Shizuoka-ken; grad. from the Naval Academy in '90; appointed 2nd Sub-lieutenant in '91, being gradually promoted to Rear-Adm. in '17; Chief Staff of the Kure Naval Station '14-16; Dir. of the Military Affairs Bureau, Navy Dept., and then Vice-Minister of Navy; despatched thrice to England on official mission; Adm. in '25. Add. Nakano, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Idei, Moriyuki** Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. in 1892 in Tochigi-ken; edu. at Waseda and Chicago Univ. where he studied economics '17-20; visited U.S.A. in 1926. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Igarashi, Tsutomu** Litt. D., Prof. at Waseda Univ., since '00; b. 1874 at Yonezawa; edu. at Waseda in Literature, class '95; has ever since been connected with the alma mater. Works; New History of National Literature, New Treatise on Composition, etc. Add. Waseda University. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 825](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n825/mode/1up))

**Iida, Shinshichi** dry goods merchant, head of the Iida Firm, one of the largest dry goods stores in Japan; b. 1859 in Kyoto; decorated for his service in the cause of public utility; keeps branches in Tokyo, Osaka, Yokohama, Tientsin, Lyons, London, etc. His eldest son Shintaro; b. in 1884, is grad. of Waseda, and Mng. of the Firm. Add. Karasumaru, Kyoto. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Hiroshi** Gov. of Kanagawa since '26; b. 1881 in Shizuoka; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '96; passed the Higher Civil Service Exam. the following year; secy. and chief police commissioner at Nara, Kanagawa, etc.; next transferred to the Home Office; Deputy Mayor of Tokyo for some years; Dir. of Social Bureau till '25; governor of Kyoto, '25-26. Add. Kanagawa Pref. Office. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Nagayasu** Baron, Mem. of House of Peers; b. in Okayama in 1883, of a family who was hereditarily a chief counsellor in feudal days of the daimiate of Okayama. Add. Oimachi, suburb of Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Nakahiro** Marquis (suc. 1899), Lieut. of the Army, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1867 in Tokyo; grad. from the Military Academy; made a tour in Europe and America, '08. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, near Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Seihin** Mng.-Dir. of Mitsui Bank; b. 1868 at Yonezawa; studied at Keio and Harvard; assist. Dir. of Mitsui Bank's Osaka Branch '98-00; toured through Europe and America to inspect banking business, '03. Add. Azabu Shin-Ryudo, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Torajiro** D.L., Dir. Civil Affairs Bureau, Justice Dep't.; b. 1879 in Saga-ken; grad. from the Imp. Tokyo Univ. English Law, class '03, and has since been in the service of Justice Dep't. as Judge, Pub. Procurator, or Counsellor of the Dep't. Ordered abroad twice on inspection tour; is also Lecturer at his alma mater. Add. Justice Dep't., Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikegami, Shiro** Adm. Superintendent of Chosen since Dec. '27; b. 1857 in Fukushima. He came to Tokyo and entered Metropolitan Police Board '77, and this paved the way to his promotion; Chief Police Inspector of Osaka '00-13, and Mayor of Osaka '13-24. Add. Chosen Gov.-General, Seoul. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikeno, Sei-ichiro** D. Sc., Prof. of Botany at the Coll. Agr., Imp. Tokyo Univ., since 1891; b. 1866 in Tokyo. Graduated at the Coll. of Sc., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '87 and further studied at the Univ. Hall and in Germany and Holland, '06-09, after having been assis. Prof. at the present Coll. for some years. Has discovered sparmatoboids in the fertilization of sago palms. Add. Koishikawa Sekiguchidai-machi, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikuta, Koji** (penname Choko), novelist and critic; b. 1882 in Tottori-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., in Law, class '06. Works; Enko and After, Environment, Tendency of Modern Literature and Thought, etc. Add. Takinogawa, near Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Ikuta, Sadayuki** Pres. Toyokuni Bank, Dir. Tokyo Bankers' Association; b. 1870, studied at Keio and then in the U.S.A.; was long in the service of Bank of Japan. Add. Ichigaya-Kagacho, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 826](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n826/mode/1up))

**Imai, Gosuke** Mem. House of Peers, Pres. of the Katakura Life Ins. Co. and Vice-Pres. of Katakura Filature Co.; b. 1859 in Nagano-ken; was awarded the Blue Ribbon Medal in recognition of his services to the development of commercial and industrial interests in '17; visited Europe and America for inspection in '19. Add. Yoyogi, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Imai, Keisho** Prof. at the Govt. Musical Academy, Tokyo; b. in Yokohama in 1871; lost his sight when four years old and devoted himself to Koto Music; has taught at the Academy since '94; composed number of tunes. Add. Koj. Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Imamura, Akitsune** D. Sc., noted seismologist, Professor of seismology at the Imp. Tokyo Univ., Mem. of Imperial Academy, Mem. Seismic Disaster Prevention Investigation Committee, Mem. Scientific Research Council, Assist. Chief of Geophysics Dept. of same council; b. 1870 in Tokyo; graduated Physics, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '94; studied seismology under late Dr. Kiyokage Sekiya, noted seismologist; was instructor of Military Cadet School for some time; later appointed professor of seismology in Imp. Tokyo Univ.; represented Japan at the International Seismological Society Conference in Strasburg (France) and the conference of the Geophysics Dep't. of the International Scientific Research Council Conference in Rome in '22; is the only great authority on seismology since the death of Dr. Fusakichi Omori. Works: "Seismology," "Lectures on Earthquakes," several articles and essays on earthquakes and seismology, in Japanese or English, published in the Reports of the Seismic Disaster Prevention Committee and in magazines, also several textbooks on science. Add. Higashi Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Imamura, Shigezo** B.A. (Cambridge), Pres. of the Imamura Bank, 2nd son of the late Seinosuke Imamura; b. in Tokyo in 1877, studied at Trinity Coll., Cambridge; inherited father's estate in Sept. '20 and became Director of the Bank. Add. Shiba Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Imaoka, Jun-ichiro** D. Eng., Pres. of the Uraga Dock Co.; b. 18[?]4; grad. from Eng. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. in 1898; stayed in England and Germany for study; Engineer to Communication and Railway Dep'ts; judge of the Higher Marine Court; Mng. Dir. and then Pres. of the Uraga Dockyard Co. Add. Aoyama, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Inada, Ryukichi** M.D., Prof. of Internal Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ. since '18 from which he was grad. in '00. After studying at the Post-grad. course, he studied three years in Germany, '02-05; Prof. at Kyoto Univ., '05-18, and now in charge of the Inada Ward, Univ. Hospital, Tokyo. Add. Univ. Hospital, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Hide** Lady-Prof. of the Nippon Women's Univ.; b. 1877 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Nippon Women's Univ. and went to U.S.A. for further study and grad. from Teachers' Coll., Columbia Univ. Add. Suganio, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Jukichi** ex-Secretary of the Foreign Office, but more widely known as a Japanese writer of English; b. 1862 in Tokushima; edu. at Rugby in '81 and then the course of metallurgy at Royal School of Mines, returning home '83; Prof. of English at Tokyo High School till '93; Japanese editor on the staff of the Japan Gazette; Secretary of Legation at Brussels and Washington, later serving at the head office till he resigned in '18. Works: Home Life in Tokyo; Jap.-Eng. and Eng.-Jap. dictionaries. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 827](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n827/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Junnosuke** Governor of the Bank of Japan; b. in Oita, 1869; grad. Law, Imp. Univ. Tokyo, class '95; entered the Bank of Japan the same year and was promoted to be Dir. Banking Bureau in '05; was the Bank's Agent in London '08-11; Vice-Pres. of Yokohama S.B. '11-13; President till '19; Governor of the Bank of Japan '24 and was Minister of Finance in the short-lived Yamamoto Cabinet, '24; again Gov. of the Bank of Japan '27. Add. Azabu Mikawadai, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Kakugoro** ex-Pres. of Yahagi Electric Co., etc.; b. in Hiroshima, 1859; stayed in U.S.A. for some years; advisor to the Korean Government in '94. Add. Koj. Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Katsunosuke** 2nd Marquis (suc. '15), Privy Councillor; b. in Choshu, 1860; an adopted son and heir of the late Marquis Inouye (d. '15) who was his uncle; was in the service of Bank of Japan before he got appointment at Foreign Office in '85; Legation secretary in Berlin '86; secretary and counsellor at the head office, '92; Minister to Germany 1898-07; Envoy to Chile in '10; Ambassador to St. James' Court '13-16; Grand Master of Ceremonies and Privy Councillor '28. Add. Azabu Uchidayama, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Kyoshiro** 2nd Viscount, D. Eng., Minister of Railways '26-27. Member of House of Peers; b. in Kumamoto, adopted son of late Viscount Ki Inouye; edu. in Mining at Imp. Tokyo Univ. '97; further studied abroad '98-04; Prof. Osaka Higher Tech. School and Kyoto Imp. Univ.; then transferred to Tokyo Univ.; Dir. of the South Man. Rly. Co. till '22. Add. Shimo Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Masaji** b. June '[?]76 in Tamba; grad. from Politics, Waseda Univ.; further studied at the Vienna Univ. and Berlin Univ.; a senior official of the Financial Dep't. Korean Government, '04 and held various local posts; next secretaryship of the Korean Household Dep't; went to Europe and America on official mission, to inspect the colonial condition there, '[?]0; resigned his official post and went to south sea island to start gum plantation. Add. Takata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Michiyasu** M.D., oculist. Proprietor and Head of Inouye Hospital; b. 1867 in Hyogo-ken; grad. Medical Coll. of Imp. Univ. of Tokyo '90; Prof. at the 3rd High School in Kyoto '95; est. his own Hospital '04. Is also noted as an "uta" poet and attached to the National Verse Office of Court. Add. Kojimachi Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Ryokei** Fleet Admiral, Visc. (Baron in '87 and Visc. in '07); b. 1845 in Satsuma; studied at Annapolis '77-81; rendered distinguished services in Korean affairs in '74, civil war of '78 and in Sino-Japanese war of '94-5; Admiral in '08; Fleet Adm. in '11. Add. Azabu Hiro-o, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Tatsuji** M.D., oculist; was b. 18[?]1 in Tokyo, and studied his speciality at Imperial Tokyo Univ., Leipzig and Royal Ophthalmic Hospital, London. Has published a number of [illegible]graphs on eye-diseases and maintains his own hospital. Add. Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Tetsujiro** Litt. D.; b. 1855 in Fukuoka-ken; pursued Univ. of Tokyo where he served '99-22; b. 1860 in Shidzuoka; grad. from the Science Coll. of the University '82; further studied in Germany; Member of Imperial Academy in April, "11; visited U.S.A. '26. Add. Yotsuya Obancho, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 828](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n828/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Kammei** formerly editor of the Jiji Shimpo, Mem. of Board of Council of Keio Univ.; b. 1859 in Mito; grad. from Keio Univ. where he taught at a time. Add. Azabu, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Rinshiro** Prof. of the Tokyo Higher Normal School; b. 1879 in Tochigi-ken; grad. from English Lit. of Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '03; took the post-graduate course. Add. Koishikawa Ohtsuka, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Sanshiro** socialist writer; b. 1876 in Saitama; edu. at the Chuo Univ. in Law, class '02; has taken active part in socialist movement, resulting in his flight to Europe in '13 and returned home in '20; again went abroad in '20. Works: Philosopher Carpenter, Western Socialist Movement, New Researches in Japanese Mythology, etc. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Teikichi** M.D., Dir. of Sugamo Brain Hospital (private); b. 1870 in Yamagata-ken. Edu. at Coll. Med., Imp Tokyo Univ., class '95; Vice-Pres. of the Yamagata Prefectural Hospital, '96; Pres. Shizuoka Pref. Hospital '99. Resumed study at the post-grad. course of his alma mater for about a year, and at the same time attended lectures on Psychology at Coll. of Literature; medical expert at the Metropolitan Police Office; Pres. of a Brain Asylum, '06; founded his own hospital, '13. Add. Sugamo Nobyo-in, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishimoto, Keikichi** 2nd Baron, Social reformer, eldest son of late Lieut.-Gen. Shinroku Ishimoto, who was War Minister in the 2nd Saionji Cabinet; b. in Tokyo in 1887 and grad. from Mining, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '14 and was for years connected with Mitsui Mining Co. The Baroness also takes active part in social reform work, and the two are earnest advocates of birth control principle. Add. Takata-Toyokawacho, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishiwara, Kenzo** Privy Councillor, nom. '27; b. 1864 in Okayama; grad. at Law, Imp. Univ. of Tokyo '80 and entered Justice Dep't.; opp. judge in '91 which he left the following year to be counsellor to local gov'ts. and Home Office; governor of Yamanashi, Kochi, Hokkaido, &c., lastly of Kanagawa; transferred to the Imperial Household Dep't.; Vice-Min. of Imp. Household '20-22. Add. Shimo Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishiwata, Bin-ichi** D.L., Mem. House of Peers; b. 1859 at Tokyo; studied law in Germany, '86-90; Judge of Tokyo Local Court; Counsellor of Dept. of Justice; Public Proc. of Tokyo Appeal Court; Lecturer of Law at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Dir. of Civil and Criminal Bureau; promoted to Vice-Min. of Justice in Sept., '03; then to Chief Sec. of Cabinet in '06-08. Add. Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishizaka, Masanobuu** Ph. B., Ph. D. (John Hopkins), Dir. of Aoyama Gakuin and Aoyama Jogakuin; b. 18[?]0 in Tokyo; after graduating from the Aoyama Gakuin College went to the United States and studied at the Albion Univ. and Johns Hopkins Univ.; returning home joined the teaching staff of his alma mater (Aoyama Gakuin) in '96; Dean of the collegiate course and middle school course of the school in '06; became Dir. of the Aoyama Gakuin in '21; also Dir. of the Aoyama Jogakuin (girls school) since '27. Add. Aoyama Minami-machi 7-chome, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 829](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n829/mode/1up))

**Ishizuka, Eizo** Mem. of House of Peers; b. 1865 at Aizu; appointed Legal Adviser in '95 to the Korean Govt. for a time; next held the post of Chief Counsellor to the Government-Gen. of Formosa; then Civil Administrator of the Occupied Districts in Manchuria, '05; app. Chief Counsellor of Chosen Govt. in '07; Dir. of Com., Agr. & Eng. Dept., Korea in '12; Pres. Oriental Development Co., '16-24. Add. Ush. Kikuicho, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Isobe, Sho** Lawyer and M.P.; b. in Fukui in 1875 and was adopted by Shiro Isobe; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '99. Add. Honjo Kamezawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Isobe, Yaichiro** Principal and Prop. of the Kokumin Ei-Gakukai (the National English Language Association) in Tokyo; b. 1861 in Oita-ken; studied English at Keio Gijuku for several years; organized the above English School in conjunction with a certain foreigner in '88. Add. Koj. Sambancho, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Isomura, Toyotaro** Dir. of Hokkaido Colliery and S.S. Co.; b. 1868 in Oita-ken; grad. from Keio Gijuku in '90; an official in the Dep't of Communications; journalist for a short while; entered the Bank of Japan in '94; a clerk in the Mitsui Bussan; its Section Chief and next promoted to its London Agent. Add. Takanawa Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Isono, Chozo** Prop. of the Meidiya, wholesale dealers in provisions, liquors, etc.; b. 1874 in Tottori-ken; grad from Tokyo Higher Commercial School and entered the Co., and was adopted by its founder. Add. Azabu Hiro-o, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Itakura, Takuzo** Prof. of Keio Univ., Editor of the Jiji Shimpo; b. in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from Keiogijuku in '03; visited Europe and America several times. Add. Hiratsuka Togoshi, near Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Itami, Jiro** Dir. Daido Electric Power Co., Special Mem. Kobe Chamber of Commerce; b. 1863 in Tokyo, younger brother to the late Baron Haruo Itami; early entered the N.Y.K. being later promoted to be Mng. of its Kobe Branch. Add. Shimo-Yamatedori, Kobe. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Itaya, Miyakichi** Mem. House of Peers, Pres. of a steamship company in Otaru, Hokkaido; b. 1885 in Hokkaido; 1st son of late Miyakichi; grad. from Waseda Univ. in 1909. Add. Otaru City. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Ito, Bunkichi** Baron (created 1900); Chairman, Manchuria Cotton Mill Co.; b. 18[?]5 in Tokyo, son of the late Prince Ito; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. Early entering the Dept. of Agriculture and Commerce he was promoted to its Counsellor and section chief, which he resigned in '22. Add. Shiba Mita, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Ito, Chuta** D. Eng., architect and Prof. at Imp. Univ., Tokyo since '05; b. 1864 in Yamagata-ken; grad., from Architecture at the University '92; despatched to China, India and Turkey in '02-05 to study his special subject. Add. Hongo Nishikatamachi, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Ito, Hayazo** M.D., ex-Prof., Imp. Univ., Kyoto; b. 1865 in Tottori; grad. Medicine, Imp. Univ., Kyoto, class '06, had charge of his adopted father's hospital at Tottori, then of the Sapporo Hospital '[?]0-9[?], meanwhile studying in Germany for two years; also had charge of the University Hospital, Kyoto. Add. Tottori. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 830](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n830/mode/1up))

**Ito, Hirokuni** 2nd Prince, Grand Master of Ceremonies since '26; b. 1870, 4th son of Gorosaburo Inouye of the former Choshu clan, and was adopted as son and heir by the late Prince Ito; studied in Germany; Deputy Grand Master of Ceremonies, '16; Dir. of Bureau of Imp. Mews '21-26. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Ito, Jujiro** Ph. D. (Pennsylvania), ex-Prof. of Waseda Univ., officer in Yamashita S.S. Co.; b. '78 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Waseda, class '03; graduate student Univ. of Chicago '05-06; same '06-08, (Harrison scholar, U. Penn., '06-07; Harrison jur. fellow, same U., '07-08). Works; "Carriage by Sea," and "Study of Commerce," '11, etc. Add. Zoshigaya, Tokyo-fu. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Ito, Kinsuke** Dir. Keio Univ., Nippon Flour, Chiyoda Life Ins., etc.; b. 1865 in Yamaguchi; grad. Keio Univ. and for several years was on the staff of the Jiji; at the Nippon Ginko '98-06; visited Europe and America '05 on official business; left the bank soon after his return, and restarted the defunct daily Nippon '06, which was discontinued after some years. Add. Hakusan Gaten-machi, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Ito, Miyoji** Count (cr. Baron '95, Visc. '07, Count '22), Privy Councillor; b. 1857 at Nagasaki; started his career in civil service from the lowest ladder; the late Prince Ito's Private Secretary '90-95; Chief Secy. of the Cabinet also under the Prince; Minister of Agriculture and Commerce in '98 in the Ito-Itagaki Cabinet; Privy Councillor in '99. On the occasion of the Japan-China War he was sent to Chefu to exchange the Signed Treaty of Peace. He was conspicuous for causing the fall of the Wakatsuki Cabinet in '27. Add. Nagata-cho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Ito, Sukehiro** Viscount; 12th of the line, of a daimiate family of Hyuga; b. in 1880 and succeeded to the family title in '04; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '07 and studied in Europe and America; once Mem. House of Peers. Add. Akasaka Hitotsugi, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Ito, Tokutaro** D. Sc., F.L.S. (London), botanist, son of the late Keisuke Ito, the Linnaeus of Japan; b. 18[?]8 in Nagoya; studied at Imp. Univ., Tokyo, and in England. Add. Koishi Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Ito, Yonejiro** Pres. of the Japan Times since '25; b. 1861 in Uwajima; grad. from the Osaka Senmon Gakko, and going over to America in '82 studied at the Michigan Univ. Returned home '94 and entered the N.Y.K. '96; Agent at Shanghai and London; Mng.-Dir. of the Co. '16-20; Pres. till '24. Add. Kamakura. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Iwahara, Kenzo** Dir. Onoda Cement Co., Shibaura Iron works, etc.; b. 1863 in Ishikawa-ken; finished Mitsubishi Nautical School '83; private secretary to Mr. R. W. Irwin, adviser of the Union Transportation Co., then on a similar service to Mr. Masuda, of Mitsui Bussan; had charge of the Co's. London Office '86-90; Manager of the Osaka and Kobe branches '90-95; then of the newly established branch office at New York '96; Mng.-Dir. of the Pusan '06; but left it being involved in the Naval Scandal '14. Add. Azabu Miyamura-cho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Iwasaki, Hisaya** Baron, (cr. '00) Dir. of Mitsubishi Co.; b. 1866; eldest son of the great Yataro Iwasaki, the "Sea King" of his time; edu. at Keio-gijuku, then the University of Pennsylvania, Philadelphia, U.S.A., of which he is LL.B., class '83; was installed that year as head of the great house of the Mitsubishi; has travelled in Europe and America a number of times. Add. Azabu Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 831](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n831/mode/1up))

**Iwasaki, Koyata** 2nd Baron, President of Banking Dept. of Mitsubishi Co.; b. 1879; succeeded in '09 to the title on the decease of his father Yanosuke, 1st Baron and brother of the "Sea King;" was educated at Imp. Tokyo University and Cambridge. Add. Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Iwase, Yu-ichi** M.D., Prof. at the Imp. Tokyo Univ. on Obstetrics and Gynecology; b. 1875 in Tokyo; edu. at above institution, class '00. Assistant at the alma mater on graduation; instructor at Okayama Medical College, '02-'04; Assist.-Prof. at the alma mater, '04-06. Further studied in Germany and France, '06-10; Prof. at the alma mater since his return. Add. Imp. Tokyo Univ. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Iwata, Chuzo** D.L., lawyer; b. in Yamaguchi-ken in 1875; edu. in Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '98; was long a lecturer on English law at Waseda and other universities; legal adviser to the Bank of Japan, the Nippon Yusen Kaisha, etc. Add. Aoyama Takagi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Iwatare, Kunihiko** electrician and Mng.-Dir. of the Nippon Electric Co., (joint undertaking between Japanese and foreign capitalists in which the Western-Electric Co., U.S.A., are prominent); b. 1854 in Fukuoka; grad. from the former Gov. Engineering Coll. '82, and entered the present Co. on its formation about '93. Add. Azabu Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Iwaya, Suyeo** author, novelist, the Hans Andersen of Japan; b. 1870 in Tokyo; edu. at German Association School and Literature Coll., Imperial Tokyo Univ.; further studied in Germany '98-03, filling at the same time chair of Japanese in the Oriental Language School, Berlin. Has written and translated quite a large number of stories for juvenile readers. Nom de plume "Sazanami". Add. Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Izawa, Takio** Mem. House of Peers, ex-Mayor of Tokyo ('26); b. 1869, in Nagano-ken; graduated from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Metropolitan Police Inspector '09; Governor of Wakayama '07; Ehime '08-12; Niigata, '12-14; Metropolitan Police Superintendent '14-15; Gov.-Gen. of Formosa '24-26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Izumi, Akira** D. C.L., Prof. of Meiji Univ.; b. 1873 in Hokkaido; grad. from the Sapporo Agricultural College; further prosecuted his studies at the Lake Forest, Wisconsin, and Columbia, '07-'12; was once Prof. of the Tokyo Higher Technical School. Add. Shimohoya, Toyotama-gun, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Jimbo, Kaku** English scholar and Prof. at Tokyo Higher Normal School; b. in Tokyo in 1883, being younger brother of late Dr. Kotora Jimbo, geologist (d. '24); grad. from said school in '05 and next Coll. Literature, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '08. Add. Tokyo Higher Normal School. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Kabayama, Aisuke** 2nd Count, Pres. of the Japan Steel Works at Muroran; Pres. of Taisho Bank; Dir. of Horai Life Ins., Hakodate Dockyard, Copper Mfg. Cos.; b. 1865, son of the late Adm. Count Kabayama; studied economics in England. Add. Nagatacho, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 832](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n832/mode/1up))

**Kadono, Ikunoshin** Dir. Keio Univ., Chiyoda Mutual Life Assurance Co., and Kyodo Fire Ins. Co., etc.; b. 1856. in Miye-ken; edu. at Keio, being one of its pioneer graduates and was long on its faculty; took to business about '01. Add. Aoyama, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kadono, Jukuro** ex-Vice-Pres. of Okura Firm, Ltd.; b. 1868 in Miye-ken; is brother of I. Kadono; grad. from Civil Eng., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; the London agent of the Firm '00-07; later Mng.-Dir. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kadono, Rempachiro** Aud. Mitsui Bank; b. in Miye-ken in 1868; entered the service of the bank in its early days; Mng. of its Yokohama Branch, etc. Add. Shiba Kanasugi-hamacho, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kagami, Kenkichi** Chairman of Fire Ins. Ass. of Japan; Pres. of Tokyo Marine Insurance Co. and of Mitsubishi Trust Bank; was born in Tokyo in 18[?]8; graduated from the Tokyo Higher Commercial School early eighties. London Agent of the Co., '05-12. Add. Koishikawa Otsuka, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kagawa, Toyohiko** Socialist, worker and friend of labours; b. in Kagawa-ken 1889; studied at the Meiji Gaku-in but left it unfinished; at Princeton '14-17; returned home to work for labourers; married a Christian girl, his follower and assistant; attended the Christian Convention, Dec. '24, Boston, and lectured in England, Germany, India, '24-25. Works: "Shisen-wo-koete" (English title "Before the Dawn"), his autobiographical novel '21, "Taiyo-wo-irumono" (Shooting at the Sun), '22; "Kabe-no-Koe," (Voice from the Wall), '24. Add. Fuki-ai-Shimmachi, Kobe. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kajiwara, Chuji** ex-Gov. of Hypothec Bank; b. 18[?]7 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '97; entered the Bank of Japan and was its London Agent for years; finally becoming its director; Vice.-Pres. and then Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank till '22; Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank, '22-27. Add. Shimo-Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kakehi, Katsuhiko** D. L., Prof. Law Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ. since '03; b. 1872 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; at Berlin Univ., Germany '98-03; is a Shinto enthusiast. Add. Ushigome Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kaku, Sagataro** ex-Dir. Civil Affairs Bureau, Formosa, '21-24; b. 1874 in Okinawa; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ. '99; entered Dep't of Agr. and Com.; transferred to Formosa where he was first Chief of Monopoly Bureau; attended the International Opium Convention in '25. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kamada, Eikichi** Privy Councillor since 1927, ex-Pres. of Keio Univ., etc.; b. 18[?]8 in Kii, for which he sat once in the House of Representatives, '95; studied at Keio '74-76; Principal of Kagoshima Zoshikan High School '81-83; Director of Oita Middle School; but returning once more to this alma mater '89 toured abroad to inspect educational affairs in Europe and America; Pres. of Keio on returning home; represented Japanese Govt. in the International Labor Conference, Washington, '19; min. of Education in the Kato (Adm.) Cabinet, '22. Add. Meguro, Tokyo. nojimachi, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 833](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n833/mode/1up))

**Kambe, Masao** D.L., ex-Prof. at Coll. of Economics, Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. in 1877 in Aichi-ken; grad. from Politics, Imp. Tokyo Univ., 1900; sent abroad for study twice in '04-'08 and '10-15; Prof. at Waseda and others till '02; Assis.-Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ. '07; lectured at Kiel Univ. Germany; afterwards resigned the Kyoto Univ. '22. Works: Lectures on Pol. Economy, Redemption of National Debts, History of Socialism and Social Movement, etc. Add. Okazaki-cho, Kyoto. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kamei, Shigetsune** Count, ex-Chamberlain to the Crown Prince and Master of Ceremonies; b. 1884 of a Daimyo family; studied literature at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; was on the suite of the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '21. Add. Koishikawa Maruyama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kamiyama, Mitsunoshin** Gov.-Gen. of Formosa since '26; Mem. House of Peers; b. 1870 in Yamaguchi; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo University; was successively Secretary and Judge of the Administrative Litigation Court; Dir. Forestry Bureau, Dept. Agr. & Com.; Gov. of Kumamoto '12-13; Vice-Minister of Agr. and Com. '14-18. Add. Taihoku, Formosa. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kanai, En** D.L. (Japan), Mem. of Imp. Academy & Emer. Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1865 in Shizuoka-ken; grad. at Coll. of Literature (Economics), Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Professor at his alma mater '90; dispatched to Europe '11. Add. Yanaka Tennojimachi, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kanasugi, Eigoro** M.D., Otorhinolaryngologist, Proprietor of Kanasugi Hospital. Crown Mem. House of Peers since '22; b. in Chiba-ken, 1865; edu. in Germany 1881-91; founded his own Hospital; represented Japan at the International Otonasopharyn Conference at Vienna, '08; was once an M.P. Add. Kanasugi Hospital, Kanda Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kanda, Masao** M.P.; b. 1878 in Tochigi; edu. at Waseda Semmon Gakko (now univ.), class '01; served as teacher in Szechuan, China, for a number of years; special correspondent of the Asahi in Peking, '07-15; on the editorial staff of the Asahi till '24. Add. Mejiro Bunka-mura, near Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kanda, Raizo** Banker, Pres. of the Kanda Bank, Tokyo; b. in 1872 in Nagoya; edu. at the local commercial school and then entered a stock-broker's shop in Nagoya; established himself in Tokyo soon after the Russo-Japanese war and has risen to the present position. Is also proprietor of the Momijiya Stock-broking House. Add. Kanda Bank, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kaneko, Kentaro** Viscount (Baron '95, Viscount '07), Privy Councillor, hon. LL.D. (Harvard); ex-Pres. of America's Friends Society and Japan-American Association; b. 1853 Fukuoka; grad. from Harvard, U.S.A., class '78; private Secretary to the late Prince Ito as Premier '85-86 and Pres. of the Privy Council '88-90; Chief Secretary of the House of Peers; Vice-Min. Agr. and Com. '94-96; full Minister '98; Minister of Justice '00-01. On the outbreak of the Russo-Japanese War he was sent to U.S.A. to represent Japan unofficially. Add. Kojimachi Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kaneko, Naokichi** Mem. of Kobe Chamber of Commerce; b. 18[?]6 in Kochi-ken; was partner of former Suzuki Firm, (once influential trading concern in Kobe, dissolved in '27) and director of various undertakings controlled by the Firm. Add. Kumoidori Kobe. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 834](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n834/mode/1up))

**Kanoko, Noboru** Dr. Eng., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. in Ehime in 1870 and was grad. from Mechanic Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '94; further studied his speciality in England and France; Prof. at 3rd High School, etc. Add. Imp. Kyoto Univ. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kanoko, Umaji** Litt. D., Prof. at Waseda Univ. where he was grad. in '94, and then studied Philosophy at Leipzig and Berlin '01-03; b. 1869 in Shinano. Works; "Situation of Mental Current in Europe", "Outline of Philosophy", etc. Add. Nakashibuya, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kanno, Katsunosuke** Mem. House of Peers (nom. '22); b. in 1867 in Gifu-ken; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '96; passed the higher civil service examination the following year; secretary of Agr. and Com., and next a similar post in Treasury; attached to the Embassy in London in '07; Dir. Printing Bureau; Dir. of the Finance Bureau; Vice-Min. of Finance '18-22. Add. Koj. Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kano, Jigoro** Crown Mem. of House of Peers since '22, ex-Pres. of Tokyo Higher Normal School; b. in Kumamoto-ken in '60; Director and Prof. of Peers' School '88; Director of the Special Education Bureau '98 and a patron of athletic sports having led the Japanese lads that attended the Olympics in Paris. Add. Koishikawa Kodokan, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kano, Tomonosuke** Pres. of the former Tokai Bank '25-27; b. 1872 in Ibaraki-ken; edu. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '95; entered the Sumitomo Bank '00; was long Chief of its Tokyo Branch; Mng.-Dir. at its head office in '17. Add. Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kanokogi, Takeshiro** Painter, Founder & Dir. of a private art school in Kyoto; b. 1874 in Okayama city; studied painting under Koyama and Matsubara; went to U.S.A. in '00 and then to France where he studied under Jean Paul Laurens in whose studio he succeeded in attaining the first seat of Concours. Add. Shimokamo, Kyoto. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kasama, Akio** secretary of the Legation at Bucharest since '25; b. 1885; grad. at Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ. '09; was in Govt. Rly. service till '17; removed to the For. Office and was Chief Sec. of Intelligence Bureau, '23; Emb. Counsellor at Rome and Dip. Resident at Constantinople '23. Add. Bucharest. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Katakami, Noburu** Russian scholar and ex-Prof. at Waseda; b. 1884 in Iyo; edu. at Waseda in Literature, class '06; Moscow Univ. from '16 to 19; again to Russia in '25. Works; Facts about Russia, Modern English Poems. Add. Waseda. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Kataoka, Naoharu** M.P., Adviser to the Minsei-to Party; b. 1859 in Kochi-ken; grad. at the Toyo Gakko; entered civil service as a district administrative official; officer of the Nippon Life Ins. Co. and later its Pres. which post he resigned in '18; was a delegate to the 5th International Actuaries Conference in '06 and elected its Vice-Pres.; Parl. Vice-Minister Home Office, '24-25; Minister of Commerce & Industry, '25; Finance Minister, '26-27. Add. Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Katayama, Kuniyoshi** D.M., Emeritus Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1855 in Shizuoka-ken; studied medical jurisprudence in Austria and Germany '84-8[?], and has since been Prof. at the Coll. of Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ. Add. Hongo Nishikata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 835](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n835/mode/1up))

**Katayama, Sen** M.A., B.D., pioneer socialist in Japan, now on the Executive Committee of the Third International of the Soviet Government; b. in Tsuyama in 1860; edu. at an American University; after returning home established Kingsley Hall and started an earnest labor and socialist propaganda which invited active persecution from the Government and made him flee to America about fifteen years ago. Works; Labor Movement in Japan (Chicago), Autobiography. Add. Moscow. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Katayama, Yoshikatsu** Dir. Chosen Bank, since '23; b. 1877 in Kyoto; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and passed the Higher Civil Service Exam. '05; Secretary of Agr. and Com. '05; sent abroad '14; promoted to Counsellor and chief of the War Ins. Bureau; removed to the S.M.R.C. as Dir.; resigned in '21. Add. Seoul. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kato, Kanji** Admiral, Com.-in-Chief of 1st Squadron and also of Combined Fleet since '26; b. in 1881; Lieut. '97; Captain, 11; attached to the Embassy at London; Private Secretary to Navy Minister; commanded the Japanese fleet detailed in Vladivostok during the Russian revolution '15-16; attended the Washington Conference as Chief Naval Adviser '21-22; Vice-Adm. and Vice-Chief, Naval Gen. Staff '22-23; Director of Naval Staff College '23-24; Com.-in-Chief of Yokosuka Naval Station '24-25; then appointed to the present post '26; Adm. '27. Add. Navy Dept., Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kato, Kazuo** magazine writer and novelist; b. in 1887 in Wakayama-ken; edu. at Meiji Gakuin established by the American Methodist Mission, where he finished Divinity, class '10. Was next connected with the Unitarian work but has lately adopted literary career. Add. Ashiya, Hyogo-ken. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kato, Masaharu** D.L., Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1871 in Nagano and was adopted by late Mr. Masayoshi Kato, Vice-Pres. N.Y.K., etc.; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '97, and further studied Bankruptcy in Germany and France. Add. Koj. Motozono, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kato, Takeo** Dir. Mitsubishi Bank; b. 1877 in Tochigi; grad. from Keio Univ. '01 and then entered service of the Bank; Chief of Osaka Branch before transfer to the head office. Add. Hongo Tatsuokucho, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kawada, Takashi** Dir. of Keio Electric R'ly Co., &c.; b. 1870, eldest son of Takeshi Kawada (learned scholar of Chinese classics); and studied at Michigan in '96 Add. Ushigome Wakamiya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kawaguchi, Torao** Dir. and Prof. of Hiroshima Higher Technical School; b. 1871 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. at Civil Eng., Imp. Tokyo Univ. '95; sent to U.S.A. and France for study; Civil engineer of Kumamoto-ken '96; Prof. of the 5th High School, '00 and Dir. of Kumamoto Higher Tech. School. Add. Hiroshima. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Eijiro** writer on labor and Assist. Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. from which he grad. in Economy, class '15; studied labor problems in America in '18-19; was secretary of the Dept. of Agr. and Com. but resigned in '22. Add. Oi, near Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Hiroyuki** Emb. Counsellor in Paris since '27; b. 1883 at Odawara; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., Law, class '08 and passed the Dip. and Con. Exam. the same year; Eleve-consul and next Attache in France, '09-13; transferred to Russia, '13; 3rd Leg. Sec. in Sweden, '14; 2nd Leg. Sec. '17; on the staff of the Peace Envoy, '20; Sectional Chief at the Head Office, '21-22; Emb. Counsellor in Brussels, '23-26. Add. Paris. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 836](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n836/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Misao** Gen. (ret), Privy Councillor since '27; b. 1864 in Oita; Sub.-Lt. of Infantry '86; grad. from Staff Coll., '97; further studied in Germany; took part in the Russo-Japanese war as a staff officer; Div. Com. and Com.-in-Chief of Kwantung Garrison, 1921-22; full Gen. and Mem. War Council '22; Chief of General Staff '23-26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Shintaro** Dr. Dendrology, Prof. at Agr. Coll. of the Imp. Tokyo University; b. 1865 in Aichi-ken; grad. Agr. Coll. in '92; Assist-Prof. at the College '94; sent to Germany and Austria to prosecute his study, for about three years; full Prof. at the same College '03. Add. Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Hajime** D.L. (Japan), Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ., author; b. 1879 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. class '02 and further studied in England, etc. Wrote "Principles of Economics," "Gold, Trust & Prices," etc. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Paul Kiyoshi** political writer, b. 1874 in Kochi-ken; ed. at Aoyama Gakuin Coll. and Kokumin Eigakkai (English Language School in Tokyo); was on the editorial staff of the Yorozu; '01-03; went to U.S.A. in '03 and studied at Iowa Univ. where he was granted fellowship; was connected with the East & west Newsagency (Chicago) for some years; was special correspondent for the Tokyo Nichi-Nichi '23-26. Has written a number of works on Japan's foreign relations. Add. Washington, U.S.A. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Naonosuke** Dir. Hypothec Bank of Japan; b. 1865; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; also studied at Berlin Univ; was long connected with Yokohama Specie Bank. Toured in America. Add. Azabu Higashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Toshihiko** ex-Min. to Poland '20-22; b. 1881 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Foreign Language School in '84; interpreter at Fusan, Vladivostok, San Francisco and Petrograd in '86-96; attached to the Army Commanding Headquarters in Liaotung in '04; Commercial Agent at Vladivostok in '06; Consul-Gen. at Harbin '07-14; Dir. South Manchuria Rly. '14-20. Add. Ushi. Nando-machi, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawamura, Kingoro** ex-Vice-Minister of Imp. Household; b. in 1863 in Okayama-ken; grad. with honors from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '87; secretary at local and central governments and Privy Council till about '08; Chief Sec. of Imperial Household Dep't. in '08; Vice-Minister of Imp. Household '10-15. Add. Aoyama, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawamura, Takeji** Crown Mem. House of Peers; b. 1871 in Akita-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '97; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; entered the service of Dept. of Communications; transferred to Home Office, then to Formosan Govt.; attended the 6th International Post Conference, Rome; Dir. of Police Affairs Bureau, '19-21; Pres. of Colonization Bureau '21; Vice-Min. of Home Affairs and next Pres. of South Manchuria Rly '22-24. Add. Takata, near Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 837](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n837/mode/1up))

**Kawanishi, Seibei** Pres. of Japan Woolen Cloth, Hyogo Elec. Tramway, San-yo Leather Cos., Dir. of Un-yu, Kobe Elec. Light, Nippon Shogyo Bank, etc.; b. 1865 in Osaka. Add. Kawasaki, Kobe. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawasaki, Hachiro-emon** Pres. of Kawasaki Bank and other concerns; b. 1866 in Tokyo; grad. Tokyo Higher Commercial School and further studied practical side of banking in America. Assumed his father's name Hachiroemon in '13. Add. Azabu Torii-zaka, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawasaki, Hajime** President of the Nippon Fire Ins. Co., Nikka Life Ins. Co., Vice-Pres. of Kawasaki Bank; b. 1884 in Tokyo; edu. at Tokyo Higher Commercial School in '03, and next at Penn. Univ. for Economics, class '09. Add. Akasaka Daimachi, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawashima, Chunosuke** Director of Yokohama Specie Bank; b. 1855 in Tokyo; studied French and naval architecture at the Gov. Dockyard, Yokohama; was engaged as interp. and clerk '71 by a Dutch firm, Yokohama; agent at Lyons of the Bank '79-93; transferred to the Head Office '94; Agent at Bombay till '95; Agent in Tokyo '02-06; then recalled to the Head Office. Add. Hongo Fukuyama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawashima, Nobutaro** Minister to Greece since '27; b. 1880 in Kyoto-fu; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com.
