School and postgraduate course, class '07, and after passing the Dip. and Con. service exam. has been in the service of the Foreign Office; later appointed 3rd Emb. Secretary and then 2nd Legation Secretary; Con.-Gen. at Hamburg, '25-27. Add. Athens. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawashima, Reijiro** Vice-Adm. (ret.), app. Court Councillor in '22; b. 1864 in Ishikawa-ken; Sub-Lieut. '88; Captain in '01 and Rear-Adm. in '07; sent to China as Com. of a detachment during the Chinese revolution '11, and then Dir. of Hydrographic Dept. in '12; Com. of Port Arthur Naval Station; Mem. of Admirals' Council, '15-17. Intendant to Prince Higashi-Fushimi '17-22. Add. Shiba Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawata, Keizo** Director of the Bank of Japan; b. 1871 in Tokyo, brother of Dr. Okano, Member of the Kato and Kiyoura Cabinets, and adopted by the family; edu. at the Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '95; has since been connected with the Bank. Add. Aoyama Minamicho, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawata, Shiro** D.L. and Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ. from which he was graduated in Law, class '07; connected with the Kokumin for a year or two; then assist. Prof. at his alma mater and finally full Prof.; sent abroad for study to Germany, England, etc. '11-16, specializing in Economy. Works: Development of Family System, Spirit of Capitalism, Women Problem, Brazil and Colonization, etc. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kawazu, Susumu** D.C.L., Dean of Coll. of Economics at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. in Tokyo 1875, son of the late S. Kawazu, former Minister of Communications; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., and further studied in Germany and England '01-14. Works: "Principles of Political Economy," etc. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Kayetsu, Takako** lady educationist, Superintendent of Nippon Girls' Commercial School; b. 18[?]9 in Kumamoto-ken; studied at a private girls' school and has ever since been engaged in education, first in her native place; established present school '03 in Tokyo. Add. Dote Sambancho, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 838](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n838/mode/1up))

**Keto, Katsumoto** D.L., Lawyer in Osaka; b. 1874 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '98, appointed Assist. Prof. of Kyoto Univ. '00; Dean '14; opened practice '16. Add. Mikage, near Hyogo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kiga, Kanju** D.L., Ph. D. (Leipzig), Dean of Economics Dep't, Keio Univ.; b. '73 in Shizuoka-ken; edu. at Keio Univ., '95; Prof. of Economics at his alma mater since '95; further studied in Germany, '98-20. Add. Shimoshibuya, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kikkoji, Shaku** M.D., in charge of Gastro-Enteric Dep't, Kyo-undo Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1878 in Tochigi-ken; edu. in Medicine, Imp. Kyoto Univ., class, '06; further at its post-graduate course and finally at Berlin Univ. '08-12. Assist. at the alma mater, '06-08, and has been in the service of the Hospital since his return home. Add. Kyo-undo Hospital, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kikuchi, Kenjiro** M.P. from Mito City; b. 1867 at Mito City; grad. from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '93; Prof. of the Yamaguchi High School; went to China and became Superintendent of the Tung Wen College in Shanghai; then General Superintendent of the Normal College in Nanking; after returning from China was Principal of the Mito Middle School for some years. Add. Mito City. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kikuchi, Kyozo** D. Eng., Pres. of the Dai Nippon Spinning Co., Pres. of 130th Bank (Osaka), Dir. of the Nagoya Cotton Mill, etc.; Crown Mem. House of Peers; b. 1856 in Ehime-ken; grad. from the defunct Gov. Engineering Coll., class '85; studied practical side of cotton spinning in England; since returning home he had been connected with a number of mills; was Pres. of the Amagasaki Mill which with the Settsu Mill was reorganized as the present mill in '18. Add. Amagasaki, near Osaka. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kikuchi, Yoshiro** ex-Minister to Czecho-Slovakia; b. in 1877 in Osaka; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '03. Passing the Dip. and Con. Serv. Exam. has since been in diplomatic service; Attache in Germany and Austria; 3rd Emb. Sec. in Russia, '08; Consul at Amoy, '10; Con.-Gen. at Vladivostok '17; in Charge of Political Affair of Siberian Expedition, '20; Emb. Counsellor in Italy, '22-24; Minister to Prague, '24-27. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kikumoto, Naojiro** Dir. Mitsui Bank; b. in Miye-ken, 1870; edu. at Keio and entered the Bank, '98. Add. Aoyama Minamicho, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Kusuyata** Dir.-Gen. Mitsubishi Partnership and of Mitsubishi Shipbuilding Co.; b. in 1865 in Kochi; edu. at Tokyo Higher Commercial School in '82, and on grad. entered the Mitsubishi; Chief of its Kobe Branch in '00; Chief of General Affairs in '15 and later Director at head office. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Sakaye** D. Sc., M.R.A.S. (Eng.), Dir. of the Gov. Geodetic Observatory at Mizusawa, since '00; b. 1863 in Kanagawa; grad. Astronomy, Imp. Tokyo, Univ., '92; attended the World's Geodetic Conference in '98 and '06; discovered error in the formula relating to the axis, and the "Kimura term" has been acknowledged by the world's astronomers; received first prize from the Imperial Academy, Japan, in '11. Add. Mizusawa, Iwate-ken. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 839](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n839/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Seishiro** ex-Deputy Gov. of Bank of Japan; b. 1861 in Okayama-ken; edu. at Keio Gijuku from which he grad. in '82; was editor of the "Chugai Shogyo" for many years; entered in '03 the Bank of Japan where he held Vice-Governorship '19-26. Add. Shiba Minami Sakuma-cho, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Shigeharu** M.A. (Harvard), Dir. of Nagasaki Higher Commercial School; b. 1874 in Osaka-fu; grad. from Harvard Univ., where he studied mediaeval history of Europe under Prof. Emerton; was for some time Prof. of St. Paul's Univ. (Rikkyo Daigaku); next Prof. of Yamaguchi Higher Commercial School, and then that of Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; the present post in '23. Add. Nagasaki City. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Tokuya** M.D., Pres. of the Izumibashi Charity Hospital donated by the Mitsui Family, Lecturer at the Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1871 in Niigata-ken. Edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '99, and further at Berlin, Munchen, etc. '10-04, specializing in Lung Diseases. Add. Izumibashi Hospital, Kanda, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Yuji** Pres. of Tokyo Life Ins. Co., since '24; b. 1873 in Miye-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '99; Sub-Manager of the 1st Bank's Seoul Branch; Dir. of Chosen Bank '09-24. Add. Sugamo, near Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kinoshita, Seichu** M.D., Pres. of Kinoshita Obstetric and Gynecological Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1869 in Wakasa; edu. in Medicine, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '94; further studied his specialty in Germany '97-98; Prof. at Osaka Prefectural School of Medicine, '99; assist.-Prof. and then full Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., '99-17. Add. Kinoshita Hospital, Kojimachi, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kiribuchi, Kyoji** D.M., Dir. of the Kiribuchi Optical Hospital; b. 1870 in Tokyo; grad. from the Medical College, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '95; further studied in Germany for three years; is one of the noted ophthalmologists. Add Shitaya-ku, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kirishima, Soichi** Dir. of Real Estate Dept., Mitsubishi Firm; b. 1864 in Kochi; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '90. Toured abroad in '13. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kishi, Keijiro** Dr. Eng., Dir. of the Shibaura Works; b. 1868 in Wakayama-ken; grad. at Eng., Imp. Tokyo Univ. '95. Add. Kirigaya, Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kishi, Seiichi** D.L., barrister, Dir. of the Osaka Gas Co., legal adviser to several Gov. and private institutions; b. 1867 at Matsuye; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '87 and began practice in Tokyo; a patron of physical training and Pres. of Japan Physical Culture Association. Add. Shiba Isarago, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kishigami, Kenkichi** zoologist, D.Sc., Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ. and expert to the Fishery Bureau, Dept. of Agr. and Com.; b. 1867 in Aichi-ken; grad. at Science, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '94; appointed technical expert of the said Dep't '95; sent to Europe and America in '97. Add. Kojimachi Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kita, Reikichi** Prof. at Daito Bunka Institute and Lecturer of Meiji Univ.; b. 1885 in Niigata-ken; edu. at Waseda in Philosophy, class '08 and further studied in America and Germany for 4½ years, returning home in '22. Works; Digest of Bergson's Philosophy. From Philosophy to Politics, etc. Add. Kami Totsuka, near Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 840](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n840/mode/1up))

**Kitagawa, Reisuke** Mng.-Dir. of Chiyoda Mutual Life Ins. Co., Dir. of the Tamagawa Electric Rly. Co.; b. 1861 in Fukui-ken; grad. from Keio Univ. in '81; was once editor of the Jiji. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kitajima, Taichi** M.D., Bacteriologist and Prof. of the Keio Univ., Mem. of the Kitazato Institute; b. 1862 in Kanazawa, graduated from Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ. '94; entered the Infectious Disease Institute, Home Dep't., till '97; further studied his speciality in Germany, '97-01; again appointed expert to the Home Dep't., '01-14, meanwhile sent to Philippines and Italy for investigation; appointed to the faculty of the Keio in '14 where he occupies chair of Bacteriology. Add. Nishi-okubo, near Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kitamura, Shigemasa** Prop. of the Seiyoken Hotel (at Ueno Park and Tsukiji which latter was burned in the disaster of '23; b. 1872; grad. from the Meiji Gakuin in '93 and further studied at a private law school. Add. Ueno, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kitazato, Shibasaburo** Baron (created '15), M.D., President of Kitazato Pathological Laboratory, Dean of Med. Coll. Keio Univ.; b. 1859 at Kumamoto; studied in Germany under Prof. Koch '85-91; discovered the germs of tetanus, diphtheria, pest (in conjunction with Dr. Aoyama), etc.; had charge of the Gov't Blood-Serum Laboratory; was granted the Red Eagle by the Kaiser '09; retired from Gov't service to open his own laboratory in '14. Add. Azabu Nakanocho, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kitazawa, Shinjiro** Ph. D., Johns Hopkins, where he stayed for four years besides three in South Univ., North Carolina, specializing in social problems and economy; graduated from Waseda Univ. in Commerce, class '10; b. 1887 in Tokyo. Works: Latest Labor Movement, Problems for Laborers, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kiyose, Ichiro** D.L., M.P. for Osaka; b. in 1884 in Hyogo-ken; edu. at Imp. Kyoto Univ. in Law, class '08 and further studied in England and elsewhere. Add. Dojima Hamadori, Osaka. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kiyoura, Keigo** Visc. (1912), ex-Premier; b. 1850 at Kumamoto; first was a school master; then a humble clerk in Saitama prefectural office; Public Procurator, '73; next Sec. of Home Office, Police Bureau, Vice-Minister of Justice in quick succession; Minister of Justice in '98-00 and '01-03; Minister of Agr. and Com. '03-06; Privy Councillor '06; Vice-Pres. of Privy Council '17-22 under Marshal Prince Yamagata; Pres. of Privy Council '22-24; Premier Jan.-July, '24. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Koba, Sadanaga** D.L., Crown Mem. House of Peers; b. in 1859; studied in Germany and Austria '82-85; Private Secretary to the Minister of Education, &c., till '95; next Director of the Ordinary Edu. Bureau, Chief Counsellor and Vice-Min. of Education '03-05. Add. Nakashibuya, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kobashi, Ichita** M.P.; b. 1870 in Kumamoto; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '98; Counsellor of Yamaguchi-ken, then of Nagasaki-ken; entered Home Dep't as Secy., then became Counsellor; sent abroad; Vice-Min. of Home Affairs '18-22; Chief Sec. to Kiyoura Cabinet '24. Add. Kami-Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 841](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n841/mode/1up))

**Kobayakawa, Shiro** Baron, ex-Vice-Grand Chamberlain to the Emperor ('22-27); studied in Germany; has long been in the service of the Imp. Household and was in the Bureau of Peerage and Heraldry. Add. 59 Shirokane-Sarumachi, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Kyuhei** D. Sc., Prof. at Waseda Univ. since '18; b. 1875 in Niigata-ken; edu. in Chemical Tech., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '00; Lecturer at alma mater and the Tokyo Higher Commercial School '02-05; chemist to the Asano and next Hoden Petroleum Co., '07-12; supervising chemist to the Japan Acetic Co., '12-13. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Masanao** Dir. Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; b. 1873 in Kyoto; studied at the Doshi-sha; entered the Mitsui Bussan Co. in '93 and was for some time Manager of its New York branch. Add. Nakashibuya, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Sakutaro** Mng.-Dir. of Shibaura Iron Works; b. 1869 in Nagasaki-ken; edu. at a Technical Apprentice School; entered the Works after finishing the course and was twice sent abroad to inspect technical industries; succeeded in improving or inventing mechanical devices and appliances and was awarded the Blue Ribbon Medal. Add. Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Takejiro** Dr. Juris., Dir. Imp. Hotel and Osaka Hotel, etc.; b. in Miyeken 1873; edu. at Peers' School and Halle and Rostock, Germany, returning home 1903. Add. Shimoshibuya, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Ushisaburo** D.L., an economist and financier, Dir. of the Japan Steel Foundry, etc.; b. 1865 in Gumma; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '94; Secretary at the Legislative Bureau '96-07; Chief of the Financial Bureau, Formosa '07-10; M.P. '12-15; Financial attache to Japanese Legation, Peking '17. Works; a number of works on finance and economy. Add. Hongo, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Koda, Nariyuki** Litt. D., a master novelist (nom de plume "Rohan"); b. 1867 a son of an artist family. His younger brother, Narimoto, is a grad. of the Litt. Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; two sisters Miss Nobu, and Mrs. Ando (her younger sister) were b. at Tokyo, and both showed a great talent for music from childhood; sent abroad by the Gov. to study music, '87-95; then both were app. Prof. of the Tokyo Music Academy, but have resigned since. Works: "Gojunoto," "Futsuka Monogatari," etc. besides some historical works. Add. Koishikawa Omote-machi, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kodama, Hideo** 2nd Count, Mem. House of Peers (since '18), Dir. of Seijo School; b. 1876 in Tokyo; eldest son of the late General Kodama, Chief of Gen. Staff at the time of the Japan Russia War; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '00 and entered the service of Korean Govt.-Gen., in which he rose to Chief of Gen. Affairs '12; transferred to Home Govt. as Chief Secretary of Cabinet '16; Dir. Bureau of Decoration '18-21; Gov.-Gen. of Kwantung, '24-27. Add. Ushigome Yakuoji, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kodama, Kenji** Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank since '22; b. 1871 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '93; was Chief of one branch after another; Dir. of the head office and Manager of its Shanghai Branch '14-22. Add. Koj. Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kodera, Kenkichi** M.P.; b. 1877 in Kobe; edu. at Kobe Com. School, Columbia, Johns Hopkins, and further in Germany, etc.; went through one-year volunteer service and served in the 1904-05 war. Has founded and maintains at his expense a middle school at Sanda, Hyogo-ken. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 842](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n842/mode/1up))

**Kodera, Sakichi** Importer and Exporter, Deputy of Yingkow Kodera Engine Oil Co., Chairman of the Yingkow Business Association; b. 1881 at Kobe city; studied at the 1st High School; went to Yingkow, South Manchuria, 1906 and established the Kodera & Co. at Newchwang; has branch offices in Dairen, Kobe, Tokyo, Vladivostock, Mukden, Harbin, Kwanchengtz, Kaiyuan, etc. Add. Kobe. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Koganei, Ryosei** M.D. and Emeritus Prof., Imp. Tokyo Univ., his speciality being brain diseases; b. 1858 at Nagaoka; edu. at Medicine, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '70; studied at Berlin '81-85 connected with the University till he resigned in '22. Add. Hongo Akebonocho, Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Koide, Fusakichi** Dr. Dendrology, Prof., Imp. Hokkaido Univ. since '07; b. 1869 in Gifu-ken; after grad. from the Imp. Tokyo Univ. was sent to Germany for furthering his studies in forestry 1900-03. App. Prof., Morioka Agricultural Coll. '08. Add. Sapporo, Hokkaido. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Koizumi, Matajiro** M.P.; b. 1865 in Kanagawa-ken; took to journalism as Editor of the Yokohama Boeki Shimbun; elected mem. of the Kanagawa Pref. Assembly; sits in the Lower House since 1912 as a Kenseikwai M.P. Add. Yokosuka City. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Koizumi, Sakutaro** M.P., Vice-President of the House of Representatives '24-27; b. 1872 in Shizuoka-ken; has been connected with various papers in the country and also sat in Tokyo Municipal Council, etc. Add. Yotsuya Hommura, Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Koizumi, Shinzo** Prof. at Keio Univ. which he finished in '01 in Politics; further studied in England and elsewhere '12-16; b. 1888 in Tokyo. Works; Studies on Social Problems, Economic Science and Social Ideas, Value and Socialism, etc. Add. Keio Univ. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Koizumi, Tetsu** writer on art; b. 1886 in Tokushima; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. which he left unfinished; once edited a literary magazine Shirakaba and was also on the teaching staff of Keio Univ. Add. Shibuya, near Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Kojima, Kazuo** ex-M.P.; b. 1865 in Hyogo-ken; formerly a journalist and chief editor of the Yorozu Choho; was returned to the Lower House six times from Tokyo City. Add. Kami-Shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Komaki, Omi** (real name Omi, Tanigoma), literary writer and attended conference at Geneva in '23 as adviser to the Government delegates; b. 1894 at Tsuchizaki; edu. at the Morning Star School (French, in Tokyo) and at Paris Univ.; published a literary magazine while he was connected with the Information Bureau, Foreign Office. Add. Akasaka Hinoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Komatsu, Kenjiro** Crown Mem. of the House of Peers; b. 1863 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '88; entered the Department of Communications for which he was Vice-Min. in '11, but resigned in '12; Min. of Rlys in the Kiyoura Cabinet. Add. Shimo-Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 843](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n843/mode/1up))

**Komatsu, Midori** Journalist connected with "Chugai Shogyo"; b. 1865 in Tokyo; grad. from Michigan Univ., '90; Translator and Secretary of the Foreign Office till '07; Dir. Foreign Affairs Bureau of Korean Government '11-17. Add. Shiba park, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Komatsu, Teruhisa** Lieut. (Navy); 1st Marquis, the House being newly created in '10 to keep up the House of the late Prince Komatsu who died without an heir; b. 1868, fourth son of the late Prince Yoshihisa Kitashirakawa and younger brother of the late Prince Narihisa who died in Paris '23; grad. at Naval Academy; married Prince Shimazu's sister, '09; visited Europe '25-26. Add. Asakusa-ku, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Komura, Kin-ichi** Marquis (2nd). Director of Intelligence Bureau, Foreign Office, since '27; b. 1882, eldest son of the late Marquis Jutaro Komura; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '07; passed the Diplomatic Service Exam. the same year; attache in China and next at London '08-10; Secy at head Office '11 and in time appointed Sectional Chief of Political Bureau; Deputy-Dir. of Intelligence Bureau, '23-27. Add. Koj., Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Kondo, Motoki** D. Eng., Vice-Admiral (ret.), ex-Inspector General of Naval Construction; b. 1864 in Tokyo; is Director of the Kogyokusha, private school founded by his father who did much in the cause of naval education. Received Hon. Prize from Imperial Academy of Japan '13. Add. Hiratsuka-mura, Tokyo-Fu. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Kondo, Rihei** Pres. R. Kondo & Co., dealers in Bee Brand Kozan Wine and others, Dir. Kokka Life Ins.; b. 1886 and was adopted into the family; edu. at Keio and then Columbia Univ., New York. Add. Nihombashi Honcho, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Konishi, Masutaro** Russian scholar; b. 1862 in Okayama-ken; studied Russian under Nicolai, a pioneer Russian missionary, and further studied Philosophy at Kief and Moscow; once received religious instruction from Tolstoi. Is now connected with Kyoto Imp. Univ. and Doshi-sha, and has written "Russia" etc., besides translating into Russian several Chinese Classics. Add. Doshi-sha, Kyoto. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Konishi, Shimpachi** Dir. of Tokyo Deaf and Dumb Sch.; b. 1854 in Niigata-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Normal Sch., '79; instructor at Chiba Mid. Sch., Tokyo Women's Higher Normal Sch., before appointment to present post in '93. Add. Koishikawa Sasugaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Konoike, Zen-emon** Baron (cr. '11), is of one of the oldest millionaire families in Japan, dating 3 centuries back; runs the Konoike Bank, and is at the same time the largest shareholder of the Nippon Life Insurance Co., and the Osaka Savings Bank. Add. Konoike, Osaka. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Konoye, Fumimaro** Prince, one of the proud Fujiwara houses and a "gossekke" (see Ichijo) and was founded by Motozane (1143-66 A.D.). The present head grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., 1917, and is Member of House of Peers. Add. Shibuya, near Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 844](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n844/mode/1up))

**Kosaki, Kodo** famous pioneer Christian and pastor of Reinanzaka Church, Tokyo, Chairman of the Kumi-ai Churches of Japan, of Sunday School Society of Japan, and of Christian Church Union of Japan; b. 1856 in Kumamoto-ken; studied at Kumamoto English School and Doshisha, Kyoto; Pres. of Doshisha Univ. in '90-97; three times represented Japan at International Congress of Religions abroad. Add. Reinanzaka, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Koyama, Junji** Pres. of Kaigai Kogyo (Emigration) Co.; b. 1870 at Kumamoto; graduated at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '95; was clerk at the Home Office and next Secretary in the provinces and also in Tokyo, and was once Sec. to the Minister of Justice and of Agriculture and Commerce; Dir. of Fishery Bureau '06; Governor of Gumma '08-12. Add. Azabu Ryudocho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kozai, Yoshinao** Dr. Agr., Ph. D. (Leipzig), Pres. of Tokyo Imp. Univ. since '20; b. 1865 in Kyoto-fu; grad. from Agr. Coll. '85; Prof. at the alma mater '89-95; studied at Leipzig '95-99; Agriculturist to the Gov. Experimental Farm at Nishigahara, Tokyo '03 and then its chief '06; Dean of Coll. of Agriculture in '11-20. Add. Imp. Univ., Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kubota, Seitaro** Pres. Adm. Litigation Court since '22; b. 1864 in Okayama; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '91; Sec. at local and the Home Office till '94; sent abroad '98-99 and was transferred to be Secretary of Agr. and Com. to draw up draft of factory law; Dir. Sanitary Affairs Bureau '03-10 and then Judge of Adm. Lit. Court, etc. Add. Akasaka Reinanzaka, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kubota, Shiro** Chairman of Tokyo Rice Exchange, b. 1872 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Tokyo Higher Com. Sch. in '96; entered Mitsui Firm the same year and remained in the service for ten years; was for years Pres. of a hydro-electric co. Add. Akasaka Tangocho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kubota, Yuzuru** Baron (cr. '07), Privy Councillor since '17; b. 1847 in Hyogo-ken; entered the civil service in connection with education in which he became Vice-Minister '94 and finally its chief '05; Member of House of Peers, '94-17. Add. Koishikawa Kanatomicho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kuhara, Fusanosuke** Pres. of the Kuhara Mining Co. at Osaka, the lessee of the Hitachi Copper Mine, etc.; b. 1869 at Osaka; edu. at Keio-gijuku '89; a clerk at the Kosaka Copper Mine which was resuscitated by him from the threatened danger of decay; supervisor of the mine, but resigned the post '98. Bought Hitachi Mine '05, and has made it one of the best copper mines in Japan; sent to Germany and Russia on official mission Oct. '27. Add. Nakanoshima, Osaka. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kujirai, Tsunetaro** D.E., Prof. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1884 in Saitama-ken; grad. from Electrical Engineering Course of Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '07; formerly a technical expert of communications; Assist.-Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; stayed in Germany, England and America for study. Add. Hongo-ku, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kujo, Michizane** Prince, one of the proud Fujiwara houses and formerly a "gossekke" (see Ichijo) founded by Kanezane (1184-1207). The present head was b. 1876 and is Chief Ritualist on duty at Court, and is a brother of the Empress. Add. Akasaka Fukuyoshicho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 845](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n845/mode/1up))

**Kuki, Takakazu** Baron (cr. '05), Privy Councillor; b. 1852 in Settsu; was for several years a high official in the Dep't of Education; Minister at Washington '84-88; Director of the Imperial Nara Museum in '90; Exhibition Commissioner, etc. Is a great virtuoso of Japanese painting. Add. Nagatacho, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kumamoto, Kenjiro** English Scholar and educationist, Prof. at Waseda; b. in '67 in Osaka; studied Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '87-89; English teacher at Himeji and next at Osaka Mid. Sch.; Prof. at 3rd Higher School (Kyoto), '95-98; at Tokyo Higher Normal '94-98; Prof. at the Peers' School '98-21; toured abroad in '19. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kume, Kunitake** Litt. D., historian, ex-Prof. Waseda Univ.; b. 1844 in Saga; was on suite of the late Prince Iwakura's mission to America and Europe in '72; in the Compilation Bureau, Cabinet, and afterward Prof. of the 1st High School, but his article about the origin of the Japanese race obliged him to resign. Add. Meguro, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kunisawa Shimbei** D. Eng., ex-Chairman of Dirs. of the South Manchuria R'ly Co. '13-19; b. 1864 in Kochi-ken; grad. in '89 from Eng. Coll. Imp. Tokyo Univ.; engineer of the nationalized Kyushu-R'ly Co., '89; Eng. to the Railway Department '92-05 with the field railroads in Manchuria before the South M. R'ly Co. was created; Vice-Pres. of the same since '06; Pres. '18-22. Add. Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kurachi, Seifu** Mng.-Dir. of Mitsukoshi Dry-Goods Store Co.; b. 1867 at Kanazawa; edu. at now defunct English Coll., Osaka, and Keio, and further in America 1890-94; entered the Meiji Fire Ins. Co. as Manager of its Kyoto Branch '95; next with Murai Shokai, tobacco dealers in Kyoto; an agent of the British & American Tobacco Co. in Korea; Manager of Kyoto Fire Ins. Co. '09 and '11. Add. Shiba Mita-Tsunamachi, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kurachi, Tetsukichi** Mem. House of Peers, Vice-Pres. of Japan-China Industrial Co.; b. 1871 in Kanazawa; edu. Law. Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '94; passed higher civil service examination in '96; Counsellor For. Office, '01; attached to the Japanese Envoy to the Peace Conference at the Hague in '07; Vice-Min. of Foreign Office, '11-12. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kuratomi, Yuzaburo** Baron (created 1926), D.C.L., President of the Privy Council since '26; b. 1853 in Fukuoka-ken; studied at the now defunct Law Coll., Justice Dept., '89; connected with the Dept. till he was removed to Korea as Vice-Min. of Justice; Chief of Compilation Com. of the Japanese Criminal Code; Dir. of the Bureau of Legislation '13-14; nominated Mem. House of Peers; Chief Auditor, Imp. Household Dep't and Privy Councillor '21. Add. Akasaka Tango-cho, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kure, Ken** M.D., Prof. at Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ., from which he was graduated in '07; further studied at Prague Univ., '11-13. Served as assistant at his alma mater '08-10; Lecturer at same in '14 and also Chief Physician of the Mitsui Charity Hospital; Prof. at Coll. Med., Imp. Kyushu Univ., '21; transferred to the present post, '24. Add. Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kure, Shuzo** M.D., Emer. Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1865 in Tokyo; grad. Medicine at Imp. Tokyo Univ., '90; studied in Ger- many, '97-00; is a specialist on insanity. Add. Hongo Yushima, Shinhanacho, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 846](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n846/mode/1up))

**Kurino, Shin-ichiro** Viscount, ex-Amb. to France; b. at Fukuoka 1852; Commissioner of the Foreign Office '81; Junior Secretary '84; Vice-Chief of the Investigation Bureau '8[?]; next transferred to the Communication Dep't where he acted as Personal Secretary to the Minister, later becoming Director of the Foreign Correspondence Bureau in the Dep't; returned to the Foreign Office in '91 as Director of the Investigation Bureau and then that of the Political Bureau; commenced his diplomatic career, first as Minister at Washington '94, at Rome '96, at Paris '97, and at Petrograd '01, in the stormy days preceding the outbreak of the Russo-Japanese War; appointed Ambassador to France '06. Add. Akasaka Hinoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kuroda, Hideo** ex-Dir. Banking Bureau, Finance Dept.; b. 1879; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., and passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Sec. to Finance Min., etc. Add. Hongo Oiwake-cho, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kuroda, Nagashige** Marquis, formerly Daimyo of Fukuoka, Privy Councillor, Lord-in-Waiting to Jako Hall; b. 1867 at Fukuoka; studied at Cambridge Univ. '84-89; Master of Ceremonies '89-90; Vice-Pres. of the House of Peers, '96-24; is President of the Oriental Association. Add. Akasaka Fukuyoshicho, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kuroita, Katsumi** D. Litt., Prof. of Literature Department of the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. from the Lit. Coll. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. '96; further studied at the Univ. Hall; went to Europe for study, '08; after his return home appointed to the present post. Add. Naka-Shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kurokawa, Shinjiro** Pres. of the Kokusai S.S. Co. (Kobe), ex-Vice-Pres. Nippon Yusen Kaisha; b. 1875; grad. from Aoyama Gaku-in College (a Christian Coll. in Tokyo) '94; entered the service of Nippon Yusen Kaisha '94; Assist. Manager of its Shanghai branch, '11; Manager of Foreign Service Dept., Head Office, '16; Manager of Kobe branch, '20; promoted to Director and Vice-President, '23; resigned the post, '24; was Delegate (on shipping affairs) to the Peace Conference at Paris, '19. Add. Azabu Zaimoku-cho, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kurosawa, Tsuguhisa** Mng.-Dir. of Nippon Fire Ins. Co., since '21; b. 1880 at Suwa; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '06; passed Civil Service Examination the same year; counsellor & Per. Secretary to the Minister of Education '07-10; Dir. of the Historical Bureau '17-21. Add. Yotsuya Higashi-Shinanomachi, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kusaka, Chojiro** Ph. D. (Jena); Dir. of the Sumitomo Goshi Kaisha; Standing Dir. of the Sumitomo Warehouse Co.; Auditor of the Sumitomo Bank, etc.: grad. from Tokyo School of Foreign Languages in '84; went to Germany in '87 and studied at Univ. of Jena, where he obtained the degree of Doktor der Philosophie; entered the Sumitomo Firm in '03, becoming a director of the firm in '18. Add. Osaka-fu. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kushida, Manzo** Mng.-Dir. of the Mitsubishi Bank; b. 1867 in Tokyo; studied in U.S.A. '85-94 and graduated at Pennsylvania Univ.; entered the Mitsubishi on returning home; present post '14. Add. Shiba Nishinokubo, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 847](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n847/mode/1up))

**Kushida, Tamizo** social writer and member of the Ohara Social Institute at Osaka which sent him to Germany and England to study his speciality '20-23; was once connected with the Osaka Asahi; edu. at elective course, Imp. Tokyo Univ. Law. Add. Ohara Institute, Osaka. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Kuwada, Kumezo** D.L., Prof. of Imp. Tokyo University; b. 1868 in Tottori-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '83; further studied in Europe '96-98; organized the Social Association on his return home; Parl.-Secy. of Education July to Aug., '15; Mem. of the House of Peers as highest-tax-payer for Tottori-ken '01-17; represented Japan at Geneva Red Cross Conference in '21. Works: "On Industrial Economy," "The General Trend of Social Problems in Europe," and "Factory Law and Labor Insurance", etc. Add. Sendagi Hayashicho, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Kuwahara, Hitsuzo** D. Litt., Prof. of the Lit. Coll. of the Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Fukui-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '96; studied in the University Hall: appointed Prof. of the 3rd High School; Prof. of the Tokyo Higher Normal School; then the present post. Add. Kyoto-fu. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Kuwaki, Gen-yoku** Litt. D., Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. which he finished in '06 in psychology; further studied at the Univ. Hall and later in Europe and America. Works; Outline of Philosophy, Ten Lectures on Current Thoughts, Kant, Descartes, Five Great Philosophers, etc. Add. Ushigome Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Kuwayama, Tetsuo** Perm. Vice-Minister of Communications since '24; b. in 1881; studied German Law at Imp. Tokyo Univ. and passed Higher Civil Service Examination; Dir. Petty Insurance Bureau, '22-24. Add. Omosawa, Araijuku, near Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Machida, Chuji** M.P., Minister of Agr. and Forestry in '26-27, Pres. of Hochi Shimbun, Dir. of 110th Bank and Kyoto Fire Ins. Co., etc.; b. 1863 in Akita-ken; grad. Imp. Tokyo Univ. and further studied in Europe and America; Parl. under-Secy. of Agr. and Com. Dept. '15-17; once connected with the Bank of Japan; is a leader of the Minsei-to Party. Add. Ushigome Minami-Enoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Maida, Minoru** D.L., B.A. and M.A., journalist connected with the Tokyo Asahi and specializes in Diplomacy, a thesis which won him the doctorate from the Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1878 in Kurume; Edu. at Kokumin English School and Oregon State Univ., class '00 and further at Iowa and California State universities. Lecturer on Diplomacy at the Gov. Coll. of Commerce. Works; Latest Diplomacy of the World, etc. Add. Zoshigaya, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Maida, Narakichi** b. in 1875; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '01; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam. and entered the service of the Dep't of Communications, in which he was Director of Communications Bureau, '24-25. Add. Koishikawa Takeshima-cho, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Majima, Otohiko** Mng.-Dir., Mitsui Bank; b. in Nagano, 1871; studied at Peers' School and then Aoyama-Gakuin; went over to U.S. to further prosecute his studies; on returning home '94 he entered the 15th Bank and then present Bank '98; sent to England by the Bank to study banking business; returned home '02; to England again '05; Man. of the Yokohama and then, Osaka Branch, 1914. Add. Kamakura. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 848](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n848/mode/1up))

**Maki, Aritsune** a Japanese alpinist and Mng. of Japan Mountaineers Society; b. 1893 in Sendai; edu. at Keio Univ., Law, '[?]7 and further at Columbia, Oxford, Berlin, returning home '21. Climbed the Alps six times during his stay in Europe, winning his fame as the first climber of the virgin Eiger, Started in June, '25 with five other sto ascend Mt. Alberta, Canadian Rockies. Add. Japan Mountaineer Society. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Maki, Bokushin** Pres. of Tokyo Marine Ins. Co. and Chiyoda Rubber Co.; b. 1854 in Shimabara, and held various offices in provincial and Central Governments. '75-'87; President of Sobu R'ly '88; was in Formosa as Governor and in other capacities '85-86; transferred to be Dir. of various Bureaus of Home Office till about '07. Once sat in Parliament. Add. Shiba Kurumacho, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Maki, Tetsu** Pres. of Ensuiko Sugar Manufacturing Co. and Karenko Timber Co., Dir. of the Taiwan Warehouse Co., etc.; b. 186[?] in Niigata-ken; studied at Keio Univ.; was in the services of the Hokuetsu Railway Co. and Oji Paper Mill Co.; afterwards made a tour through Europe and America; entered the service of the Ensuiko Sugar Manufacturing Co. in '07 as its Mng.-Dir. Add. Ushigome, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Makino, Ei-ichi** D.L., Prof. of Criminal Law at Imp. Tokyo Univ. his alma mater, class '03; b. 1878 at Takayama, Hida: Lecturer at the alma mater, '03; Procurator at the Tokyo District Court, '05; reappointed at the alma mater, '07; studied in Germany, Italy, England, chiefly criminal law, '08-13. Works; Criminal Law of Japan, Criminal Procedure, etc. Add. Imp Tokyo Univ. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Makino, Kikunosuke** L.L.D., Sectional Chief of the Court of Cassation; b. 1866 in Tokyo; grad. from Law Coll. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ., '91; served as Judge at the Shizuoka and Yokohama District Courts; attended the International Conference on the Bills of Exchange Law at the Hague as the Japanese representative, '01. Add. Akasaka-ku, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Makino, Nobuaki** Count (cr. Baron in '07, Viscount in '18), Lord Keeper of Privy Seal since '25; b. 1851, second son of the late Toshimitsu Okubo; Priv. Sec. to the then Premier Count Ito (late Prince) '88; a local Governor '91-92; Vice-Minister of Education '93; Minister at Rome '97; then at Vienna; Min. of Education '06; Privy Councillor in '09; Min. Agr. and Com. '11-12; of Foreign Affairs '13-14. Represented Japan at Versailles '18; Min. of Imp. Household, '21-25. Add. Komaba, near Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Makino, Tomitaro** Dr. Sc., a botanist and lecturer at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1863 in Kochi; started the first botanical magazine in Japan in '86; published "Flora Japonica" in '89; is editor of "Shokubutsu Kenkyu Zasshi" (a monthly devoted to the study of botany). Add. Imp. Tokyo Univ. Botanical Garden, Koishikawa, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Makita, Tamaki** Dr. Eng., Mng.-Dir., Mitsui Mining Co., Electro-Chemical Industry Co., Hokkaido Iron Works, etc.; b. in Osaka, 1871; grad. from Mining, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; entered Mitsui firm as expert, gradually rising to the present eminence. Add. Azabu Kita-Higakubo, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 849](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n849/mode/1up))

**Makoshi, Kojiro** D. Pharm., Standing Dir. of the Dai Nippon Brewery Co.; Dir. of the Electro-Chemical Industry Co. Add. Azabu-ku, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Makoshi, Kyohei** Pres. of Japan Brewery Co., Dir. of other Cos.; b. 1843 at Okayama; reached Tokyo '70 to find work with almost empty purse; started a lodging house; became acquainted with Baron Takashi Masuda and was appointed clerk of an exp. and imp. firm at Yokohama; transferred to the Mitsui Bussan: left it '88 to start the Yebisu Brewery at Meguro. Twice M.P. but has since abandoned politics. Add. Shiba Sakuragawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Mano, Bunji** ex-Pres. Imp. Kyushu Univ. ('13-26), Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. Institution of Mechanical Engineers, London; b. 1861 in Shizuoka; grad. from Mech. Eng., former Eng. College '81; further studied at Glasgow University; studied practical side of the science at Armstrong Works and returned home '90. Was appointed Prof. at the Imp. Univ.; sent to Paris on the occasion of World's Fair; Dir. of Technical Education Bureau '01-13, retaining the chair in the University. Add. Fukuoka. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Masaki, Naohiko** Dir. Fine Art Academy, Tokyo, since '01; b. 1862 in Kyoto; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '82; Personal Sec. to Min. of Edu. '97. Add. Ushigome Yarai, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Giichi** M.P., prop. of various popular magazines, Dir. of several Cos.; b. at Takata in 1869; grad. from Waseda Semmon Gakko, 1893; on the staff of several newspapers. Toured around the world in 1914. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Takashi** (Baron cr. '18), adviser to the Mitsui Family; b. 1848 in Sado of a petty samurai family; appointed cavalry officer and at the same time interpreter by the Shogunate: after the Restoration entered the Senshu Kaisha, an export and import firm in Yokohama; transferred to the Mitsui in which he has risen to the highest post. He is a noted connoisseur of arts, his collection of Buddhist pictures and images being reputed to be the finest in the country. Add. Shinagawa Gotenyama, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Taro** Man.-Dir. of Taiwan Sugar Mfg. Co., Nippon Artificial Fertilizer, Manju Life Ins. Co., Imperial Theatre, etc. b. in Tokyo, 1875, eldest son of Takashi Masuda; educated in England; is also a popular amateur playwright. Add. Nihombashi Hama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Tonosuke** Emeritus Prof. of Waseda Univ. where he was Prof. since '94; b. 1865 at Tsu. Studied English at Tokyo English School, '84-86; taught English at Kokumin English School, Tokyo, for a few years; founded with W. Eastlake a private English School and taught there, '91-92; was editorial writer of Ise Shimbun when only 17. Works: A. New English-Japanese Dictionary; Lectures on English Rhetoric. Add. Hongo Maruyama Shimmachi, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Masujima, Rokuichiro** D.L., lawyer, Barrister of Middle Temple '83; b. 1857 at Hikone; one of the pioneer graduates of Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., in '77. Opened with several friends the Eng- lish Law School (now Central Univ.); is a consulting lawyer to many Japanese and foreign establishments. Add. Koj. Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 850](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n850/mode/1up))

**Matsuda, Genji** M.P.; b. 1878 in Oita; grad. at Chuo Law School, in 1896; passed same year Higher Civil Service and Juridical Service examinations; sat on the Bench two years and then started practice in Tokyo; once a whip of the Seiyukai; Senior Sec. of Home Office '20-21; Vice-Chairman of Lower House in '22; now a leader of Minsei-to. Add. Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsuda, Michiko** B.A., lady Director of the Doshisha University, Kyoto since '19; b. in Tango, 1868; studied at the Kyoto Prefectural Girls' High School and then at the Doshisha Girls' College; taught English at a certain mission school, Y'hama; further studied at the Bryn Mawr Coll., Penn.; teacher at Kobe Jogakuin '99; Head of English Literature Dept., Doshisha Girls' College, and then the present post. Add. Doshisha, Kyoto. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsuda, Do-ichi** D.L., Ambassador in Rome since 1926; b. in Tokyo 1876; edu. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., passed Higher Civil Service Exam. and Juridical Serv. Exam., '01-02; jun. Procurator '02; Foreign Office Counsellor '02; Attache '03; 3rd Leg. Secy at Brussels, '07; Secy. at head office, '12; Sectional Chief of Political Bureau, '13; Emb. Counsellor at Rome, '17; on service at head office, '18; Dir. Com. Bureau, '18; Dir. Treaty Bureau, '19; Emb. Counsellor and Dir. of League of Nations Office in Paris '20-26. Add Japanese Embassy, Rome. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsudaira, Tsuneo** Ambassador at Washington since '25; b. in Tokyo, 1877; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ. (Economics). '02; passed Dip. Serv. Exam.; Legation and then Embassy Sec. at Peking, London, Paris, etc.; Con.-Gen. at Tientsin; was in charge of Dip. affairs of the Siberian Expedition '18-19; Dir. of the Europe-American Bureau, For. Office; Japanese representative in the Russo-Jap. negotiation at Changchung '22; Vice.-Min., '24-25. Add. Washington, U.S.A. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsudaira, Yasutaka** Marq., head of the old Daimyo of Fukui; Pres. Japan Agr. Society; b. 1867; studied in Germany '84; then at Cirencester Agr. Col., England; established a model exp. farm at his hereditary estate in Fukui. Add. Koi, Sekiguchi, Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsudaira, Yorinaga** Count, Mem. House of Peers; b. in 1874; grad. from Waseda Univ. '93; succeeded to the title '03, the House being formerly the Daimyo of Takamatsu; on Board of Trustees, Waseda University. Add. Somei, near Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsui, Keishiro** Baron (cr. '20). Ambassador to the Court of St. James since Oct. '25, Crown Mem. House of Peers, Min. of Foreign Affairs in the Kiyoura Cabinet; b. 1868 at Osaka; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '89; Secy. at Seoul '90-93; Secy. at Washington '94; at London '97; at Peking '02; Counsellor in Paris '06; transferred to Washington in '08 and then Vice-Min. of Foreign Affairs '13; a Peace Envoy while Ambassador at Paris '14-20, and was created Baron in recognition of his service during the War. Add. Japanese Embassy, London. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsukawa, Iwao** former Prince; b. 1862, 1st son of late Prince Masayoshi Matsukata; studied in Germany and after returning home held the post of Diplomatic Probationer, but soon resigned it to enter business; Auditor of the 15th Bank in '97 and then its Dir. and afterwards Pres. which post he resigned in '99. Retired from active life in '25 owing to the Bank's trouble. Add. Meguro, near Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 851](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n851/mode/1up))

**Matsukata, Kojiro** Pres. of the Kawasaki Dock Yard, the Kobe Gas Co., the Kyushu Electric Tramway Co., the Kawasaki Steamship Co., etc.; educated at the Peers' School and the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Europe and America; after returning home became Lecturer of the Tokyo Imp. Univ., which post he soon resigned to enter the business world; elected M.P. for Kobe in '21; involved in the financial trouble of the Dockyard. Add. Yamamoto-dori, Kobe. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsuki, Kan-ichiro** ex-Vice-Pres. Yamashita Firm; b. 1872 in Iyo; grad. at Law, Imp. Univ., Tokyo, class '96; Secretary of Communications Department '97; Secretary and Director of the Imperial Railways; Dir. Electric Bureau, Tokyo City Office, '20-22. Add. Akasaka Shin-saka, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Bunzaburo** Litt. D., Prof., Coll. of Litt., Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. 1869 in Kanazawa; grad. from Coll. of Litt., Imp. Tokyo Univ., in '93 and further studied at Berlin in '99; Lecturer at Waseda, etc., Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., then Prof. of Kyoto Univ. '06; Dean '08; visited India '18. Add. Imp. Kyoto Univ. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Joji** D.L., Pres. Kwansai Univ. (private), Osaka; b. 1877 in Saitama-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and passed Higher Civil Service Exam., 1900; Counsellor, Dept. of Agr. and Com. '01, combining professorship at his alma mater '03; dispatched by the Govt. to England, Germany and France to study commercial law, '06; Dir. S.M.R. Co. '19-23; Dir. of Legislative Bureau, '24. Add. Kwansai Univ., Osaka. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Ju-i** Vice-Gov. of Japan Industrial Bank since '23; b. 1873 in Fukushima-ken; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '98; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Comptroller of Taxes; Sec. of Finance Dept. and Section Chief of Tobacco Monopoly; Dir. Taxation Bureau, Finance Dept., '22-23. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Kumpei** M.P., a political writer, Parl. Counsellor of the Navy Dept.; b. 187[?] in Shizuoka-ken; studied in U.S.A.; was returned to the Lower House four times. Add. Omori Shimmei-yama, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Matataro** D. Litt., Ph. D. (Yale Univ.), Prof. of Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Gumma-ken; grad. from Lit. Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '93; studied Experimental Psychology in Yale Univ., '96-98; then at Leipzig Univ., '98-'00; Prof. of the Tokyo Higher Normal School; Lecturer and afterward Professor at the Literature College of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Kobinata Daimachi, Koishikawa, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumura, Ninzo** D. Sc., Botanist, ex-Prof. Science Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1856 in Ibaraki; studied in Germany for years, Assist.-Prof. of the Univ. in '83; full Prof. in '90; D. Sc. in '90; made an inspection tour through Europe and America '06; retired in '21. Add. Hongo Akebonocho, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumura, Shonen** D. Sc., D. Agr., Entomologist, Prof. at the Hokkaido Imp. Univ. from which he was graduated in '95; Dr.Sc. granted in '03 and Dr. Agr. in '05. Add. Sapporo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 852](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n852/mode/1up))

**Matsumura, Tsuruzo** D.E., Prof. of Eng. Coll. of the Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Hokkaido; grad. from the Eng. Coll. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; appointed Prof. of the 3rd High School, '96; Assist.-Prof. of the Kyoto Imp. Univ., '97; sent to Germany for study, '99; Prof. of the Science and Eng. Coll. of the Kyoto Imp. Univ., '01; Prof. of the Eng. Coll. of the same Univ. since June '14. Add. Kyoto. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsunaga, Naokichi** Emb. Counsellor in Brussels since '27; b. in 18[?]3 in Saga-ken. Edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Politics, '09 and passed the Dip. and Con. Serv. Exam. the same year; Eleve-Con. and then Attache in China, '08-10; Con. at Hamburg, '10; 3rd Emb. Sec. at Berlin, 14; recalled in '14 and made acting Chief of Secretariat, '16; Con. at Seattle the same year; Sec. and Sectional Chief at Head Office '20-23; Con-Gen. in Ottawa, '23-27. Add. Brussels. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsunaga, Yasuzayemon** Vice-Pres. of the Toho Electric Power Co., Dir. of Toho Gas Co. (Nagoya), etc.; b. 1875 in Iki; grad. from Keio Univ. in '00; entered the Bank of Japan, '00; afterwards established a firm and carried on coal business in Kobe and Osaka over ten years; founded an electric railway concern in Fukuoka, Kyushu, in '90. Add. Shimoochiai, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsunami, Nin-ichiro** D.L., Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ.: b. 1857 in Osaka; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '93; studied in Europe for years; Prof., Naval Cadet Sch. and next at alma mater; attended International Conference regarding maritime law several times; was on the staff of the Peace Envoy '19. Add. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsuo, Yoshio** 2nd Baron, Military pharmacist (ret.), 2nd son of late Shinzen Matsuo, 1st Baron and Governor of Bank of Japan; b. in Tokyo in 1876; grad. from Chiba Medical School in '97 and was appointed pharmacist which he left a few years after. His elder sister is wife of Dr. Kitazato, noted bacteriologist. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsuoka, Kimpei** 2nd Baron, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1876 in Tokyo; edu. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '00; further studied Political Economy at the post-grad. course. After teaching at the alma mater for two years as assist-Prof., proceeded to Europe in '05, returning home in '09 after studying Economics in England, etc. Resumed duty at the Univ. till '19; Japanese delegate at the International Seamens' Conference at Genoa in '20. Add. Azabu Zaimoku-cho, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsuoka, Yoyu** Vice-Pres. of the South Manchuria Rly Co., app. '27; b. in Yamaguchi-ken 1880; edu. at Law, State Univ., Oregon, U.S.A.; passed Diplomatic Service Exam. in '04; Secretary of Kwantung Leased Territory, next at Foreign Office, Legation and then of Embassy; was Consul-Gen. before he entered the present Co. Add. S.M.R. Co., Dairen. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsushima, Hajime** Minister to Poland; b. 1883 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Politics, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and passed Dip. Service Exam. '07; Eleve-Con.; 3rd Sec. Petrograd Embassy, recalled home '16; Per. Sec. of For. Minister and Counsellor to the Office, '17; Sectional Chief of the same '18; Consul-Gen. at Moscow, Harbin, etc.; represented Japan in the Russo-Japanese Conference at Dairen, '21; Emb. Counsellor at Paris, '24-27. Add. Jap. Legation, Warsaw. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 853](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n853/mode/1up))

**Matsushita, Teiji** M.D. (Japan and Halle), M.D.; b. 1875 in Kagoshima; edu. at the Fifth (Kumamoto) High School, class '94; further at Freiberg, Giessen, Halle, etc. '95-20; Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '[?]3-20, occupying chair of Hygiene, Micro-biology, etc. Works; (in Japanese) Parasitic Diseases, Wonderful Habits and Sights of Animals, etc. Add. Aoyama 6-chome, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Matsushita, Yusaburo** Pres. of the Yamato Shimbun Publishing Co., Ltd., a mine-owner; b. 1890 in Tokyo; grad. at the German Law Course of the Law College of the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; on the death of his adoptive father, succeeded to the presidency of Yamato Shimbun. Add. Ushigome Minami-Enoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Matsu-ura, Atsushi** Count (suc. '08), Mem. House of Peers, formerly of the house that held the fief of Hirado famous in the history of early foreign intercourse of Japan; studied at Imp. Univ., Tokyo, and at Trinity, Cambridge. Add. Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Matsu-ura, Chinjiro** Pres. of Seoul Imperial Univ.; b. in 1872 at Uwajima; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '98; Secretary at Tokyo Prefectural Office and at the Department of Education '98-11, sent to Germany '07 to inquire into education matters; Dir. of Special Edu. '12-24; Vice-Min. of Education, '24-27. Add. Seoul, Chosen. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Kaiun** D. Litt., Pres. of the Ryukoku Univ. Kyoto; b. 1857 in Miye-ken; studied Chinese classics and Buddhism; became Educational Superintendent to the Lord Abbot of Hongwanji Temple, '91; Superintendent of the Hongwanji Daigaku (Buddhist Univ.), '97; Lecturer at the Coll. of Literature, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; Lecturer of the Tokyo Univ. and then the present post. Add. Hongo-ku, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Tamon** Government Delegate to the International Labour Conference; b. in Tokyo; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '09; was once Private Secretary of the Home Minister and later Deputy-Mayor of Tokyo City. Add. Nakano-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Toshinari** Marquis, head of the great House of Kaga (founded in 16th cent.); b. 1885, son of a scion of the family and was adopted by it as heir. Attached to the British Army in France '15-16; was on the suite of Prince Higashi Fushimi on a visit to King George on behalf of the Emperor in '18; to Paris in connection with the Peace Treaty, '20. where the Marchioness died. Add. Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Toshisada** Visc., Min. of Communications in Adm. Kato Cabinet formed in '22, Min. of Agr. and Com. in the Kiyoura Cabinet. Mem. House of Peers; Lieut. Infantry (ret.); b. 1874 in Echigo, elder brother of above; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '03; took part in the Japanese-Russia War. Is also Pres. of Chuo Life Ins. Co., and Dir. of various concerns. Add. Nishi-Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Mikami, Sanji** Historian, Litt. D., Chief of Compilation Board, Imp. Household Dept., Prof. at Imp. Tokyo University since '92; b. 1865 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ., in '89; at the Post-Grad. Course; Lecturer at the same Coll., '91; Prof. at the Girls' Higher Normal School '92; sent to Germany to attend a conference of the International Oriental Society at Hamburg in '02. Add. Hongo Sendagi, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 854](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n854/mode/1up))

**Mikimoto, Kokichi** dealer in and originator of culture pearls; b. 1857 at Miye-ken; early started cultivation of pearl oyster, establishing a station in the Ago Bay, Toba, which has won many prizes and medals at home and foreign expositions. Add. Ginza, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minami, Daiso** M.D., Pres. of Minami Gastro-Enteric Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1878 in Fukushima-ken; edu. in Medicine, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '04; further at the post-grad. course and in Germany ('10-13). Served as assistant at his alma mater in '04 and next as regular staff member of the Stomach and Intestine Hospital, Tokyo. Add. Minami Hospital, Kobikicho, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minami, Hiroshi** Mem. House of Peers; b. 1869 in Toyama-ken; grad. Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; Secretary of the Cabinet finally its chief Secretary '08; Governor of Fukuoka; Vice-Min. of Education '18-22. Add. Yotsuya Naitomachi, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minami, Takajiro** Dr. Agr., Prof. at Imp. Hokkaido Univ. (Sapporo), since '81; b. 1859 in Hizen; grad. from the Sapporo Agr. Coll., class '81; visited U.S.A. as a commissioner of the Columbus Exhibition '89; again visited U.S.A. on party of Japanese business-men invited by American Cham. of Com. in '13. Add. Sapporo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minakata, Kumakusu** authority on lichens and moss; b. in Wakayama-ken; studied natural history at Michigan and Cambridge; wrote a number of works on his specialty in English. Add. Tanabe, Wakayama-ken. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minobe, Shunkichi** ex-Gov. Bank of Chosen '16-24; b. 1869 at Takasago, Harima; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '89; Personal Sec'y and Sectional Chief at Dep't Agr. and Com. and the Treasury; Pres. of Hokkaido Colonization Bank '98-16. Add. Aoyama Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minobe, Tatsukichi** D.L., Mem. of Imperial Academy, Prof. Imperial Univ., Tokyo; b. 1873 in Hyogo-ken, brother of S. Minobe, above; grad. from Law, Imp. Univ., Tokyo, class '97; studied in Germany, France and England '99-01. Add. Koishikawa Takehayacho, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Minoura Katsundo** ex-M.P. (of unbroken record); b. 1854 in Bungo; for a long while manager of the Hochi. He began political life under the then Count Okuma: three times entered the Govt. service first as Dir. Com. Bureau, Dep't of Agr. and Com. in '97, second as Vice-Min. of Communications in '98; was once elected Vice-Pres. of the House of Representatives; Min. of Communications '15-16. Was implicated in the Osaka Licensed Quarter Scandal in 1926-27. Add. Ushigome Higashi-gokencho, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Mishima, Shodo** Visc., 3rd son of the late Yataro Mishima, Governor of the Bank of Japan; b. 1897 in Tokyo; edu. at the Peers School; has since devoted himself to literary work and also to questions of social service; is an active manager of the Boy Scout Movement in Japan and attended the World Conference at Copenhagen in '24. Works: "Tragedy at Teradaya," "Miss and I," etc. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Mitsuchi, Chuzo** M.P.; Minister of Finance since May '27; b. in Kagawa-ken in 1871; grad. from Tokyo Higher Normal Sch. and further studied in England and Germany; Prof. at his alma mater; educational adviser of the former Korean Govt.; later took to politics and was repeatedly returned M.P.; Sec. to the Education Min. and then Chief Sec. of the Cabinet under the Seiyukai Administration; then Parl. Vice.-Min. of Agr. & Forestry; Minister of Education Apr. '27 and then present post. Add. Aoyama Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 855](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n855/mode/1up))

**Mitsui, family, The** Is one of the oldest millionaire families and the most noted hereditary houses of "business kings" in Japan, managing the big family concerns, the Mitsui Bank. Mitsui Bussan Kaisha (Trading Dep't), Mitsui Mining Co., Mitsui Trust Co., etc. Baron Hachiroemon Mitsui, the head of the family and Pres. of Mitsui Firm, was b. in 1857 in Kyoto, and studied at New Brunswick, U.S.A., '72-74 and England; succeeded to the estate in '85, he being the youngest brother of his predecessor who had no issue; created Baron '00. The house includes Morinosuke (Pres. Mitsui Bussan) Motonosuke (Pres. of Mitsui Mining Co.), etc. Add. Azabu Imaicho, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Mitsuma, Shinzo** D.L., Prof. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. and Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; b. 1879 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; stayed in Germany and Italy for study. Add. Azabu Naka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Matsunaga, Hoshio** Pres. of the Nippon Dempo Tsushinsha (Japan Telegraphic News Agency), Ltd.; b. 1866 in Kumamoto-ken; commenced his career as a politician; then a staff writer of the Osaka Asahi and acted as its war correspondent during the Japan-China war: later an official of the Formosan Government-General; afterwards established, with Shinji Gondo, the Nippon Dempo Tsushinsha. Add. Harajuku, Sendagaya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Miura, Kanoyuki** D. Litt., Prof. Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Shimane-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll. Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; appointed Compiler of Japanese history, '05; present post since 1909. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Miura, Kinnosuke** M.D., Emeritus Prof. Imp. Univ. of Tokyo, ex-Physician-in-Ordinary to the Court; b. in Tokyo 1864; grad. Med. Coll. Imp. Tokyo Univ. '87; prosecuted his study in Germany and America '89-92; Ass't-Prof. in his alma mater on returning; full prof. in '95. He accompanied the Peace Envoys '18 as an unofficial member, and the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '21. Add. Kanda Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Miwa, Shintaro** M.D., specialist in Children's Diseases, and Dir. of the Enjudo (private) Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1870 in Kanagawa; studied at Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '94; further in Germany '95-98; served as assist. at first at his alma mater; was lecturer there on returning home; Prof. '01-15, when he resigned to begin practice. Add. Ura-Sarugaku-cho, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Miwa, Zembei** Prop. of the Marumiya Firm, manufacturer, whole-sale dealer and exporter of toilet articles, drugs, soaps, etc.; b. 1871 in Tokyo and succeeded to his father's business in 1[?]71; once Mem. of Tokyo Chamber of Commerce. Add. Nihombashi Tachibanacho, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Miwata, Motomichi** adopted son of late Miwata Masako, lady educationalist; and Principal of Miwata Girls High School, etc.; b. 1872 in Kagawa-ken; grad. from Philosophy and Sociology, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '01. Add. Kojimachi Sanbancho, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 856](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n856/mode/1up))

**Miyabe, Kingo** D. Sc., (Japan), D.Sc. (Harvard), Prof. at Imp. Hokkaido Univ., Dir. of Botanical Garden, Sapporo; b. in Tokyo 1860; grad. at the Sapporo Agr. College, '81; sent to U.S.A. to study botany under Asa Gray; returned home '87. Add. Sapporo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyairi, Keinosuke** D.M., Prof. at Medical Coll., Imp. Kyushu Univ. since '06; grad. from Medical Coll., Imp. Kyoto Univ.; is an authority on parasitology. Add. Fukuoka, Kyushu. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyajima, Mikinosuke** M.D., Pathologist, M.P., Editor of Japan Medical World, and Mem. of the Kitazato Infectious Disease Institute; b. 1882 in Yamagata-ken; Edu. at Coll. of Science, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '98; further at the Post-grad. course till '00; Lecturer on Zoology at the alma mater '00; studied insects with special bearing to diseases at the Post-grad. course, Kyoto Univ. in '01 and was Lecturer at the Univ. the same year; expert at the Vaccine Laboratory, Tokyo, '03; sent to U.S.A., '04; expert at the Gov. Infectious Disease Institute, '05; sent to Philippines '09; was granted the degree by submitting thesis '09; removed to the present Institute in '14. Japanese delegate at the 2nd International Health Conference at Geneva, '19; Dir. of the Sanitary Dept. of the Tokyo Municipality '21-23; visited U.S.A. '26 at the invitation of the Rockefeller Institute. Add. Kanda Jimbo-cho, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyajima, Seijiro** Mng.-Dir. of the Nisshin (Japan-China) Spinning Co.; b. in Tochigi-ken in 1879; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '06; served at Sumitomo Mining Dept. for a few years; entered Tokyo Spinning Co. '08 and remained there till he was installed in the present post '14. Add. Shiba Isarago, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Hiidzu** M.D., Mem. House of Peers, Mem. Imperial Academy, Emeritus Professor of Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1848 in Tokyo; educated in France and was Prof. and Dean of Med. Coll. Imp. Tokyo Univ. Add. Koishikawa Takehaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Kiichi** D. Sc., Ph. D., M.A., Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1876 in Hyogo-ken; studied botany at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied at Cornell Univ., U.S.A., '02; then at Bonn Univ., Germany, '02-05. Add. Aoyama Minami-machi, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Yasu-ko** Mrs., author who is publishing the monthly called 'Women Current'; b. 1890 in Kyoto; edu. at Ochanomizu Girls' School, and is a well-known contributor to journals; has written a number of stories. Add. Yotsuya Aizumi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Yonekichi** Litt. D., Dir. of Tokyo Higher Normal School, Court Councillor; b. 1860 in Wakayama-ken; studied at Keio '72-75; Assist. Prof. of Higher Normal School, '81-86; made an inspection tour abroad '86-88; was once a Commissioner of the Imp. Household and Dir. of the Imp. Museum. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Yujiro** D. Litt., author and journalist; b. 1860 at Kanazawa; grad. Philosophy, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '81; visited the South Seas and in '03 made a trip round the world. He is a conspicuous figure in the world of letters and journalism, and has written many works, chiefly philosophical and literary. He was editor of the fortnightly magazine, 'Nihon oyobi Nihon-jin', but left it a few years ago and started his own organ 'Gakan.' Add. Akasaka Shinsakamachi, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 857](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n857/mode/1up))

**Miyakegawa, Momotaro** Pres. Mitsubishi Trading Co.; b. in Ehime-ken, 1869; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch., '92; entered the firm's service '93; Branch Manager in various places and then promoted to the present post. Add. Nakano near Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 858](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n858/mode/1up))

**Miyao, Shunji** Governor of Hokkaido, '22-23; b. in Niigata-ken; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '[?]8; Sec. of the Finance Dept.; transferred to Formosan Govt.; Vice-Pres. of the Colonizaton Bureau, 1910; Gov. of Aichi-ken '21; Gov. of Oriental Development Co., '24. Add. Ushigome Yarai, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 858](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n858/mode/1up))

**Miyaoka, Tsunejiro** Lawyer; b. 1866 in Osaka; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., 1887 and entered the Govt. service, serving at Washington and then Berlin, and then at the Head Office which appointed him Min. Plen. in '09; retired and started general practice; appointed by the trustees of the Carnegie Endowment for International Peace as its special correspondent; attended the meetings of the American Bar Ass'n and the Canadian Bar Ass'n. as guest in '18, and elected honorary member of each ass'n.; visited London and Paris in '24 as guest of the Bar of England and the Bar of Paris; is an associate member of the Association of the Bar of the City of New York and local counsel in Japan for U.S. Steel Corporation and other American corporations; also acts as counsel for the N.Y.K., Tokyo Marine & Fire Ins. Co., Oki Electric Co., and several other Japanese corporations. Add. Aoyama Minami-machi 4-chome, Tokyo. (Office, Yuraku Bldg., Marunouchi, Tokyo). — p. 63 ([scan leaf 858](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n858/mode/1up))

**Miyata, Mitsuo** Crown Mem. House of Peers, Superintendent of Metropolitan Police since '27; b. in Miye-ken in 1878; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ. at Law in '05 and passed Higher Civil Service Exam.; Sec. to the House of Peers, and Prefectural Gov. till '22; Chief Secretary of Cabinet in Kato (Adm.) Ministry. Add. Shimo-shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 858](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n858/mode/1up))

**Miyauchi, Kunitaro** Dir. of Mining Bureau since '23; b. 1876 in Ibaraki-ken; grad. from the Law Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ. and entered the Dept. of Agr. and Com. '08; passed Higher Civil Service Exam.; Secretary to the Patent Bureau and then Private Sec. to the Min. of Agr. and Com. Add. Kol. Sasugaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 858](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n858/mode/1up))
