**Tamura, Shinkichi** ex-M.P., Prop. of Tamura Trading Firm, Kobe, ex-Chairman, Kobe Chamber of Commerce, Japan-Canada Bank and Nippon Rice Cleaning Co.; b. 1863 in Osaka; he proceeded to Canada when 25 years old and finally opened above bank. Add. Sakai-cho, Kobe. — p. 103 ([scan leaf 898](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n898/mode/1up))

**Tanabe, Benkichi** Dir. Kuhara Trading Co.; b. 1872 in Kyoto Prefecture; grad. from Politics Imp. Tokyo Univ., '99; entered Sumitomo Bank but removed to Kyushu Colliery and Steamship Co. as its Manager; then at Okura Mining and finally entered the present Co. Add. Ushigome Sadowaracho, Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanabe, Chuyu** Prof. at Senshu and Rikkyo Univ's; b. 1891 in Nagasaki; edu. at Morning Star School (Tokyo), 1st High School and Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Economy, class '16. Works: Translation of Cole's World of Labor; Fundamental Idea of Cole's Sociology, etc. Add. Rikkyo Univ., Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanabe, Gen** Litt. D., Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. 1875 in Tokyo; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Philosophy, class '98; Assist. Prof. at Tohoku (Sendai) Univ. till '19; sent abroad for further study '22-24. Works; Latest Natural Science, Studies on Science, etc. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Gi-ichi** Baron (cr. '20) Gen. (ret.), Premier and Minister of Foreign Affairs since Apr. '27, Leader of Seiyukai since '25; b. 1863 in Yamaguchi; Sub-Lieut. '86; grad. from the Staff Coll. '98; Dir. of Military Affairs Bureau '10-13; despatched abroad '13; Com. of the 2nd Brigade, Infantry; Lieut.-Gen. and Vice-Chief of the Gen. Staff, Oct. '15; Minister of War in Hara, Takahashi and Yamamoto Cabinets; organized a ministry in Apr. '27. Add. Aoyama Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Ginnosuke** Director of Tanaka Mining Co. and Auditor of the Taihoku Savings Bank; b. 1873 in Tokyo, grandson of the late Heihachi Tanaka, widely known as "Raw-silk-king" in his day; studied at Trinity Hall, Camb., England; is a well-known figure in social circles. Add. Azabu Ichibei-cho, Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Hozumi** D.L., M.A. (Columbia), Chairman, Board of Dir. Waseda Univ.; b. in 1875 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Waseda '96 and further studied '01-03 in America, England and Germany; once connected with the Yomiuuri and was also the editor-in-chief of the Nichi Nichi. Toured abroad in 1921-22. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Jiro** Mng.-Dir. of Nippon Petroleum Co.; b. in 1873 at Saga-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '98; passed the higher civil service examination; Chief of the Bureau of Post and Telegraphs of the Communication Dept. '15; represented Japan at the International Wireless Conference held at Berlin '06-08; retired from the official service in '17. Add. 270 Chojamaru, Kami Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Mitsu-aki** Count (cr. Visc. '87, Count '90), ex-Minister of the Imp. Household; b. 1843. in Kochi-ken; has had a varied career, having been connected with the Army till '81 when he retired from the service; Chief Commissioner of the Metropolitan Police, '89-91; President of the Peers School, '92; Vice-Min. of the Imp. Household; full Minister '98-09; Pres. of the Compilation Office of the Imp. Household, '18-19. Add. Iwabuchi, Shizuoka-ken. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Shohei** D. Sc., b. in Awaji 1862; studied acoustics at Berlin, '84-98, and received a reward from the Kaiser for his invention of wind instrument; is a consulting engineer of the Imp. Railways and is also doing researches on Western and Japanese musics to improve the latter. Add. Shiba Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 104 ([scan leaf 899](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n899/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Son** Dir. of Oriental Compressor Co.; adopted son of Count Tanaka, ex-Minister of Imperial Household; b. 1864 in Fukushima, brother of Sei Iwagami; studied law and economics in France '91-00; lecturer at Peers' School '01; M.P. from Kochi in '02. Add. Kojimachi Motozono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Ryuzo** M.P., ex-Dir. of Osaka Shosen Kaisha; b. 1864 in Akita-ken; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo, Univ. '89; entered Dep't. of Agr. and Com. in which he was Dir. of Mining Bureau '92; became lawyer '93; Secretary of the House of Representatives '95; took to business and Mng.-Dir. of Fujita Firm; Vice-Min. of Agr. and Com. in the Seiyukai Ministry, '19. Add. Azabu Nakano-cho, Tokyo. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Tokichi** Amb. to Soviet Russia since '25; b. in Kyoto-fu 1878; studied at Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch.; Consul at Singapore, Hongkong, Seattle, etc. '03-10; Emb. Counsellor at Washington; Dir. Trade Bureau, '22; Vice-Min. For. Affairs and Director of Intelligence Bureau '22-23; Pres. Japan Times, '25. Add. Moscow. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tanakadate, Aikitsu** D. Sc., Mem. House of Peers; b. 1856 in Iwate-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., and was in England and Germany to complete his studies in physics; Prof. of physics '91-20; member of the Committee of Earthquake Investigation of the Imp. Geodetic Commission and of the Imp. Academy; Committee of Mil. Aviation since '12; aeronautic scientist, Emeritus Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ. Was granted 2nd Class Rising Sun for his service about military balloons during the Russo-Japanese War. Add. Hongo Yayoicho, Tokyo. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Taniguchi, Shoshin** Vice-Adm., Com.-in-Chief of Kure Naval Station since '26; b. 1870 in Hiroshima; graduated from the Naval Cadet School, '92; steadily promoted till he was appointed Rear-Adm. in 1917 and Commander of the Makong Naval Port, Pescadores, having in the meanwhile been Adjutant to the Minister, captain of a battle-cruiser, Dir. of Personnel Bureau; Vice-Adm., '23; Dir. of the Naval Academy and then Com.-in-Chief of 2nd Squadron '25-26. Add. Kure Naval Station. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tanimoto, Itaro** Mng.-Dir. of Mitsubishi Warehousing Co.; b. 1871 in Tottori-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '98; entered Mitsubishi Firm and has ever since been connected with it; present post in Oct. '17, after several years at the warehousing business as Manager of the Co's branches at Osaka and Kobe. Add. Aoyama Minamicho, Tokyo. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tasaki, Shinji** Pres. of Kobe Higher Com. School since '25; b. in Nagasaki-ken. Grad. Tokyo Higher Com. School, class '01. Lecturer at the alma mater for about a year; sent to England for study, '02-06; Prof. at Nagasaki Higher Com. School in '06; at Kobe Higher Com. School, '08, teaching marine insurance. Visited Europe in '14 and England in '23. Add. Kobe Higher Com. School. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tashiro, Yoshinori** M.D., Prof. in Med. Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1864; grad. from the Med. Coll. in '89; a chair at his alma mater '92; established the Tashiro Hospital in '95; was for a time in charge of the Mitsui Charity Hospital opened in '08. Add. Shitaya Neribei-cho, Tokyo. — p. 105 ([scan leaf 900](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n900/mode/1up))

**Tatsuki, Shichita** ex-Amb. to Brazil; b. 1867 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and passed Dip. service Exam. '96; Eleve-Consul, Legation Attache and Embassy Sec. successively; Secretary and Section Chief of the For. Office; Emb. Counsellor in Petrograd and then Paris, 1909. Min. to Chili '17; Minister to Netherlands '20-23; Amb. to Brazil, '23-27. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tatsumi, Konojo** Dir. Yokohama Specie Bank; b. 1864 in Wakayama-ken; studied at Keio; early entered the service of the Bank; was one time Manager of its London Branch. Add. Ushigome Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Kuni-ichi** D. Eng., Mem. of the Imperial Academy, Prof. of Metallurgy, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1872 in Shimane-ken; grad. from above institution, class '97, and after some years' further studies in Germany, was appointed to his alma mater. Add. Imp. Tokyo Univ. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Magoichi** M.P., Par. Vice-Min., Dept. of R'lys & Fin. '24-27; b. 1869 in Shimane-ken, being elder brother of the above. Grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. class '95. Has since been in the civil service, first in Korea and then in the provinces, rising to the post of Gov. of Miye in '12, Miyagi in '14, and Hokkaido '15-20; is a leader of the Minsei-to Party. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Yoshisumi** M.D., Dir. of the Sanitary Laboratory, Home Office '87-23. Mem. of Japan Pharmacopoeia Com., Home Office, etc.; b. 1855 in Saga-ken; grad. from the Imp. Tokyo Univ. (Pharmacy) class '81; further studied his speciality by Gov. order at Berlin, Heidelberg, etc., '90-93. Has since devoted himself to pharmaceutical affairs as expert of the Home Office or member of institutions in this line. Add. Kyobashi Echizen-bori, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Yutaka** Dir. of Mitsubishi Paper Dep't, etc.; b. in Hyogo-ken in 1871; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. '95; entered the Mitsui Firm and remained in the service till 1908; Govt. Commercial Commissioner in London, '08-10, and re-entered the Co. Add. Naka-Shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Terajima, Sei-ichiro** 2nd Count, Mem. of House of Peers; b. 1870 in Tokyo, eldest son of the late Count Terajima who in his days occupied chair of Foreign Office; studied at Pennsylvania and next at Paris; returned home in '04; Pers. Secretary to Count Hayashi, then Foreign Minister. Add. Shiba Shirokane, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tochinai, Sojiro** Full Adm. (ret.): was b. in 1866 at Morioka; Midshipman in '83; was the Personal Secretary to the Minister '04-08; Com. of the Training Squadron. Chief of the Naval Affairs Bureau, etc., Vice-Min. of Navy '16-20; Com. of the 1st Fleet, '20-21; Com. of Saseho Naval Station '22-23. Add. Mita Tsuna-machi, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Togo, Heihachiro** Count (cr. '07), Admiral of Fleet, O.M. (Br.), 1st Class Golden Kite and Grand Order of Chrysanthemum, Mem. of Supreme Mil. Council, Lord Tutor to the Crown Prince; b. in 1847, a son of petty retainer of the Lord of Kagoshima. He commenced sailor's career at 16 and at 21 first came under fire, in fighting with the late Enomoto's Kwaiten; studied in England '71-73. In the Japan-China War, he commanded the cruiser Naniwa, and sank the Chinese transport Kowsing, a British steamer flying the British flag; Rear-Adm. after the war; Vice-Admiral in '90; Commander-in-Chief of the Combined Fleet in the '04-05 War and hero of Japan Sea battle fought on 27th May, '05; full Admiral, June '04; Chief of Naval Staff, '05; Mem. Sup. Mil. Council '09; Adm. of Fleet in '12. Attended King George's Coronation on the suite of Prince Higashi-Fushimi. Add. Koj. Kami-rokuban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 106 ([scan leaf 901](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n901/mode/1up))

**Tokonami, Takejiro** M.P. and Adviser to the Minsei-to; Minister of Home Affairs in the Seiyukai Cabinet '18-22; b. in Kagoshima, 1863; grad. from politics, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '90; entered civil service at the Home Office, either at head office or local office, in which he rose to be Governor of Tokushima-ken, '01; Dir. Local Administration in '03; Vice-Minister, '11-12; Pres. of Imp. Govt. Rlys., '13. Add. Azabu Mikawadai, Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tokugawa, Iyesato** Prince (cr. '84), President of the House of Peers since '03; b. 1863 at Tokyo; is the third son of Yoshiyori Tokugawa, one of the 3 Tokugawa branches, and was adopted as heir in '[?]8 by the last of the Tokugawa Shoguns; studied in England, '73-77. Toured abroad '10; a Japanese Delegate at the Washington Disarmament Conference in '21. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tokugawa, Kuninori** Marquis, head of the clan of Mito, one of the three Houses of Tokugawa which was founded by Yorifusa, 11th son of Iyeyasu. The Marquis was b. 1886, and is now Vice-Pres. of the Red Cross Society. Add. Honjo Komme, Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tokugawa, Satotaka** Count (cr. '94), of the Tayasu branch of the Tokugawa; Grand Chamberlain to H.I.M. the Emperor '23-26; b. 1865 in Tokyo; toured in Europe and America '89-90; nominated Mem. of the House of Peers in '93. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tokugawa, Yoshichika** Marquis (2nd), Mem. of House of Peers; b. '86 of the Matsudaira (Marq.) family and adopted by the late Girei Tokugawa, ex-Daimyo of Owari, one of the three branches of the Tokugawa. Edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '91-93 in Literature and Zoology; is noted as a big game hunter. Add. Azabu Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tokunaga, Shigeyasu** D. Sc., Prof. at Waseda Univ. since '11 and Dir. of Waseda Technical School; b. 1874 at Kagoshima; edu. in Geology, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '97. Supervised a gold mine at Kaori, '03-09; Dir. of Tokyo Eng. School, '07-09; made an inspection tour in Europe and America, '23. Add. Waseda Univ., Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tokutomi, Iichiro** Journalist, chief editor and proprietor of the Kokumin Shimbun, Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '11); b. 1868, at Kumamoto; edu. at the Doshisha, Kyoto; published about '86 "The Future Japan"; issued in '87 a monthly magazine "The Friend of the Nation"; started in '90 the "Kokumin" (Nation), a daily paper and an English monthly the "Far East"; Counsellor of the Home Office in '97; visited Europe and America in '96; founded a philanthropic institute Aoyama Kaikan by donating his own mansion in '22. Works: "Yoshida Sho-in", "History of Modern Japan", etc. Add. Omori, Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tomi-i, Masa-akira** Baron (created '26), D.L. Privy Councillor; Emeritus Prof. of Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1858 at Kyoto; grad. Lyons Univ., France '83; Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. '85; once Dean of the College. Add. Ichigaya Yakoji-machi, Tokyo. — p. 107 ([scan leaf 902](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n902/mode/1up))

**Tomonaga, Sanjuro** Litt. D., Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. 1871 in Nagasaki-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Philosophy, class '98 and further studied at Heidelberg, Leipzig, and other places. Works; Kant's Pacifism, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Toshimitsu, Tsurumatsu** Pres. of Kinugawa Hydro-Elec. Co., Dir. of Tokyo Gas Co., etc.; b. 1863 in Oita-ken; edu. at Meiji Univ.; became lawyer and struggled his way up to success; once M.P., but soon resigned to enter into business; Dir. of the then Tokyo Railway Co. Add. Kami Osaki near Tokyo. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Toyama, Chinkichi** M.D., Pres. Tokyo Microscopic Society. Dir. Tokyo Sanitary Laboratory, etc.; b. 1857 in Yamagata-ken; edu. in Bacteriology (elective course), Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '83; further studied his speciality in Germany and America, '07-09; Instructor and Dir. of the Yamagata Medical School, '83-88; est. a private Tokyo Microscopic Institute in '91 which has turned out thousands of young microscopists. Works; (in Japanese) Prevention and Treatment of Beriberi, Applied Bacteriology, etc. Add. Shiba Shirokane, Tokyo. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Toyama, Mitsuru** Political boss, ex-Pres. of the Gen-yosha (a political club); b. 1855 in Fukuoka-ken; was thrown into prison for having sided with the rebels in the Civil War of '77; now stands aloof from politics; has befriended Chinese and other revolutionists. Add. Azabu Reinanzaka, Tokyo. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Toyoshima, Naomichi** D.C.L., b. 1871 in Tokyo; grad. '95 from Law Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Pub. Proc. in '97; attached to the Supreme Court '07; Counsellor of Justice Dept., '11. Has also been Lecturer at his alma mater, Waseda Univ., etc. Add. Hongo Akebonocho, Tokyo. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Tsuboi, Kumazo** Litt. D., Emeritus Professor of Imp. Tokyo Univ. (Lit. Coll.); b. in 1858 at Osaka; grad. in Litt. of the Univ.; sent by Govt. to Europe for study; Prof. at his alma mater in '91; Mem. of Imp. Ac'y. Add. Hongo Yayoicho, Tokyo. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Tsubouchi, Shiko** actor, dramatist, and literary writer, nephew of Dr. Tsubouchi; b. 1887 in Nagoya; edu. at Waseda Univ. in English Literature, class '09; stayed in England for six or seven years; after returning home he was for a while an actor of a new school, then an instructor at the alma mater; now adviser of the Takarazuka Opera School near Osaka; is famous as a dancing master. Works; Translation of Moliere's complete works, Masterpieces of Ibsen, and some original works. Add. Takarazuka Kageki School. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Tsubouchi, Yuzo** Litt. D. author and a man of letters; b. 1859 in Aichi-ken; has been connected with Waseda Univ., from its inception '82, being now its Emeritus Prof.; grad. Litt. Coll. of the now Imp. Tokyo Univ. '82; is more widely known in public as pioneer novelist and dramatist and a Shakespearian scholar of Japan, having almost completed translation of his plays; has also written several works on ethics; organized in '11 the Literature and Drama Society but dissolved it in '13. Add. Ushigome Yochomachi, Tokyo. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Tsuchii, Rinkichi** (nom de plume Bansui), Prof. at 2nd (Sendai) High School; b. 1871 in Sendai; grad. Litt., Imp. Univ. Tokyo; further studied literature in Europe '01-04. Has written a number of poetical works; translated Sartor Resartus. Add. Sendai. — p. 108 ([scan leaf 903](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n903/mode/1up))

**Tsuchiya, Masanao** Visc., Master of Ceremonies and Chamberlain to the Emperor; b. 1881 of a former Daimyo family; grad. Literature, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '08; was on the suite of the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '21. Add. Aoyama-Onden, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsuda, Umeko** lady educationist and founder of the Joshi Eigaku-juku (Girls' English School) in Tokyo; b. 1865 in Tokyo; daughter of the late Tsuda Sen, an agriculturist; was among the first batch of girls sent to U.S.A. for study by the Imp. Govt. '71-82; teacher in the Peers' School and lecturer in the Women's Higher Normal School on returning home; has frequently visited America. Add. Kojimachi Gobancho, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsukakoshi, Utaro** Mng.-Dir. of Nippon Chemical Fertilizer Mfg. Co.; b. in 1879 in Tokyo; studied pharmacy and chemistry at Tokyo Pharmaceutical School and at the Institute of Technology, Boston, U.S.A.; appointed Chief of Dry Distillation Works, and subsequently of Chemical Laboratory of the Karafuto Gov't; left the official service '12 and formed Nippon Chemical Pulp Co. in '13 and later the present Co. Add. Ichigaya Tanimachi, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsukamoto, Kiyoharu** formerly Vice-Minister of Home Office; b. 1872 in Hyogo-ken. Grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '02. After passing the Higher Civil Service examination he was given subordinate posts at the local and central Government as junior Sec. at Fukui, Kanagawa, Kyoto, etc.; then Dir. of Shrines Bureau, Dir. of Legislative Bureau in '24; Perm. Vice-Min. of Home Affairs '24-26. Add. Akasaka Shin-saka, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsukamoto, Yasushi** D. Eng., ex-Dean of the Eng. Coll. and Prof. of Architecture, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1869 in Kyoto; grad. in '93 from Imp. Tokyo Univ.; further studied in England, Germany and France '99-02; Prof. at the alma mater on his return. Add. Koishikawa Hisakatacho, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsumura, Hidematsu** D. L., Economist, Director of the Kuhara Firm, Ltd.; b. 187[?] in Wakayama-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Com. School and Post-graduate course, class '99; further studied in Germany and America, '99-03; Prof. at the Kobe Higher Com. School on returning home; entered the Firm in '20. Works; Principles of National Economics, New Principle of Political Economy, Commercial Policy, etc. Add. Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsunashima, Kakichi** Pastor of Bancho Congregational Church, Tokyo; b. 1860 in Okayama-ken; studied at Doshisha, and Yale Univ., U.S.A.; Pastor of the present church for about twenty years. Peace Envoy to U.S.A. in '13 representing Christian churches of Japan, and to Constance in '14 representing Japan. Add. Koj. Nakarokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsuneto, Noritaka** D. Agr., Pres. of Rasa Island Phosphate Co.; b. 1857 in Oita-ken; grad. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. '83; Expert at Dept. Agr.; Prof. Agr. and Forest. School, at Morioka, then at Kagoshima; Expert of Formosan Gov't; retired from official service '04. Add. Ushigome Wakamiyacho, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsurumi, Sakio** Vice-Min. of Agr. and Com. in Kiyoura Cabinet; b. 1873 in Toyama-ken; grad. from Politics, Imp. Tokyo Univ. and entered the Home Office '99; served in the local Govt. before he was transferred to the Dept. '08; Dir. of the Aquatic Product Bureau '17; Dir. of the Commercial Bureau '20-24; twice dispatched abroad; represented Japan in the Economic Conference of the Allies in Paris during the Great War. Add. Aoyama Takagi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 109 ([scan leaf 904](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n904/mode/1up))

**Tsurumi, Yusuke** a publicist and writer.; b. 1885 in Gumina-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law; was long on civil service, the last as Secretary of the Govt. Railway Dept. which he resigned in '22; was resident officer for the Railways during '16-19 in America and Europe; lectured at Brown Institute on Japan-American relations in '25; unsuccessfully contested in the general election of '24; toured through U.S.A. '27. Works: South Sea Travel, Impressions of Great Men in Europe and America, Americanism and Future of the Japan-American Relations, Stories of Three Cities, Contemporary Japan (in English), etc. Add. Azabu Sangenya, Tokyo — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Kakichi** Mem. House of Peers; b. 1866 in Tokyo; graduated Law, Imperial Tokyo Univ. '91; entered the Department of Communications, in which he held post of Dir. of Shipping Bureau till '10; Dir. of Civil Affairs, Formosa, '11-15; Vice-Min. of Communications, '17-18; Gov.-Gen. of Formosa '23-24. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Ko** (penname Ro-an), a man of letters and novelist deep in Russian Literature; b. 1857 in Tokyo but has no school career; adviser to Maruzen Co. Ltd. Add. Yodobashi Kashiwagi, Tokyo. — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Sadatsuchi** Min. Resident in Turkey '20-23; b. 1865 at Kokura; grad. Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; attache in '89; eleve Consul at Shanghai in '90; to Seoul in '93; Consul-General in '02; Min. and Con.-General in Brazil in '07; Min. to Sweden, '11-18; placed on retired list in '23. Represented Japan in the International League as Senior Commissioner. Add. Nakashibuya, Tokyo. — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Yasuya** Count (Baron '07, Vis. '11), Privy Councillor since '24; twice Minister of Foreign Affairs; b. 1865 in Kumamoto-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; attache at Washington '87; Per. Sec. to the Min. of Agr. and Com. and at the Foreign Office; Legation Sec. at London '93; at Peking '93-98; Dir. of Political Bureau; Vice-Min. of Foreign Affairs '00; Minister to Peking '01-06; Amb. at Vienna, and next at Washington '09; Minister of Foreign Affairs in '12; again the same portfolio in the Hara and Kato (Adm.) Cabinets. He was Ambassador to Petrograd during the Great War. Countess Uchida was educated at Vassar College, U.S.A. Add. Nishi Okubo, near Tokyo. — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchigasaki, Sakusaburo** M.P. (Kenseikai), and Mng. International League of Nations Ass'n., Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1877 in Miyagi-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., English Literature and is one of the pupils of Lafcadio Hearn, also studied at Oxford '08-11. Again went abroad '20-21. Works; Life and Literature. Religion of Contemporaries, Lloyd George, Lincoln, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchimura, Kanzo** Christian teacher and essayist; b. 1861 in Saitama; grad. from Sapporo Agr. Coll. '81; clerk at the Dep't of Agr. and Commerce; studied at Amherst Univ. '[?]3-'[?]3; in- structor at the 1st Higher School, Tokyo, and from religious scruples he had to resign; then took to journalism; and is now educationalist and non-Sectarian Christian teacher; is publishing a religious monthly "Seisho-no-Kenkyu"; started in '26 another religious magazine Christian Intelligencer. Wrote "Representative Men of Japan," "How I Became a Christian", etc. Add. Tsunohazu, near Tokyo. — p. 110 ([scan leaf 905](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n905/mode/1up))

**Uchiyama, Kojiro, Baron** General (ret. '22); b. 1859 in Tottori; Sub. Lieut. (Art.) '79; attached to Legations in Russia and France; Commanded Art. Corps in the Russo-Japanese War; attache at Petrograd till '07; Commander of Yura Fort '08 and then Divisional Commander; Chief Aide-de-Camp to the Emperor '12-22; General '15. Add. Harajuku, Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Ugaki, Issei** Gen. Supreme War Councillor; b. in 1863; Sub-Lt. in '93; Lt.-Gen. in '19, having been in the meanwhile Dir. of Staff Coll., attache at Petrograd, Vice-Min. of War, etc.; Minister of War '25-27. Add Yotsuya Naito-machi, Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Ukita, Kazutami, D.L** historian and educationalist, Prof. Waseda Univ.; b. 1860 in Kumamoto; studied English under Capt. Janes of U.S.A.; grad. Doshisha '79; engaged for some while in religious work and journalism; Prof. at his alma mater; entered Yale '92 and studied philosophy and politics; again Prof. at the Doshisha, '97. Add. Takata, near Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Uno, Hogara, D.M** Emeritus Prof. Imp. Tokyo Univ. and Prop. of the Rakuzando Hospital for skin diseases; b. in Shidzuoka in 1850; grad. Medicine at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '76; Assist.-Prof. in the Coll. in '81, and subsequently appointed Vice-Pres. of University Hospital; sent to Germany in '80 for study. Resigned the chair in '01 to establish the hospital. Add. Hongo Yumicho, Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Urabe, Hyakutaro, D.L. (Keio)** Prof., Keio Univ.; b. 1869 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. from Keio, '95; was on the editorial staff of the Jiji '95; Prof. at his alma mater and the Chief Editor of the Nippon. Add. Keio Univ., Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Usami, Katsuo** Pres. National Resources Invest. Board; b. in Yamagata-ken, 1869; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., 1896; Counsellor to various local Governments and then to Home Office; Gov. of Toyama-ken, 1908; Chief of Home Affairs Dep't., Korea in '10; Governor of Tokyo-fu '21-25; Pres. of Decoration Bureau, '25-27. Add. Official residence, Shiba Park, Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Ushio, Keinosuke** Dir. Local Adm. Bureau since '23; b. 1881; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '05; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam. and entered Home Office; Dir. Sanitation Bureau till '22. Add. Ushigome Yakoji, Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Uyeda, Mannen, Litt. D** philologist, Emeritus Prof., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; Pres. of Jingu Shinto Institute at Ise; b. 1867 in Aichi-ken; grad. at above Coll. in '88; sent to France and Germany to prosecute further study; once acting Dir. of the Tokyo Foreign Language Sch.; Counsellor and Bureau Chief of the Edu. Dep't; Dean of the Lit. Coll. till '20. Represented Japan in the meeting of the International Univ. Club held in Brussels in '23. Add. Shitaya Yanaka Shimizucho, Tokyo. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Uyeda, Teijiro, D.L** Prof. at Tokyo Univ. of Commerce, his alma mater, from which he graduated '02; studied in Germany and England '05-09 as student sent by Government; b. in '79 in Tokyo; Gov. Delegate at Washington in the 1st Conference held at Washington. Add. Tokyo Univ. of Commerce. — p. 111 ([scan leaf 906](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n906/mode/1up))

**Uyehara, Se[illegible]ujiro, Dr. Sc** (London Univ.), M.P., Parl. Counsellor, Foreign Office since '27; b. in 1878 in Nagano-ken; grad. from the Univ. of Washington, U.S.A.; lecturer at the Tokyo Higher Tech. Sch. '11-14; Prof. at Meiji Univ. '13-20; Parl. Counsellor of Dept. of Commerce & Industry in '24; is Silver medalist of Univ. of London. Works; The Political Development of Japan (English), Studies on the Constitution of Japan (Japanese), Democratic Institution in Japan (Jap.), etc. Add. Aoyama Takagicho, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uyehara, Shikazo** Dir. Keisei Electric R'ly. Co., Borneo Rubber Co., Japan Electro-Industrial Co., etc.; b. 1860 in Oita-ken; grad. from the Oita Normal School and Tokyo Semmon Gakko (forerunner of Waseda Univ.); passed the Exam. for Bar and began practice in Tokyo; once returned M.P. Add. Koshikawa Shin-Suwacho, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uyehara, Yusaku, Baron (cr. '97)** Marshal, ex-Chief of General Staff Board; b. 1856 in Hyuga; Sub-Lieut. (Eng.) '79; studied in France '89; staff officer to the 1st Army in the Sino-Japanese War; attended the Coronation ceremony of the Tsar and also the Hague Peace Conference; chief staff to Marshal Nozu in the Russo-Japanese War; Com. of 7th Division '08; Minister of War '12-13; Chief, Mil. Edu. Board '14; Field Marshal in '19; retired from the Gen. Staff in '23. Add. Akasaka Daimachi, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uyemura, Chozaburo** Mng.-Dir. Japan Beer Brewery Co., etc.; was b. 1862 in Tokyo; was for some years an official of the Departments of Agr. and Com. and of Communications. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uyeno, Seiichi** Vice-Pres. of the Asahi Shimbun, Tokyo and Osaka; b. in Osaka, 1882; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '07 and took to journalism. Add. Osaka Asahi, Osaka. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uyeno, Yasutaro, M.P** b. in Toyama-ken, 1865; was in the local government service for years; next a newspaper editor and businessman; Sen. Sec. to the Dept. of Justice in the Seiyukai Administration 1920. Add. Azabu Kogai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uyesugi, Shinkichi, D.L** Professor, Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1878 in Ishikawa-ken; studied politics at Imp. Tokyo Univ. and app. its Assist.-Prof., '03; studied in Germany, '06-09; full Prof. '12; toured abroad '20. His specialty is Japanese Constitution. Add. Otsuka Sakashita, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Uzawa, Somei, D.L. (Japan)** barrister, ex-M.P., Dir. of the Meiji Univ.; b. 1872 in Chiba, where his precocious ability attracted wide attention; grad. Law, Imp. Univ. Tokyo, class '99, and has been practising since then, being reputed as an expert in criminal cases. Add. Tsukiji, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Wada, Eisaku** painter of Western style, Prof. in the Tokyo Fine Art Acad.; b. 1874 in Kagoshima-ken; grad. from the Academy in '96 and Assist.-Prof. at his alma mater for some years; sent to France where he studied under Raphael Collin; is a Mem. of the Hanging Committee of Imperial Academy of Arts. Add. Azabu Kasumicho, Tokyo. — p. 112 ([scan leaf 907](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n907/mode/1up))

**Wakamiya, Sadao, M.P** Vice-Min. Dept. of Communications '21-24; b. in Aichi-ken in 1875 and adopted by the family; studied law at Imp. Tokyo Univ. and entered the Govt. service; Dir. Mercantile Marine Bureau during the Great War. Add. Akasaka Omote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakamiya, Unosuke** Sociologist; b. 1872 in Toyama-ken and studied in American Catholic Univ. of America, 1900-04, and London School of Economic and Political Science, '04-05, studying as self-supporting student. Add. Shimo-meguro, near Tokyo. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Ikutaro, M.P. for Yokohama City** Pres. Yokohama Wakao Bank, Yokohama Chamber of Commerce; a wellknown silk merchant; b. 1884 in Yokohama; grad. from the Yokohama Commercial School. Add. Honcho Shichome, Yokohama. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Ikuzo** silk exporter, ex-M.P., Mem. of the Yokohama Chamber of Commerce, Dir. Wakao Bank, Hiranuma Bank, Yokohama Elec. Rly, Y'hama Warehouse Co. etc.; b. 1857 in Yamanashi-ken; engaged in foreign trade chiefly exporting raw silk. Add. Honcho, Yokohama. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Kinnosuke** Mem. Kofu Chamber of Commerce, Chairman Wakao Bank and Nippon Electric Chemical Industry Co., Dir. Tokiwa Life Ins., Tokyo Electric, Tokyo Gas, etc.; b. 1882; studied Politics at Imp. Tokyo Univ. Add. Kofu. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Kotaro** Pres. of the Tokyo Commercial Bank, Pres. of the Mitsubishi Commercial Co., Managing-Dir. of the Mitsubishi Family Co., and Dir. of the Katsuragawa Electrical Industry Co., Ltd.; b. 1896, e.s. of Shohachi Wakao. Add. Hiraoka, Shimo Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Shohachi, Crown Mem. House of Peers since '27** Pres. of Tokyo Electric Light Co., Dir. of Naigai Kogyo, Tokyo Taxi-Cab, Nippon Selma, and other concerns; b. in 1873 and adopted into the family '96 by marriage; studied at the Chuo Daigaku. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakasugi, Kaname, B.A., M.A. (New York Univ.)** Sec. of Foreign Office, Sectional Chief of Intelligence Bureau (For. Office); b. 1883 at Kumamoto-ken; grad. from the Toa Dobun School in Shanghai '06; entered the service of the Foreign Office the same year; held junior consular post at various places; passed the Dip. and Con. Service Examination '17; Consul or Embassy Secretary at Shanghai, Paris, London, and Los Angeles before appointed to the present post in '25. Add. Kojimachi Hirakawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Wakatsuki, Reijiro** Prime Minister (Mar. '26 to Apr. '27), Crown Mem. House of Peers, adviser of the Minsei Party; b. 1866 in Shimane-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '92; Counsellor of the Treasury soon after graduation; Bureau Dir. '03; Vice-Min., 05; Financial Agent in London in '06; recalled home and appointed for second time Vice-Minister of Finance '[?]8; resigned in '11; Minister of Finance, '12-[?] and '14-15; Prime Minister as successor to Viscount Kato, also leader of the Kenseikai party; resigned in Apr. '27. Add. Koj. Nakarokubancho, Tokyo — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Washio, Shogoro, Ph. D. (Harvard)** b. in 1883 in Awaji; travelled in Germany and France '11-12 as scholarship student of Harvard; again travelled abroad with Visc. Goto in '19 when he studied social and labor conditions in England; now staff writer of the Japan Advertiser. Add. Suginami-mura, near Tokyo. — p. 113 ([scan leaf 908](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n908/mode/1up))

**Washino, Yonetaro, M.P. for Kyoto City** Dir. of the Pacific Marine & Ins. Co.; b. 1885 in Kochi-ken; grad. from Tokyo Higher Normal School, and also from Law, Kyoto Imp. Univ. Was Prof. at Taiwan National Language School, and then Dep. Mayor of Kyoto City. Add. Kamikyo-ku, Kyoto. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Chifuyu, 2nd Visc., Mem. House of Peers since '20** Mng.-Dir. of the Franco-Japanese Bank (Tokyo Branch); b. 1876; adopted as heir by his late uncle Viscount Kunitake Watanabe; grad. in Law, Imp. Univ. (Tokyo) '98; further educated in France, Germany and America; Manager of Industrial Bank of Japan for some years; Mng.-Director of Nippon Seikosha and Hokkaido Colliery and Steamship Co.; for a while proprietor and editor of a Tokyo daily. Add. Azabu Nishimachi, Tokyo. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Kaichi, D. Eng., C.E., B.S** Pres. Ishikawajima Ship-building Co., Dir. Keihan Elec. Rly.; b. in Nagano-ken 1853; grad. in '83 Gov't Eng. College; conferred C.E. and B.S. by Glasgow Univ., class '89; studied practical side under Sir John Fowler and other noted British engineers; elected member of Civil Eng'ring Society, London. Add. Akasaka Omote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Katsusaburo** ex-Pres. of Tokyo Gas Co. which he resigned for failure of the Watanabe Bank and other interests; b. in Tokyo 1873; grad. from the now defunct Shogyo Gakko '00; a promotor of Tokyo Taxi-cab Co. est. '18; is also connected with various other enterprises. Add. Shiba Nishi-kubo, Tokyo. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Katsusaburo** Pres. Oriental Development Co. since '25; b. in 1872 in Okayama-ken; grad. Law Coll. Imp. Tokyo Univ., '96 and has been in service of local Gov't, lastly Gov. of Tokushima-ken; Dir. of Local Adm. Bureau, Home Dept.; Mayor of Yokohama '24. Add. Seoul. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Osamu** Pres. of the Osaka Elec. Lamp Bulb Co., the Uwa Hydro-Elec. Co.; b. 1859 in Ehime-ken; grad. from Keiogijuku in 1881; Vice-Consul at Wonsan, Korea, 1882; Comptroller of the Patent Bureau of the Agr. and Com. Dep't, 1889; Director of the Nagasaki Post and Telegraph Office, 1891; returned to the lower house seven times; Mayor of Sasebo City, 1902-1906; afterwards entered business. Add. Kojimachi-ku, Hirakawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Ryusei** Dir. Nagoya Higher Com. Sch.; b. in Niigata-ken, 1866; grad. from Tokyo Semmon Gakko (now Waseda Univ.) '87; studied Ethics in U.S.A. and Germany; app. Prof. of the Tokyo Academy of Music, Tokyo Higher Normal School, and the Dir. of the former school, etc. Despatched to China in 1913 and Europe and America 1920. Add. Nagoya. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Totsuzo** Chief Secretary of Tokyo Chamber of Commerce, Prof. at Imp. Tokyo Univ., his alma mater, where he was graduated in Law; devotes himself to the study of municipal affairs from social standpoint; founder of a Municipal Research Society and adviser of the Tokyo Municipal Office. Works: European War and Food Mobilization in Germany, etc. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Toshijiro** Dir. of Watanabe Bank Yokohama Canvas Co. etc.; b. 1882, son of Fukusaburo Watanabe, Pres. of the Bank and Dir. of the Yokohama Specie Bank, Y'hama Elec. Co., etc.; grad. Economic Imp. Tokyo Univ. '14; toured in U.S.A. '15. Add. Nishitobe, Yokohama. — p. 114 ([scan leaf 909](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n909/mode/1up))

**Watase, Shozaburo, Ph. D., D.S.C** Prof. at Imperial University of Tokyo '00-24; grad. from College of Agriculture (Sapporo) '84; studied Zoology at Imp. Tokyo Univ. '84-86; Johns Hopkins Univ. '86-90; Clark Univ. '90-92; represented Japan at International Zoological Assemblage at Cambridge, '98; Lecturer at the University of Chicago, '92-1900; visited Canada in '22 to investigate fox-breeding. Add. Ushigome Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yabuki, Keiki, D. Litt** ex-Director of the Social Affairs Bureau, Tokyo City; b. in Tokyo; grad. from Litt. Coll. Tokyo Imp. Univ., class 1909; further studied in England; then Assist.-Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ. and also Prof. of Shukyo Univ. Add. Nishi Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yada, Shichitaro** Con.-Gen. at Shanghai since '23; b. in 1875 in Shizuoka-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Politics, class '06; eleve-Con. in China, '07; 3rd Emb. Sec. at Rome, '12; to the head office, '13; 3rd Emb. Sec. at London, '16; Consul at Mukden, '16; Con.-Gen. at London '19; at San Francisco, '20, at Shanghai, '23. Add. Japanese Consul.-Gen., Shanghai. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yagi, Hideji, D. Eng** Prof. Tohoku Imp. Univ.; b. in Kyoto; grad. in Elec. Eng. Tokyo Imp. Univ., class 1909. Once Prof. of the Sendai Higher Technical School; then studied in England. Add. Ryokakuin-cho, Sendai City. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yahagi, Eizo, D. L** Prof. of the Coll. Economics, Imp. Tokyo Univ., Pres. of Teikoku Agricultural Association; b. 1869 in Saitama-ken; grad. from said univ., class '95 and then specialized in Agr. Economy at the Univ. Hall; Lecturer at the Tokyo Higher Com. School, '96; Inspector of the Industrial Bank, '97-98; Lecturer and then Ass. Prof. at the Agr. Coll. Imp. Univ., till '00; sent to Europe for study, '00-08 during which he represented Japan at the International Agr. Conference at Rome ('06); on returning home was given chair of Agr. Policy at the Agr. Coll. and of Economics at the Law Coll. which dual position he still occupies. Has also been appointed mem. of various Economic Committees organized by the Central Government, besides being sent on an inspection tour in Europe and America, '11-13. Works: Real Estate Banks, Collected Essays on Agr. Economics. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yajima, Eisuke** Prop. of the Yajima-gumi, Pres. of the Yajima Filature Co., etc.; b. in 1869 in Yamanashi-ken; was granted a silver medal by the Min. of Agr. and Com., 1908, in recognition of his distinguished services to silk industry; was on the Committee of the Central Sericultural Association, his province; Mem. of the House of Peers representing highest taxpayers of Yamanashi-ken, 1909-1915. Add. Midori-cho, Kofu City. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Buntaro, Dr. Eng** Dir., Mitsui Mining Co.; b. in Fukui-ken, 1861; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. '86; further studied in Germany, '89-91; returning home, expert to Ikuno Mine and next Dir. of Kamioka Mine owned by the Mitsui. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Junjiro** Dir. of Sanitation Bureau, Home Office since '22; b. 1883; grad. at Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '08; passed Higher Civil Service Exam. and has since been in the service of Home Office; Dir. of Shrine Bureau '19-22. Add. Koishikawa-ku Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 115 ([scan leaf 910](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n910/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Naoya, Dr. Eng** Director of Mitsui Mining Co. and Hokkaido Colliery and S.S. Co.; b. in 1860 Tokyo; grad. from Mining and Metallurgy at Imperial Tokyo Univ. '85; Mining Supervisor of Gov., then he entered the Mitsui. Add. Kojimachi-ku Nakarokuban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Saburo, D.L** Prof. Law Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1869 in Nara-ken; edu. at the Tokyo Semmon Gakko (now Waseda Univ.) and then Law Coll., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '96; further studied at the Univ. Hall and in Germany, England, etc. '97-01; Prof. at the Univ. on his return; is on Higher Civil Service Exam. Committee. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Waka** lady writer interested in social and labor problems; b. 1878 in Kanagawa-ken; edu. in American schools for six years supporting herself by hard work. Works; Translation of Magdalene Mark's "Women", Youthful Love and Intellectual Awakening. Female Emancipation and Sex Education. Woman, Man and Mother, Investigation of White Slavery, etc. Add. Yotsuya Minami-Iga-cho, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamagata, Iso-o** former Editor and Prop. of the Seoul Press; b. 1869 in Shiga-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; on the staff of the Yorodzu; Editor of Herald of Asia, Tokyo; toured abroad '23. Add. Takata, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamaguchi, Einosuke, D. Sch** Court Councillor and ex-Director of the Imperial Archives and Mausolea; b. 1863 in Shimane-ken; grad. in Physics at Tokyo Univ. in '84; Prof. in Imp. Kyoto Univ. '97; President of Peers' Sch. in '05. Add. Koj. Iidamachi, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamaguchi, Kisaburo, Ph. D** Mng.-Dir. of Furukawa Mining Co. and Dir. of Furukawa Trading Co., Asahi Denka Co., etc., b. in Tokyo, 1874; graduated from Johns Hopkins, '02. Add. Shinagawa Higashi-dai, near Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamaguchi, Seitaro** Mng.-Dir. of Yamaguchi Bank, Tokyo, since its est. Aug. '17; b. in 1885 in Niigata-ken; edu. at Harvard; class '10; Mng.-Dir. of Hokuetsu Hydro-Electric Co. Add. Nihonbashi Honcho, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamaguchi, Shozo** Mng. the Fujiya Hotel (Hakone); when young went to America and stayed eight years in Canada, England, France and Italy; returned to Japan through India and China in '00; afterwards again visited America in '19. Add. Miya-noshita, Hakone. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamaguchi, Tetsuya, D. Eng** Director of the Uraga Dockyard. Insp.-Gen. of Naval Construction (ret.); b. in Tokyo in 1856; studied engineering at the former Gov. Engineering Coll. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamaji, Joichi, M.P. for Hiroshima** Parl. Counsellor for Edu. Dep't; b. 1882 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. at Politics, Waseda Univ.; has been returned to the Parliament five times. Add. Ushigome-ku Minami-machi, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Gitaro, D. Eng** of Electric Eng. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. since '87; b. at Saitama-ken in 1860; grad. electricity from the Gov. Engineering Coll. in '82; stayed in Europe for study '96-99; has written several works on his speciality. Add. Koishikawa Kagomachi, Tokyo. — p. 116 ([scan leaf 911](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n911/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Hashio, D.C.L** ex-Dir. of the Legislation Board ('25-27); b. in Nagasaki-ken, 1873; grad. from Politics, Imp. Tokyo Univ. '98; studied International Law in the Univ. Hall; passed Higher Civil Service Exam.; was Councillor of the Navy Dept. and the Foreign Office; as Naval Interpreter attended the 2nd International Peace Conference at Berne; Dir. Treaty Bureau, '23-25; Dir. of Legislation Bureau till Apr. '27. Add. Aoyama Minamimachi, Tokyo. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Hitoshi** a socialist leader whose magazine article has been a signal for "leftward" movement in Japan, and who has been imprisoned four or five times on charge of disturbing peace, violating press law, etc.; b. 1880 at Kurashiki, Okayama; edu., only common school; has been active writer in the cause of socialism and started in '19 with his wife a monthly. Works; Lenin and Trotsky, Outline of Marx's Capitalism, Labor Movement and Socialism. Is an invalid from consumption. Add. Tarumi, Suma, Kobe. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Kikue** wife of above. Born in 1890 in Tokyo and studied at Tsuda English College; has written or translated a number of works, besides acting as assistant to her husband. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Kenjiro, Baron (cr. '15) D. Sc** Emeritus Prof. of Imperial Tokyo Univ., and Mem. of Privy Council since '23; b. 1854 at Aizu; studied physics in Russia and America; connected with Tokyo Imp. Univ. after his return in '73, ultimately rising to be President; Mem. House of Peers '04; Pres. of Imp. Kyushu Univ. on its inauguration in '11; Pres. of Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '13, combining ad interim a similar post at Kyoto in '14. Add. Ikebukuro, near Tokyo. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Gombei, Admiral (ret.) Count** (cr. Baron '02, Count '07), G.C.M.G. (England) ex-Premier; b. 1852 in Satsuma and fought in the War of the Restoration on the Imperial side; one of the pioneer graduates of the Naval Academy, class '77; was on board a naval training vessel that visited America early in Meiji era; round-the-world voyage on board a German warship; Lieut. '80; as a commissioner sent to England '85 to bring home the cruiser Naniwa built there; dispatched to Europe and America in '87 on suite of the then Vice-Minister of the Navy (Adm. Kabayama); Captain '90; Personal Secretary to the Minister till '95; Rear-Admiral and Bureau Director, '95; Vice-Adm., '96; Minister from '98 to Jan. '06; full Admiral '04. Dispatched to England and U.S.A. on suite of Prince Fushimi '07; formed Cabinet '13-14 succeeding the 3rd Katsura Ministry; resigned '14 owing to the Siemen's Scandal; formed the "Earthquake Cabinet" '23-24. Add. Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Jotaro, M.P. (Seiyukai)** Pres. of South Manchuria Rly. Co. since July '27, ex-Executive Dir. of Mitsui Bussan; b. in 1867 in Fukui; entered the service of the Mitsui Company in '82, and was attached to the Branch at Shanghai, where he soon rose to be its Manager and remained till '07; transferred to the head office in '12; was involved in the Naval Scandal. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Tada-oki, D. Sch** Prof. at Waseda Univ. since '12; b. 1880 in Kochi-ken; grad. at Electric Engineering, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '05; Lecturer at the alma mater '07-08; further studied his speciality at Carlruhe and the practical side at the G.E. Co. Advisory Technical Dept., New York; served at the Shibaura Eng. Works, '05-08. Add. Waseda University. — p. 117 ([scan leaf 912](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n912/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Tatsuo, Baron (cr. '20)** Mem. House of Peers, adviser to the Minsei Party, ex-Gov. of Hypothec Bank of Japan; b. 1856 in Oita-ken; edu. at Keio; connected with educational work '80-82; entered Mitsubishi Firm, '82; Dir. of Nippon Yusen Kaisha; entered in '90 Bank of Japan in which he was appointed Dir. of Banking Bureau and next Governor '98-03; Gov. of Hypothec Bank of Japan '10; Minister of Agr. & Com. in '11-12; again in '13-14 and for the third time in '18-22. Add. Kami-Niban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Teijiro, D. Ph** Minister of Agr. and Forestry since Apr. '27 Chair. of board of directors of the Taiwan Sugar Mfg. Co., M.P.; b. 1870 in Niigata-ken; studied at the Halle and Leipzig Univ. in Germany for eight years; returned to Parliament seven times; appointed to the present post on the formation of the Tanaka Ministry in Apr. '27. Add. Kami-Meguro Gohong[illegible], near Tokyo. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamamuro, Gumpei** Lieut.-Commissioner of the Salvation Army in Japan; b. 1871 in Okayama-ken; raised himself from a printer's apprentice and studied at Doshisha, to enter Salvation Army in '95; travelled through Europe in '25-26; is known as an influential preacher as well as an able social reformer. Has written a number of Christian works. Add. Salvation Army, Headquarters, Kanda, Tokyo. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamamuro, Sobun** Dir. and Mng. of Osaka Branch of Mitsubishi Bank; b. 1880 in Kumamoto-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class 1907; studied in England and America for three years till 1919; entered the Mitsubishi Bank, on return home; manager of its New York Branch, 1917; manager of its London Branch, 1920-23; the present post since 1923. Add. Muko-gun, Miwage-machi, Hyogo-ken. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamanari, Kyoroku** Dir. of Toyo Sugar Mfg. Co.; b. 1873 in Okayama-ken; grad. '91 from the Accountant's School, formerly attached to Tokyo Higher Commercial School, and entered the 34th Bank to be made Mng. of its Keelung Branch; removed in '85 to the Bank of Taiwan and had charge of its Kobe and Osaka Branches; next Assistant-Chief of the Dept. of General Affairs of its Head Office and finally a Mng.-Dir. till 1920. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamanashi, Hanzo** Gen. (retired), Gov.-Gen. of Chosen; b. 1865 in Kanagawa-ken, Sub-Lt., infantry, '86; Capt. '95; Major, 1900; Colonel, '09; Maj.-Gen. '15; in the meantime instructor at the Military and Naval Colleges. Regimental and then Brigade Com., Section Chief of the Gen. Staff Office, and Chief of the Inspection Dept., Military Education Board in '16; Chief Staff of the Besieging Army of Tsingtao during the Great War; Lt.-Gen. and Vice-Min. of War under Gen. Tanaka in '17; Minister of War, '21-23; full Gen. '22; present post, Dec. '27. Add. Seoul, Chosen. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamanouchi, Ichiji** Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '14) and Lord-in-Waiting of the Kinkei Hall; b. in Kagoshima in 1865; grad. Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ. in '88; Secretary and next Section Chief of the Police Bureau, Home Office. Governor of Aomori '02-4; Dir. of the R'ly Bureau '04-6; Vice-Pres. of the Imp. R'lys., and then Director of R'ly Adm. Dept., '09-12; Governor of Hokkaido, '12; Chief Secy. to the Cabinet in Yamamoto Ministry; Min. of Railways '23-24. Add. Azabu Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 118 ([scan leaf 913](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n913/mode/1up))

**Yamanouchi, Kakusaburo, D.C.L** Lawyer, M.P.; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class 1898; was Director of the Tokyo Court of Appeal, and then Vice-Minister of Justice. Add. Sugamo-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamanouchi, Shiro** ex-Consul-Gen. at Harbin (till '26); b. in Fukuoka, 1872; studied Law at Imp. Tokyo Univ.; passed Dip. Serv. Exam. and entered Foreign Office. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamanouchi, Toyokage, Marquis** Capt. of Infantry (ret.); b. in 1876 of the former fief of Tosa; graduated from the Military Cadet School in '00; sent to France for study '09 and returned home in '10; married in '03 Princess Sachiko (d. '19), eldest daughter of the late Prince Fushimi. Add. Yoyogi, near Tokyo. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamaoka, Juntaro** Chairman of Dirs. of Osaka Iron Works, ex-Pres. of Osaka Chamber of Commerce, b. 1866 in Kanazawa; official of the Dept. of Communications '92-98; entered O.S.K. '98; its Section Chief '99; Manager '08; Dir. and Vice-Pres., '14-20, combining several other posts. Add. Tennoji, Higashi-nari-gun, Osaka-fu. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamaoka, Mannosuke, D.C.L** Dir. Police Bureau (Home Office) since '27; b. 1876; grad. from the Nippon Law Coll. '99; passed Judicial Service Exam. and long served as judge or procurator; in the meantime sent to Germany for study; was Director of Prison Bureau '25-26. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Kakujiro, D.L** Prof. at Coll. Economics, Imp. Tokyo Univ., Mem. of Imp. Academy; b. 1868 in Shizuoka-ken. Edu. at Economics Course, said Univ., class '89, and further at the Post-graduate course and in Germany till '95. Prof. at the Tokyo Higher Com. School, '97; assist.-Prof. at his alma mater, '02; full Prof., '06; Dean of the Economics Coll., '20-23. Works; An Economic Study of the English Cotton Industry, Principles of Economics, Principles of Banking, etc. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Keiichi** Min. to Peru since '25; b. in Kanagawa-ken, '82; grad. from the Special Course of Tokyo Higher Commercial School and passed Dip. Service Exam. '05; Eleve-Con. the same year; Consul, Embassy Sec. and to Consul-Gen. in London and in Shanghai; Consul-Gen. in Honolulu '21-24. Add. Lima. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Kiyoshi** Mng.-Dir. of Nippon Fire Ins. Co., Tokyo; b. in 1875 at Mito; grad. from the Post and Telegraph School '96; clerk in the Communication Department till '02 when he entered the present Co. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Naokata, D.Sc** Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ. and Tokyo Higher Normal School, Mem. of Imp. Academy; b. 1870 in Hokkaido; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., class 1895; studied geology in Germany, 1898-1901; present post since 1911; attended the Pan-Pacific Congress in Honolulu in the Summer of '27. Add. Koishikawa-ku Otsuka Kubo-cho, Tokyo. — p. 119 ([scan leaf 914](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n914/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Shioroku** b. in 1868 at Saga; grad. from Imp. Tokyo Univ., Law '96, and at the same time passed the Higher Civil Service Exam.; Tax Inspector '97, attached to Nagasaki Taxation Office; transferred to Finance Dept. as its Sec. '00; Dir. of Finance Bur., then Debt Bureau, lastly Treasurer, Imp. Household. Add. Yoyohata, near Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yamashina, Reizo** ex-Vice-Pres. of Tokyo Chamber of Commerce, Dir. of Nippon Marine Eng. Co.; b. 1864, Hiroshima-ken; est. Yamashina Marine Eng. Office which was subsequently amalgamated with Mitsubishi Marine Eng. Dept.; toured abroad as representative of the Chambers of Commerce in Japan '18, and again in '22. Add. Kyobashi Kobikicho, Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yamashita, Gentaro** Adm. (ret.) Supreme War Councillor; b. in 1863 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from the Naval Academy '83; 2nd Lieut. '85; Commander '98; Captain '06; Rear-Admiral '08; Vice-Admiral '12; President of the Naval Academy '10-14; Deputy Chief of the Naval Staff Board and Mem. of the Adm. Council in '14-15; Com-in-Chief of Sasebo Admiralty, '16-17; Admiral and Com.-in-Chief of First Fleet '18; Chief of Naval Board of Command '20-24. Add. Koj. Hirakawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yamashita, Kamesaburo** Pres. of Yamashita Steamship Co., etc.; coming up to Tokyo while under 20, he became in '98 a small coal-dealer; started shipping business in '03, next lumber and warehouse business. The War and unusual activity of shipping business established his fortune. Add. Shiba Takanawa. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yamawaki, Haruki** ex-Gov. of Aichi-ken; b. 1871 in Kyoto, adopted son and heir of the above; grad. Law, Imp. Univ. in '96; official in the Dep't of Agr. and Com.; Commissioner of Anglo-Japanese Exhibition in '10; Dir. of Monopoly Bureau, Formosa; chief Commissioner to Panama Exhibition in '14-15; Gov. of Yamanashi, Miye, etc. Add. Koj. Hirakawacho, Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yamaya, Tajin** Admiral (ret.); b. in 1866 in Tokyo; grad. from Naval Academy '86 and Naval Staff College '97; Captain '05; Chief of Staff to the 4th Squadron during the Russo-Japanese War; Rear-Admiral '09; Chief of the Bureau of Personnel '09-13; Pres. of Naval College '13-14; Com. of the 1st Fleet Aug. '15 and partook in the European war; Vice-Chief of Naval Staff Board, '15; Vice-Adm. and then Com.-in-Chief of the first Fleet, '18-20; placed on the retired list in '23. Add. Azabu Kogai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yanagi, So-etsu** an authority on philosophy of religions; b. 1889 in Tokyo; edu. at Peers School and Imp. Tokyo Univ., Psychology, class '13. Works; Science and Life, William Blake, Truth about Religion, Miracles in Religion, etc. Add. Yoshida, Kyoto. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yanagida, Kunio** Lecturer of the Keio Univ., editorial adviser of the Asahi Shimbun; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; was once Chief Secretary of the House of Peers. Is an authority on folklore. Add. Keio Univ., Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yanagisawa, Yasutoshi, Count** Mem. House of Peers. Mem. of International Statistical Society. Hon. Mem. of Royal Statistic Society (England), etc.; b. 1870 in Niigata-ken; grad. at the Peers' College '94; further studied in Berlin, Vienna, Strasburg, Brussels and Paris, studying statistics; returned home in '00; attended the International Statistical Conference six times as representative of Japan; Pres. of Yanagisawa Statistical Inves- tigation Office, founded by him. Add. Shiba Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 120 ([scan leaf 915](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n915/mode/1up))

**Yanagiya, Usaburo, Paym. Lieut. (ret.)** Adviser to Yasuda Bank, ex-Vice-Gov. Hypothec Bank of Japan; b. in 1867 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., '90; entered the Bank of Japan immediately after; travelled Europe in '96; Chief of the Issue Dep't., etc.; resigned '13; Dir. and later Vice-Gov. of the Hypothec Bank '13-27. Add. Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yano, Fumio** Vice-Pres. of Osaka Mainichi Shimbun, author, and retired diplomat; b. 1850 in Oita prefecture; grad. from Keio Gijuku, '70; Secretary to the Treasury, '17; resigned in '81 and took charge of the Hochi Shimbun; took part in the formation of the Kaishinto (progressive party); made tour in Europe and America, '86-88; Master of Ceremonies and Chief of the Bureau of Imperial Tombs, '90-96; Minister to China, '97-98; placed on the retired list in '99. Works: "Keikoku Bidan", "Ukishiro Monogatari" (a marine novel); "New Society," etc. Add. Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yano, Tsuneta** Pres. and Mng.-Dir. of 1st Mutual Life Insur. Co.; b. 1865 in Okayama; grad. from Third High School in '89; entered Japan Life Insur. Co. and in '93 was made manager of the Kyosai Life Insur. Co.; attended the International Congress of Actuaries held in Sept. '95 in Belgium; entered a German Life Insur. Co. for business training; appointed an official in the Dep't of Agr. and Com. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yashiro, Rokuro, Baron (cr. '16)** Adm. (ret.), Privy Councillor since '26, ex-Mem. Sup. Mil. Council; b. 1860 in Aichi; Naval Ensign in '85; Captain in '01; Second in Command of the Yashima; commander of the Asama in Russo-Japanese War; Dir. of Naval Staff College, '12-13; Com. of Maizuru Admiralty: '13-14; Minister of Navy, '14-15; Admiral '16; Com. of 2nd Fleet '17; was once Attache to the Legation at Petrograd. Add. Azabu Kogaicho, Tokyo. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yasuda, family** One of the business magnate families owning, running or controlling scores of big concerns such as banking, trading, building, insurance, etc., throughout Japan. Zenjiro, the late head of the family, sprang from obscurity; founded several banks besides financing many other useful undertakings; assassinated in Sept. '21 by a degenerate and spend-thrift in his residence at Oiso. Zenjiro (eldest) (the head of the family and Pres. of Yasuda Firm, etc.), Zengoro (2nd) (Dir. Yasuda Bank, Pres. of Teikoku Marine, Transport and Fire Ins., etc.) next Zenshiro, Zen-ye and Zembei, all relatives by blood, all take active part in the management of the Yasuda interests. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yasugi, Sadatoshi** Prof. of the Tokyo School of Foreign Languages (in charge of Russian Language); edu. in Philology, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class 1901; then stayed in Russian to study the Russian language. Add. Nagasaki-mura, near Tokyo. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yasui, Tetsu** Pres. of the Tokyo Women's College; b. 1870 in Tokyo; grad. from the Tokyo Female Higher Normal School, 1890; studied in England, 1896; was Prof. of Tokyo Female Higher Normal School for many years. Add. Uyenohara, Nakano-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yasukawa, Kei-ichiro, Baron (cr. '19)** highest tax-payer, mine-owner. Dir. of several Cos.; b. 1819 in Fukuoka-ken; studied at Keiogijuku '72; has made a fortune in coal-mining; founded single-handed the Meiji Semmon College in 1908 by contributing ¥3,000,000, and has been duly rewarded. The institution was transfered to the Gov't with all its properties in 1920. Add. Wakamatsu, Fukuoka. — p. 121 ([scan leaf 916](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n916/mode/1up))

**Yasukawa, Yunosuke** Dir. of Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; b. 1870 at Kashiwahara, Kyoto; grad. from Osaka Higher Commercial School in '89 and entered Mitsui Firm the same year; was promoted to Dir. '17, after having been Chief of one Branch or other. Add. Ushigome Tsukudo-Hachiman, Tokyo. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yata, Chonosuke** ex-Min. to Siam '21-24; b. 1871 in Shimane-ken; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School '95; passed Diplomatic Service Exam. '99; Eleve-Con. 1900; stationed at Tientsin, Mexico, Vancouver, etc. as consul and Legation Secretary; Consul-Gen. at Ottawa, and New York, '13-19. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yatabe, Yasukichi** Minister to Siam since 1928; b. 1882 in Yamaguchi; grad. from special course, Tokyo Higher Com. School, '07; attache in Siam '08; Consul at Antung, Suchow, Sydney, etc., Sec. of Formosan Gov.-Gen. '17; Sec. of Foreign Office '19; attended the Seamen's Labor Conference held at Rome, '20 and '21; 1st Sec. at Rome the same year; Emb. Sec. at Washington '24; then Counsellor of Foreign Office and Sectional Chief of Intelligence Bureau; Consul-Gen. at Tsingtao '27-28. Add. Bangkok. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yazu, Naohide, D. Ph. (Columbia Univ.), D.Sc** Prof. of Zoology, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Director of Misaki Marine Biological Station; b. 1877 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Yotsuya Higashi-Shinano-machi, Tokyo. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yokota, Hideo, D.L. (Japan)** Pres. of Meiji Univ.; b. 1862 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Law, Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '88; sat at Tokyo, Chiba and Hakodate District Courts, and Tokyo and Hakodate Appeal Courts, Judge of the Supreme Court, '01-24; was sent abroad on official mission '09; Pres. of the Supreme Court, '24-27. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yokoyama, Matajiro** geologist, D. Sc., Prof. of Imp. Tokyo Univ.; b. 1860 in Nagasaki; grad. Geology in Imp. Tokyo Univ.; an official at the Dep't of Agr. and Com. in '82; sent to Germany to prosecute further study in '86; appointed Prof. at his alma mater on returning home. Add. Ushigome Higashi-Gokencho, Tokyo. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yoneda, Shotaro, Litt. D** Prof. at Imp. Kyoto Univ.; b. in 1873 in Naraken; edu. at General Theological Seminary (New York), Columbia and at some European Univs. specializing in sociology. Works; Investigation of Modern Social Thoughts, Contemporary Thoughts and Civilization, Philosophy of History by Rickert, etc. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yoneyama, Umekichi** Pres. of the Mitsui Trust Co., est. in '24; b. 1869 in Tokyo; adopted into the present family as husband of its daughter; edu. at the Aoyama Gakuin and U.S.A. Entered the Bank '95 and toured abroad with Seihin Ikeda, the Pres., for inspecting banking '98; Manager of the Yokohama and then, Osaka Branch; Mng.-Dir. Mitsui Bank '04-24; second tour in Europe '13. Add. Aoyama-Minamicho, Tokyo. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yosano, Kan** Poet and Prof. at Keio Univ.; b. in 1873 in Kyoto; edu., common school, and studied poetry under the late Naobumi Ochiai and Surgeon-Gen. Mori; travelled in Europe '11-14. Works; collected poems. Add. Kojimachi-ku Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 122 ([scan leaf 917](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n917/mode/1up))

**Yosano, Akiko** wife of the above and a poetess; b. 1878 in Sakae where she finished a Girls school; has been with her husband a leader of new poetry movement and has a large number of poetical works to her credit; travelled abroad in '14. Add. Kojimachi Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshida, Isaburo** Minister in Switzerland; b. in Kyoto-fu in 1878; grad. German Law, Tokyo Imperial Univ., class '03; passed Dip. and Con. Service Exam. '04 and appointed Eleve Consul at Hongkong the same year; diplomatic attache at Washington, '04; 3rd Embassy Sec. at Washington, '08; on service at the For. Office, '11; 2nd Embassy Sec. in London, '12; 1st Embassy Sec., '16; Mem. Peace Treaty Committee, '20; Embassy Counsellor and transferred to Peking, '21; Mem. Japan-China joint committee for the settlement of Shantung question and so forth; transferred in the capacity of Emb. Counsellor to Washington '24-25 and then to London. Add. Japanese Legation, Berne. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshida, Kumaji, D. Litt** Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Lit., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Europe and America, '04-07; Prof. of the Tokyo Female Higher Normal School '04; present post since 1916. Add. Koishikawa-ku Hakusangoten-machi, Tokyo. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshie, Takamatsu** (penname Kogan), Prof. at Waseda from which he graduated in Literature, class '05; further studied in France '16-20. Works; Modern Civilization and Literature, Love and Art, Translation of Maupassant, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshimoto, Seitaro, M.D** Chief of Internal Division of Japan Red Cross Society Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1873 in Fukuoka-ken. Edu. at Coll. Med., Imp. Tokyo Univ., class '97; assist. to Dr. Prof. Aoyama, head of the Internal Dep't, Univ. Hospital, '97-03; physician of the Red Cross Hospital, '03, which sent him to Germany to study Pathology and Pathological chemistry at Berlin, '06-09. Served in the Russo-Japanese War attached to the Red Cross Medical Corps. Add. Japan Red Cross Hospital. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshimura, Manji** Director of the Imperial Fuel Research Institute, of Agr. and Com.; b. in 1882; grad. from Mining and Metallurgy in '06 at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; studied abroad '13-15; passed Higher Civil Service Examination. Add. Azabu Yamamotocho, Tokyo. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshimura, Manjiro** Mng.-Dir., Furukawa Trading Co.; b. 1886 in Tochigi-ken; grad. from Keio in '02; further studied in the Halle and the Berlin Universities for 5 years. Entered Furukawa firm, 1915; married the sister of Baron Furukawa. Add. Shimo-shibuya, Tokyo. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshino, Sakuzo, L.L.B** Political contributor to the Tokyo Asahi since '24, resigning the chair at the Tokyo Imp. Univ. which he graduated from Politics, class '04; b. 1878 in Miyagi-ken and further studied in Europe; for several years in the service of the Chinese Gov.; is a writer of socialistic leaning. Add. Komagome Shimmei-cho, Tokyo. — p. 123 ([scan leaf 918](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n918/mode/1up))

**Yoshioka, Yayoi** a lady physician, Principal of the Tokyo Female Medical Coll., Pres. of Tokyo Shisei Hospital; b. 1871 in Shizuoka-ken; grad. from the former Saisei Gakusha (a private medical school); established the Medical Coll. in 1904 and became its Principal. Add. Kojimachi-ku Iida-machi, Tokyo. — p. 124 ([scan leaf 919](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1928/page/n919/mode/1up))
